talivaṉ parattiyik kūṭip puṉalāṭiyatu kēṭṭup pulakkiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, cūtār kuṟuntoṭic cūrami nuṭak kattu eṉap parattiyi nalam pārāṭṭupavaḷ pōlak kūṟiṉāḷ. “kaṟpuvaḻip paṭṭavaḷ paratti ēttiṉum, uḷḷattu ūṭal uṇṭeṉa moḻipa.” toṭiyum vaḷiyumākiya aṇinala mallatu piṟa kaṟpunalam ilaḷāyiṉum, avaḷpāl niṉakku uṇṭākiya aṉpu peritu eṉpāḷ niṉ veṅkātali eṉṟum, puṉalāṭiyapōtēyaṉṟi ataṟku muṉpum piṉpum muṟiyē avaḷit taḻīip pōvatum varuvatum ceytaṉi eṉpāḷ taḻīi eṉṟum, puṉalāṭiya kālam cēyttaṉṟu, mikka aṇittāya nēṟṟippōtu eṉpatu cuṭṭi, neruni āṭiṉi puṉalē eṉṟum, niṉ puṉalāṭṭum palar aṟiya nikaḻntatu eṉṟaṟku eṉpa eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “alariṉ tōṉṟum kāmattu mikuti” “kiḻavōṉ viḷiyāṭṭu āṅkum āṟṟē”3 eṉa āciriyar kūṟutaliṉ, alar vitantu kūṟappaṭṭatu. itu puṟañcol māṇāk kiḷavi, “kaḷavum kaṟpum alarvari viṉṟē”1 eṉpa taṉāl, talivi alar eḻuntami kūṟal amivatāyiṟṟu.
puṉalāṭiya ceyti kēṭṭup poṟāḷākiya talivi ata ṉāl eḻunta alarik kūṟiṉāḷāka, atu kēṭṭa talivaṉ avvāṟu illi eṉpatupaṭap pala collavum, avaḷ, aviyāvum avaṉ taṉ parattimiyi maṟittaṟkuk kūṟiyaṉa eṉṟu koṇṭu, nī ettuṇip poymmoḻikaḷāl ataṉi maṟippiṉum, atu maṟiyātu yāvarum aṟiya alarmikuva tāyiṟṟu eṉpāḷ, alarē maṟittal ollumō eṉṟāḷ. “ūra, poyyāl aṟiveṉniṉ māyam atuvē, kiyakap paṭṭami aṟiyāy, neruni, miyeḻil uṇkaṇ maṭanti yoṭu viyi, ērtaru putuppuṉal uritiṉiṉ nukarntu, paratti āyam karappavum ollātu, kavviyākiṉṟāṟ peritē”2 eṉṟum, ivvakiyāl eḻunta alari maṟittal muṭiyātu eṉpati, “arimatar maḻikkaṇ māa yōḷoṭu, neruniyum kamaḻpoḻil tuñci iṉṟum, perunīr viyi avaḷō ṭāṭip, pularā mārpiṉi vantuniṉṟu emvayiṉ, karattal kūṭumō maṟṟē........ alarākiṉṟuatu palarvāyp paṭṭē 3” eṉṟum varuvaṉa kāṇka.
viṇṇiṉum maṇṇiṉum taṉ oṇkatir parappip pēroḷi ceyyum ñāyiṟṟi orukāl kārmukiṟ kūṭṭam ciṟitu maṟikka muyaliṉum, ñāyiṟṟoḷi maṟiyātu viṇṇa kattum am mukiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa nilappakuti ciṟitu oḻinta piṟāṇṭum naṉku viḷaṅkutal pōla, nī maṟikka muyalum alar niṉṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭa niṉ paratti oḻiyap piṟar palarum aṟiya niṟpatāyiṟṟu eṉpāḷ, putittal ollumō ñāyiṟṟatu oḷiyē eṉṟāḷ, itu piṟitu moḻital. niṉceyal ciṟitum maṟikkappaṭātu eṉpatu karuttu. “ñāyiṟṟik kimmaṟippār il”4 eṉṟār piṟarum.
meyppāṭu : vekuḷi. payaṉ : pulattal.
	72.	vayaṉmala rāmpaṟ kayilami nuṭaṅkutaḻit
titali yalkuṟ ṟuyalvaruṅ kūntal
kuvaḷi yuṇka ṇēer melliyal
malarār malirniṟi vanteṉap
puṉalāṭu puṇartuṇi yāyiṉa ḷemakkē
talimakaḷ pulavi nīkkit taṉṉōṭu putuppuṉal āṭa vēṇṭiya talimakaṉ, kaḷavukkālattup puṉalāṭṭu nikaḻnta taṉi avaḷ kēṭpat tōḻikkuc colliyatu.
uri :
vayalil malarnta āmpalāl toṭukkappaṭṭa mūṭṭu vāy poruntiya acikiṉṟa taḻiyiṉiyum, ari paranta alkuliyum, aciyaviṭap peṟṟa kūntaliyum, kuvaḷi malar pōṉṟa mitīṭṭiya kaṇkaḷiyum, aḻakum meṉmi yum poruntiya iyalpiṉiyu muṭiyavaḷ, paṇṭu yām kaḷavil oḻukiya kālattup palvakip pūkkaḷic cumantu putunīr vantatāka ataṉkaṇ yām āṭutaṟkuriya tuṇiyāyi ṉāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
iḷiya makaḷir āmpaṟṟaḻi aṇital marapākaliṉ āmpaṟṟaḻiyiṉi mutaṟkaṭ kūṟiṉār. “ciṟuveḷḷāmpal, iḷiya mākat taḻiyā yiṉavē”1 eṉac cāṉṟōrum kūṟiṉar. tuyalvarutal - acital, ēer - aḻaku; “nalattaki nallavarkku ēer”2 eṉpar cāṉṟōr. aḻakāvatu, “iṉpappayaṉit talip paṭutal” eṉṟaṉar parimēlaḻakar. puṇartuṇi iṟantakālam tokka viṉittoki. puṇartuṇi āyiṉaḷ eṉa iṟanta kālattāṟ kūṟiyatu, ippuṉalāṭṭuk kaḷavuk kālattatu eṉṟaṟku.
talivi koṇṭa pulaviyi nīkkum kuṟippiṉaṉ ākaliṉ, avaḷ paṇṭu aṇintirunta taḻiyiṉiyum ēṉi nalaṅ kaḷiyum pārāṭṭiṉāṉ. taṉṉoṭu putuppuṉalāṭa vēṇṭu kiṉṟamiyiṉ paṇṭu nikaḻnta nikaḻcciyi niṉippittu avaḷ maṉattu nikaḻum pulaviyuṇarvi maṟappippāṉ, malarār malirniṟi vanteṉa eṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl talivaṉpāl aṉpupoka niṟṟal eṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. eṉpāl ivaḷ aṉṟu koṇṭa aṉpu iṉṟu curuṅkiṟ ṟaṉṟākaliṉ, ippōḻtum puṉalāṭap pōntu puṇartuṇiyāvaḷ eṉpāṉ, puṉalāṭu puṇartuṇi yāyiṉaḷ emakkē eṉa iṟanta kālattuk kuṟippē eytak kūṟiṉāṉ. “ārkali veṟpaṉ mārpupuṇi yākak, kōṭuyar neṭuvarik kavāaṉ pakalē, pāṭiṉ aruvi āṭutal iṉitē”1 eṉat talivi kaḷaviṉkaṇ talivaṉoṭu tāṉ āṭiya puṉalāṭṭu nikaḻcciyi nayantu kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
“yāṟum kuḷaṉum kāvum āṭip, patiyikantu nukartalum uriya eṉpa”2 eṉpataṟku itaṉik kāṭṭi, itu talivi pulavi nīṅkit taṉṉoṭu puṉalāṭal vēṇṭiya talivaṉ, muṉpu puṉalāṭiyataṉi avaḷ kēṭpat tōḻikku urittatu” eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvar. meyppāṭu: iḷivaral. payaṉ : talimakaḷ kēṭṭup puṉalāṭaṟku nērvāḷāvatu.
kayalami nuṭaṅkutaḻi eṉṟum pāṭam uṇṭu. itaṉik kayalami vayalmalar āmpal nuṭaṅkutaḻi eṉa iyittu, kayal mīṉkaḷ poruntiya vayalkaḷiṭattu malarnta āmpalmalarāl toṭukkappaṭṭa nuṭaṅkutaḻi eṉṟu urikka.
	73.	vaṇṇa voṇṭaḻi nuṭaṅka vāliḻi oṇṇuta larivi paṇṇi pāynteṉak kaṇṇaṟuṅ kuvaḷi nāṟit
taṇṇeṉ ṟiciṉē peruntuṟip puṉalē.
ituvumatu.
uri :
veḷḷiya iḻiyiṉiyum oḷḷiya nutaliṉiyumuṭiya arivi aḻakiya taḻiyuṭi iṭiyiṟ kiṭantu aciyap paṇṭu kaḷaviṉkaṇ peruntuṟip puṉalil viḷiyāṭ ṭayartaṟkup pukuntāḷāka, atu tēṉ poruntiya naṟiya kuvaḷiyiṉ maṇam kamaḻntu taṇṇitāyiṟṟu; ākalāṟṟāṉ, ippōtum ataṉi virumpuvēṉāyiṉēṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
vaṇṇam - aḻaku; niṟamumām, vāliḻi - muttumāliyu mām. puṉalāṭalum viḷiyāṭṭē yātaliṉ, paṇṇi eṉappaṭṭatu; “keṭavaral paṇṇi āyiraṇṭum viḷiyāṭṭu”1 eṉpa. eṉṟiciṉ, iṟantakāla muṟṟuviṉit tiricol. “avaṟṟuḷ, ikumum ciṉṉum ēṉi yiṭattoṭum, takunili yuṭiya eṉmaṉār pulavar”2 eṉpataṉāl, ciṉ paṭarkkikkum amivatāyiṟṟu. kaṇnaṟuṅkuvaḷi eṉakkoṇṭu, kaṇpōlum naṟiya kuvaḷi eṉṟalumām.
puṉalāṭiya ñāṉṟu taliviyatu iḻiyum nutalum oḷi tikaḻntu polivu ciṟanta kāṭci talivaṉ maṉattip paṟṟi nīṅkātu viḷaṅkiṉamiyiṉ atu kuṟittu avaṟṟi vitantu, vāliḻi oṇṇutal eṉac ciṟappittāṉ. talivi paṇṇi pāyvōḷ virintōṭit taṉṉittaḻuvi makiḻvu mikku viḷiyāṭiya kālattil, avaḷ iṭiyiṟ kiṭantu vaṇṇam ciṟakka acintu ciṟantamiyiṉ, taḻiyiṉi vaṇṇa oṇṭaḻi nuṭaṅka eṉa uḷḷataṉ uṇmi kūṟit talivaṉ iṉpuṟṟāṉ. akkāli, puṉaṟkuvaḷi taliviyiṉ kaṇṇi nikarttum, appuṉaliṉ taṭpam avaḷatu mēṉit taṭpatti nikarttum muṟiyē kāṭciyiṉpamum ūṟṟiṉpamum payantamiyiṉ, kuvaḷi nāṟip puṉal taṇṇeṉṟiciṉ eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ paṇṇi pāyāvaḻip puṉal iṉpam ceyyā teṉṟum, eṉavē ippōtu nikaḻum puṉalāṭṭiṟkum avaḷ iṉṟiyamiyāḷ eṉṟum tōḻi uṭkoḷḷumāṟu, paṇṇi pāynteṉak kuvaḷi kaḷ  nāṟiyatu eṉavum, puṉal taṇṇeṉṟiciṉ eṉavum pārāṭṭiṉāṉ. meyppāṭum payaṉu mavi.
iṉi, “tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉ”3 eṉṉum cūttiravuriyiṉ kaṇ pērāciriyar, “iḵtu uruvuvamap pōli” eṉṟum, taliviyip pulavi nīkki avaḷoṭu puṉalāṭiya tali makaṉit tōḻi, “nī puṉalāṭiya ñāṉṟu paratti pāyntāṭiya puṉalellām taṇṇeṉṟatu” eṉakkūṟiyavaḻi, talivaṉ itaṉik kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu koṇṭu, “attaṭam pōl ivaḷ uṟak kalaṅkit teḷintu taṇṇeṉṟāḷ eṉpatu karuti yuṇarap paṭṭatu” eṉṟum, “avaḷoṭu puṉal pāyntāṭiya iṉpac ciṟappukkēṭṭu niliyāṟṟāḷ eṉpatu karuttu” eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
veṇṭaḻi eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, veḷḷāmpalāl toṭukkappaṭṭa taḻi eṉṟu urikka. taṇṇeṉ kuvaḷi nāṟi eṉṟa pāṭattukku, vikārattāl tokum ummiyi virittu, taṇṇeṉṟa kuvaḷi nāṟiyum puṉal taṇṇitākātu arivi paṇṇi pāytalāl taṇṇitāyiṟṟeṉa urikka. kaṇṇuṟuṅkuvaḷi eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu.
	74.	vicumpiḻi tōkic cīrpōṉ ṟiciṉē
pacumpo ṉaviriḻi piya niḻaṟṟak
karicēr maruta mēṟip
paṇṇi pāyvō ṭaṇṇaṟuṅ katuppē.
ituvumatu.
uri :
paciya poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu viḷaṅkum iḻikaḷ mella oḷivīcak kariyil niṉṟa marutamarattiṉ mēlēṟip paṇṇi yāṭiya ivaḷatu kuḷirnta naṟiya kūntal, vicumpi
ṉiṉṟu iḻiyum tōkimayiliṉ cīri ottiruntatu kāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
tōki - mayil. cīr, viriyātu kuvintu paṭintu nīṇṭu viḷaṅkum kalāvam; “meṉcīrk, kalimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa ivaḷ, olimeṉ kūntal”1 eṉavum, “tōṭukoḷ, urukeḻu maññi olicīr ēyppat takaram maṇṇiya taṇṇaṟum mucci”2 eṉavum cāṉṟōr makaḷir kūntaṟku mayiliṉ cīriṉi uvamaṅ kūṟi
yiruttal kāṇka. nīṇṭa tōki kāṟṟil aciya, oḷiviṭṭu ilaṅkum mayil vicumpiṉiṉṟum iḻital pōla, paṇṇi pāyntavaḻit taṇṇaṟuṅ kūntal kāṟṟil paṟakkap poṟkalam oḷitikaḻa marutamarattiṉiṉṟum talivi iḻintatu kūṟaliṉ, itu vaṭivum toḻilum viravi vanta uvamamām. “viraviyum varūum marapiṉa eṉpa”1 eṉpatu uvamaviyal. “miṉṉunimirn taṉṉa kaṉaṅkuḻi imippap, piṉṉuviṭu neṟiyiṟ kiḷiiya kūntalaḷ, variyiḻi mayiliṉ olkuvaṉaḷ otuṅki, miṭiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ”2 eṉap piṟa cāṉṟōr ik karuttiṉik kuṟittal kāṇka. pōṉṟiciṉ iṭiccol aṭiyākap piṟanta muṟṟuviṉit tiricol. pāyntāḷ eṉaṟpālatu  mayaṅku moḻik kiḷavi”3 yāy etirviṉ kaṇ vantatu. “iṟappē etirvē āyiru kālamum ciṟappattōṉṟum mayaṅku moḻik kiḷavi” eṉpa. uyarciṉi marutattu ucci yēṟi nīrnilikkuṭ kutittu viḷiyāṭum marapiṉit toṭittali viḻuttaṇṭiṉārum, “uyarciṉi marutat tuṟiyuṟat tāḻntu, nīrnaṇip paṭikō ṭēṟic cīrmikak, kariyavar maruḷat tiriyakam pitira, neṭunīrk kuṭṭattut tuṭum eṉap pāyntu 4” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
taliviyip pulavinīkki avaḷoṭu puṉalāṭak karutu kiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ paṇṭu kaḷavil oḻukiya ñāṉṟu avaḷ puṉalāṭiyataṉi vitantu kūṟip pulavikkarutti nīkkuvāṉ avaḷ kēṭpak karicēr marutam ēṟip paṇṇi pāyvōḷ eṉṟāṉ. eṉavē, paṇṇiyiṟ piṟakkum iṉpattukkuk karicēr marutam utaviyāyatu pōla, kaḷaviṉkaṇ tāṉ  tuytta iṉpattukku avaḷatu viḷiyāṭṭu upakāramāyiṟṟu eṉṟāṉām. eṉavē, ippoḻutu tāṉ tuykkak karutum iṉpattukkum puṉalāṭṭu vēṇṭiyirukkumāṟu cuṭṭiyavāṟu kāṇka. marutamēṟip paṇṇi pāynta viṭattu, avaḷatu nīṇṭa kūntal kāṟṟiṟ paṟantu pēraḻaku tikaḻak kaṇṭa kāṭci, talivaṉ maṉattē nilipeṟa niṉṟu niṉikkuntōṟum mikka iṉpam payantatu kuṟittut taṇṇaṟuṅ katuppu vicumpiḻi tōkic cīrpōṉṟiciṉ eṉṟāṉ. kaḷavuk kālattu avaḷik kūṭun tōṟum, avaḷuṭiya kūntalit tāṉē kiceytum taṉ maṉakkiṉiya pūccūṭiyum iṉpuṟṟā ṉākaliṉ, ataṉāl ataṉit taṇṇaṟuṅkatuppu eṉac ciṟappittum, nīrāl naṉintu viṭṭu viṭṭu imittaliṉ avaḷ aṇinta iḻiyiṉ  oḷiviḷakkattip pacumpoṉ aviriḻi piya niḻaṟṟa eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷ. meyppāṭum payaṉum avi.
kaḷavukkālattu nikaḻntaṉi niṉippikkum mukattāl kaṟpiṉuḷ talivaṉ taliviyiṉ aḻakiṉip pukaḻvatu vēṭki mikutiyāl avaḷatu pulavinīkkum kuṟippiṟṟātaliṉ amiyum eṉka. “nikaḻtaki maruṅkiṉ vēṭki mikutiyiṉ, pukaḻtaki variyār kaṟpiṉuḷḷē”1 eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār viḷaṅkak kūṟumāṟṟālum ivvamiti tuṇiyappaṭum.
	75.	palariva ṇovvāy makiḻna vataṉāl
alartoṭaṅ kiṉṟā lūrē malara
toṉṉili marutattup peruntuṟi
niṉṉō ṭāṭiṉa ṭaṇpuṉa latuvē.
parattiyōṭu puṉalāṭi vanta talimakaṉ ataṉi maṟittuk kūṟiyavaḻit tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
uri :
makiḻna, malarkaḷiyuṭiya paḻamiyāṉa nilimit tākiya marutamaraṅkaḷ ceṟinta peruntuṟikkaṇ orutti niṉṉuṭaṉ taṇṇiya puṉalāṭiṉāḷāka, ataṉi nī emmuṉ iciyātu maṟippiṉum niṉṉi āṅkuk kaṇṭōr iv vūriṭattup palarkāṇ; ākavē, ūr alar kūṟattoṭaṅ kiṟṟu. iṉi nī maṟippatiṟ payaṉ illi eṉṟavāṟu.
makiḻna, peruntuṟikkaṇ, orutti niṉṉoṭu taṇ puṉalāṭiṉaḷ; nī ovvā yāyiṉum ataṉik kaṇṭōr palar; ataṉāl ūr alar toṭaṅkiṉṟu eṉa iyiyum. palarum ēṟkat takuvatu oṉṟaṉik kūṟiṉ, avarkaḷ “aḵtu okkum, okkum” eṉpatu pōla, ottal icital; “okkum aṟival yāṉ ellā viṭu”2 eṉpatu kāṇka. ataṉāl eṉpatu cuṭṭu mutalākiya kāraṇak kiḷavi; iḵtu, īṇṭu, kaṇṭōr palar eṉṟa kāraṇattic cuṭṭi niṟkiṉṟatu. toṭaṅkiṉṟu, iṟanta kālat terinili muṟṟuviṉi. malara eṉṉum peyareccak kuṟippu marutam eṉṉum peyar koṇṭatu. toṉṉili, paḻimiyāṉa nilimi. tokinūlkaḷil itu perukiya vaḻakkiṟṟu. “niṉṉilit tōṉṟumniṉ toṉṉilic ciṟappē”1 “toṉṉili muḻumutal tumiyap paṇṇi”2 “toṉ ṉili nekiḻnta vaḷiyaḷ ”3 eṉap payila vaḻaṅkumāṟu kāṇka. ēṟpuṭiya coṟkaḷ eccavakiyāṟ peyturikkap paṭṭaṉa.
“nī marutattup peruntuṟikkaṇ niṉ parattiyoṭu puṉalāṭiṉi yalliyō?” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟiṉi “illi” eṉat talimakaṉ maṟuttu uṭaṉpaṭā ṉāyiṉamiyiṉ, ovvāy makiḻna eṉṟum, “nī ovvā yāyiṉum, niṉṉip puṉalāṭṭiṟ kaṇṭōr, cilar allar” eṉpāḷ ivaṇ palar eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. toṉṉili marutattup peruntuṟi eṉa marutattiyum tuṟiyiṉiyum ciṟappittāḷ, paḻamiyil tiritalum kaṟpupperumiyillāp parattiyarōṭu āṭalum vilakkum kuṟippiṉaḷākaliṉ. ivvāṟu, tōḻi talimakaṉilum uyarntāḷ pōlak kūṟutal vaḻuvāyiṉum, avaṉ ceykiyāl talivikku uḷatākum uṟukaṇit tāṅkutal avaṭku iyal pākaliṉ amiyum eṉpar; “uṟukaṇ ōmpal taṉṉiyal pākaliṉ, uriyatākum tōḻikaṇ ṇuraṉē”4 eṉa āciriyar amikkumāṟu kāṇka. “ūra, periya nāṇili maṉṟa pori yeṉap, puṉkaviḻ akaṉṟuṟip poliya oṇṇutal, naṟumalark kāṇvarum kuṟumpal kūntal, māḻi nōkkiṉ kāḻiyal vaṉamuli, eḵkuṭi eḻilnalat toruttiyoṭu neruni, vikupuṉal ayarntaṉi eṉpa atuvē, poypuṟam potintu yām karappavum kiyikantu, alarāki ṉṟāl tāṉē ”5 eṉa varum paraṇar pāṭṭiṉum tōḻi alar poruḷākat talivaṉik kaḻaṟik kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. itu tōḻipāl tōṉṟiya puṟañ col māṇākkiḷavi. “oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi mēṉa”6 eṉpataṉāl, im meyppāṭu tōḻikkum koḷḷap paṭuva tāyiṟṟu. ēṉi meyppāṭu: vekuḷi. payaṉ : vāyiṉ maṟuttal.
	76.	pañcāyk kūntaṟ pacumalarc cuṇaṅkiṉ
taṇpuṉa lāṭittaṉ ṉalamēm paṭṭaṉaḷ
oṇṭoṭi maṭavara ṉiṉṉō
ṭantara makaḷirkkut teyvamum pōṉṟē.
ituvumatu.
uri :
taṇṭāṉ kōri pōlum kūntalum paciya malar pōlum cuṇaṅkum oḷḷiya toṭiyumuṭiya maṭavaralā vāḷ, niṉṉuṭaṉ taṇṇiya puṉalāṭi vāṉattu makaḷirum vaṇaṅku taṟkuriya teyvam pōṉṟu taṉ nalam mēmpaṭa lāṉāḷ; ākaliṉ, nī ataṉi maṟittuk kūṟuvateṉṉi eṉṟavāṟu.
pañcāy - taṇṭāṉkōri. itaṉi nārāyk kiḻittuk kaṇṇiyum māliyum toṭuttal marapu; “pañcāyk kōri palliṟ cavaṭṭip, puṇarnārp peyta puṉiviṉ kaṇṇi, īruṭi yiruntali yārac cūṭi”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. nīṇṭa kūntaluṭiyāḷi, ikkālattum “kōri pōl nīṇṭa talimayiruṭiyāḷ” eṉṟu makkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu muṇṭu. pacumalar eṉpuḻip pacumi putumi mēṟṟu. maṭavaral - maṭappa muṭiya peṇ. “maṭavara liṉṟuṇi”2 eṉap piṟāṇṭum varumāṟu kāṇka. ummiyi antaramakaḷirum eṉa māṟuka. parattiyi antara makaḷirkkut teyvam pōṉṟu eṉṟatu kuṟippumoḻi.
kulamakaḷir tam kaṟpuṭimiyiṉāl antara makaḷirum vaṇaṅkum pattiṉit teyvamāy nalam mēmpaṭṭup palarum aṟiyattakka viḷakka meytuvar; “vāṉuṟi makaḷir nalaṉ ikal koḷḷum, vayaṅkiḻi karanta vaṇṭupaṭu katuppiṉ, oṭuṅkī rōtik koṭuṅ kuḻi kaṇava”3 eṉpataṉāl kulamakaḷir kaṟpāl vāṉōr paravum nalam mēmpaṭumāṟu kāṇka. maṟṟu, niṉ maṭavaral, niṉṉoṭu taṇpuṉalāṭiya ciṟappiṉāl, palarum aṟiya niṟpatoru viḷakkameytiṉaḷ” eṉpāḷ, antara makaḷirkkut teyvamum pōṉṟu eṉṟāḷ. “nāṇi niṉṟōḷ nilikaṇṭu yāṉum, pēṇiṉe ṉallaṉō makiḻna, vāṉat taṇaṅkaruṅ kaṭavu ḷaṉṉōḷniṉ makaṉṟā yātal purivatāṅ keṉavē”1 eṉap piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷiṉum, taliviyāl, paratti antara makaḷiriṉ vittu urikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. “pala makaḷirik kūṭi nalamuṇṭu kaḻikkum niṉ maṉattip piṇittu nī piriyātu uṭaṉvarac ceṉṟu puṉalāṭiṉa ḷākaliṉ, avaḷ ēṉip palariṉum nalam mēmpaṭṭavaḷ eṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭṭatu” eṉpāḷ, taṇpuṉalāṭit taṉ nalam mēmpaṭṭaṉaḷ eṉṟāḷ. vaṇṭō raṉiya niṉṉip piṇit taliṉ nalamikutiyum, parattiyar palarum kaṇṭu tam māṭṭāmi niṉintu iḷivaral eytumāṟu niṟṟaliṉ, viḷakka mikutiyum uṭiyaḷāyiṉaḷ eṉṟavāṟām. “kiḻavōḷ piṟaḷkuṇam iviyeṉak kūṟik, kiḻavōṉ kuṟippiṉi yuṇartaṟku muriyaḷ”2 eṉpataṉuḷ, “paratti yeṉṉātu piṟaḷ eṉṟa taṉāl,...... tōḻi kūṟuṅkāl taliviyarik kūṟappeṟāḷ eṉpatūum, parattiyarik kūṟiṉ, avarkku mutukkuṟimi kūṟik kūṟuvaḷ eṉpatūum koḷka.” eṉak kūṟi, itu pōla varum tōḻikūṟṟu amikkappaṭum. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ : orumukattāl vāyil maṟuttavāṟu.
nalam mēmpaṭṭaṉa voṇṭoṭi eṉṟum, piñ cāyk kūntal eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu. piñcāy - paciya kōri; “paḻaṉap piñcāy koḻuti”3 eṉa varutal kāṇka.
	77.	amma vāḻiyō makiḻnaniṉ moḻival
pērū ralareḻa nīralik kalaṅki
niṉṉoṭu taṇpuṉa lāṭutum
emmoṭu ceṉmō cellaṉiṉ maṉiyē.
muṉṉoru ñāṉṟu taliviyōṭu puṉalāṭiṉāṉ eṉak kēṭṭu, “ivaṉuṭaṉ iṉi āṭēṉ” eṉa uṭkoṇṭa paratti, “putuppuṉal āṭap pōtuka” eṉṟa talimakaṟkuc colliyatu.
uri :
makiḻna, niṉakku oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ, kēṭpāyāka: nī niṉ maṉikkuc cellātu emmōṭu kūṭip pōtaruvāyāyiṉ, yām ipperiya ūr alarkūṟa, nīr alittalāṟ kalaṅki makiḻcci mikumāṟu taṇṇiya puṉalāṭuvōm eṉṟavāṟu.
amma, kēṭpittaṟ poruṭṭāya iṭiccol. moḻi val eṉṟa orumi parattittalivi mēṟṟu. āṭutum, em moṭu eṉa niṉṟa paṉmi, at taliviyiyum, avaḷatu āyattiyum uḷappaṭuttu niṉṟatu. alikkalaṅki eṉpatu toki nūlkaḷil mikappayiṉṟa vaḻakkiṟṟāya coṟṟoṭar. “peyalalik kalaṅkiya malippūṅ kōti,” “paṉiyalik kalaṅkiya neñcam”1 “veñciṉa vēntaṉ pakiyalik kalaṅki,” “nōyalik kalaṅkiya mataṉaḻi poḻutu ”2 eṉa varumāṟu kāṇka. “nīralik kaliiya īritaḻt toṭiyal,” “nīralik kali iyakaṇṇi” “nīralik kaliiya kūḻi ”3 eṉa vēṟu vāy pāṭṭiṉum vaḻaṅkutaluṇṭu. ceṉmō, cellal eṉpaṉa eccap poruḷavāyc celliṉ, cellātu eṉa niṉṟaṉa.
muṉpu taliviyōṭu puṉalāṭiṉāṉ eṉak kēṭṭu, “iva ṉuṭaṉ iṉi āṭēṉ” eṉa uṭkoṇṭu pulantirunta parat tiyi appulavi nīkkit taṉṉōṭu puṉalāṭa vēṇṭiya talimakaṟku uṭaṉpaṭuvāḷ. niṉṉoṭu taṇpuṉa lāṭutum eṉṟum, atu kuṟittu avaṉ taṉmaṉikku ēkātu tammoṭu pōntu nīrāṭa vēṇṭum eṉpāḷ, emmoṭu ceṉmō cellal niṉ maṉiyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taṉakkuriya maṉikku ēkātavāṟu talivaṉit takittal ariya ceya lātaliṉ, ataṉi moḻivāḷ mutaṟkaṇ niṉmoḻival eṉa mukampukuntāḷ. tāṉ āṭaṟku virumpum puṉalāṭṭu maṟinta nikaḻcciyākātu, palarum aṟiya nikaḻvatoṉṟāka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, puṉalāṭṭum neṭitu āṭik kaṇ civappuṟak kaḷikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum kuṟippāḷ, pērūr alareḻa eṉavum, nīralikkalaṅki eṉavum moḻintāḷ. itaṉāṟ payaṉ, talivi kēṭṭup poṟātu pulanta uḷḷattaḷāvatu. cellal niṉ maṉiyē eṉṟataṉāṟ parattipāl poṟāmik kuṟippu meyppaṭṭatu.
itu, “pullutal mayakkum pulavik kaṇṇum”1 eṉṟa cūttirattu, “eṇṇiya paṇṇiyeṉ ṟivaṟṟoṭu piṟavum” eṉpuḻi, paṇṇi poruḷāka nikaḻum kūṟṟu vaki yākum. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ, oru mukattāṟ pulantavāṟu.
	78.	katirili neṭuvēṟ kaṭumāṉ kiḷḷi
matilkol yāṉiyiṟ kataḻpuneṟi vanta
ciṟiyaḻi putuppuṉa lāṭukam
emmoṭu koṇmōven tōḷpuri puṇiyē.
ituvumatu.
uri :
makiḻna, niṉ maṉikkaṭ ceṉṟu niṉ maṉiviyoṭu kūṭip puṉalāṭuvati viṭuttu emmoṭu kūṭip pōntu em tōḷi nikarkkum puṇiyip paṟṟip puṉalāṭuvi yāyiṉ, yām oḷivīcum iliyiṉiyuṭiya neṭiya vēlum virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya kutiriyumuṭiya kiḷḷiyiṉatu pakivar kaḷiṉ matili aḻikkum yāṉipōlat taṉṉeṟiyē virintu vantu ciṟiyi aḻikkum putuppuṉalil niṉṉōṭu āṭuvēmkāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
kiḷḷi - cōḻavēntarkkuriya peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. kaṭumāṉ kiḷḷi eṉṟatu cōḻavēntaṉ oruvaṉatu iyaṟpeyarākak karutuvārum uḷar. matilkol yāṉi yeṉavē, pakivar matil eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu; “nutimuka maḻuṅka maṇṭi oṉṉār, kaṭimatil pāyumniṉ kaḷiṟuaṭaṅ kalavē”2 eṉpataṉālum aṟika. kataḻpu, virivup poruṭṭāya uriccolliṉ aṭiyākap piṟanta viṉiyeccam; “kataḻvum tuṉivum viriviṉ  poruḷa”3 eṉa āciriyar kūṟuvar. matilkol yāṉiyiṉ ciṟiyaḻi putuppuṉal, neṟivanta putuppuṉal eṉa iyikka. neṟi varutalāvatu, karikaṭantu ceṉṟu tīṅku ceyyātu, vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu pāyntu varuvatu; “vēṇṭu vaḻi naṭantu tāṅkutaṭi porutu...... putunāṟṟañ ceykiṉṟē cempūm puṉal”1 eṉavarum paripāṭal kāṇka. iṉi, neṟivanta ciṟiyaḻi putuppuṉal eṉpataṟku, neṟiyiṭi akappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷik koṇarntu eṟṟic ciṟiyi aḻikkum putup puṉal eṉiṉumām. “mipaṭu cilampiṟ kaṟiyoṭum cāntoṭum, neykuṭi tayiriṉ nuriyoṭum piṟavoṭum, ev vayiṉāṉum mītumī taḻiyum”2 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟupa. ciṟi, aṇi. āṭukam eṉpuḻi, ammīṟu kakaravoṟṟup peṟṟu muṭintatu. puṇi, vēḻattāṟ ceyta teppam; “vēḻaveṇ puṇi,” “koḻuṅ kōl vēḻattup puṇi”3 eṉa varutal kāṇka.
talimakaṉ paṇṭu taliviyuṭaṉ puṉalāṭiṉāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭup poṟātu pulakkiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, atu tōṉṟa emmoṭu koṇmō eṉṟum, talimakaḷōṭu āṭiya puṇi avaḷuṭiya tōḷ pōṟaliṉ, ataṉi viṭuttut taṉ tōḷ pōlum piṟitoru puṇi koḷka eṉpāḷ, emtōḷ puri puṇi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. ivvāṟu, poṟāmiyāl, “iṉi, ivaṉuṭaṉ puṉal āṭēṉ” eṉap paratti taṉṉuṭ koṇ ṭirunta kuṟippu naṉku veḷippaṭa, putuppuṉa lāṭukam eṉṟāḷ. emmoṭu koṇmōem tōḷpuri puṇiyē eṉpatu meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ ākāteṉa vilakkiya nimpiri yāyiṉum, parattiyiṉ kātaṟ karuttic ciṟappittaliṉ amiyumeṉka; “ciṉaṉē pētimi nimpiri nalkuravu, aṉināl vakiyum ciṟappoṭu varumē”4 eṉa āciriyar vitikkumā ṟaṟika. itu ciṟupāṉmi parattikkum eytappeṟum; avaḷum taliva ṉoṭu ūṭiyum uṇarntum urittaṟ kuriya ḷākaliṉ. piṟāṇṭum itupōlvaṉa aṟintu kūṟikkoḷka.
pakivar tamakku araṇāka amitta matilik kiḷḷiyiṉ yāṉi aḻittāṟpōla, uyirkaḷiṉ nalappēṟṟukku araṇāka aminta ciṟiyiṉip putuppuṉal aḻikkum eṉṟataṉāl, eṅkaḷ iṉpappēṟṟukku araṇāka aminta niṉ kūṭṭatti nī taliviyuṭaṉ āṭum puṉalāṭṭu aḻikkum eṉṟāḷām. ākavē, iḵtu uvamiyum poruḷumāy niṉṟu karup poruṭkuc ciṟappuk koṭuttaliṉ, tiṇiyuṇarvakiyil ēṉiyuvama meṉat taḷḷappaṭā tāyiṟṟu, “uḷḷuṟi yuvamam ēṉi yuvamameṉat, taḷḷā tākum tiṇiyuṇar vakiyē”1 eṉa āciriyar kūṟuvar. “kiḻavōṟ kāyiṉ uraṉoṭu kiḷakkum, ēṉōrk kellām iṭamvari viṉṟē”2 eṉpavākaliṉ, parattiyāl iḵtu amivatāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, “itu, kāmakkiḻatti niṉ maṉiviyō ṭaṉṟi emmoṭu puṇikoḷḷiṉ yām āṭutum eṉṟu puṉalāṭṭiṟku iyintāḷ pōla maṟuttatu” eṉpar. meyppāṭum payaṉum avi.
katimāṉ eṉṟum katavuneri tanta eṉṟum pāṭa vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
	79.	putuppuṉa lāṭi yamartta kaṇṇaḷ
yārmaka ḷivaḷeṉap paṟṟiya makiḻna
yārmaka ḷāyiṉum maṟiyāy
nīyār makaṉiyem paṟṟi yōyē.
taṉṉoṭu kūṭātu taṉittup puṉalāṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉak kēṭṭut taliniṉṟu oḻukappaṭāniṉṟa paratti tāṉum taṉiyē pōyp puṉalāṭiṉāḷāka, avaḷi ūṭal tīrttaṟ poruṭṭākat talimakaṉ ceṉṟu tāṉ aṟiyāṉ  pōla nakiyāṭik kūṟik kippaṟṟiyavaḻi avaḷtōḻi colliyatu.
uri :
putup puṉalāṭic civanta kaṇṇi yuṭiya ḷākiya  ivaḷ yāvarmakaḷ eṉac collik kippaṟṟiya makiḻna, ivaḷ yāvarmakaḷāyiṉum aṟiyāmalē em kiyip paṟṟiṉi yākaliṉ nī yāvarmakaṉ? ataṉi muṉpu kūṟuka eṉṟavāṟu.
amarttal - īṇṭuc civattal; nīril neṭitāṭiyavaḻik kaṇ civakkum; “nīrnīṭāṭiṟ kaṇṇum civakkum”1 eṉpa. colli eṉpatu colleccam. āyiṉum eṉṟatu, ivaḷ niṉṉāl naṉku aṟiyappaṭṭavaḷ eṉpatu eytuvittatu. tōḻi em paṟṟiyōy eṉṟatu, “tāyatti ṉaṭiyā vīyac cellā, viṉivayiṉ taṅkā vīṟṟuk koḷappaṭā, emmeṉa varūuṅ kiḻamit tōṟṟam, allā vāyiṉum pulluva vuḷavē”2 eṉpataṉāl amiyum. makaṉi eṉpuḻi aikāram cāriyi.
tāṉ avaḷōṭu kūṭip puṉalāṭātu taṉittāṭiṉāṉāka, atu kaṇṭu poṟātu taṉittāṭi avaṉ talippeyarumaḷavum appuṉalilēyē neṭitu iruntāḷākaliṉ, putuppuṉalāṭi amartta kaṇṇaḷ eṉṟum, avaḷ pārvitāṉum avaḷ maṉattu nikaḻnta pulavik kuṟippi iṉitu pulappaṭutti ayaṉmi tōṉṟa niṉṟamiyiṉ yārmakaḷivaḷ eṉṟum kūṟi appulavi tīrumāṟu kippaṟṟiṉāṉāyiṟṟu.
meyyē aṟiyāṉāyiṉ, yārmakaḷ ivaḷ eṉa viṉavik koṇṭē, avaḷik kippaṟṟā ṉākaliṉ, īṇṭi viṉā, aṟintuvittum aṟiyāṉpōla viṉaviyatām. ākavē, itaṉāṟ payaṉ paratti pulavitīrvāḷāvatu. maṟṟu, aḵtu aṅṅaṉamākātu mikutal kaṇṭu, atu tīrumāṟu kippaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu tōṉṟat tōḻi, paṟṟiya makiḻna eṉṟāḷ. tali niṉṟoḻukum parattiyi aṟiyāṉpōlat talivaṉ makka ḷiṉat taṉṟit tēva makaḷpōla nalañciṟantu viḷaṅkum ivaḷ eṉṉum karuttuppaṭa yār makaḷ ivaḷ eṉṟāṉāka, “ivaḷ niṉ peṇṭē” eṉiṉum ataṉi maṟantu taṉittup pōyp puṉa lāṭiṉi yātaliṉ itupōtu iṉṉaḷ eṉa eṭuttuk kūṟiṉum nī aṟiyāy eṉpāḷ yār makaḷāyiṉum aṟiyāy eṉṟum, aṟiyāy pōla viṉavuvatum, aṟiyumuṉpē aṟintāy pōlak kippaṟṟuvatum ceytaliṉ niṉṉi naṉku aṟiya virumpu kiṉṟēmeṉpāḷ, nī yār makaṉi em paṟṟiyōyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. meyppāṭu : vekuḷi. payaṉ : vāyil maṟuttal.
	80.	pulakkuvē mallēm poyyā turimō
nalattaki makaḷirkkut tōṭṭuṇi yākit
talippeyaṟ cempuṉa lāṭit
tavanaṉi civantaṉa makiḻnaniṉ kaṇṇē.
taṉṉi yoḻiyap putuppuṉalāṭit tāḻttu vanta tali makaṉōṭu talimakaḷ pulantu colliyatu.
uri :
makiḻna, naṟkuṇaṅkaḷāl takutiyuṭiyarāya makaḷirkkut tōṭṭuṇiyāki, mutaṟpeyalākiya maḻiyāl peruk kuṟṟu vanta putuppuṉal āṭiṉamiyāl, niṉ kaṇkaḷ mikac civantaṉa; aḵtu uṇmiyaṉṟō? kūṟuka. atupaṟṟi yām pulappēmallēm; nī poyyātu urip pāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
nalattaki makaḷir eṉṟatu parattiyari. puṉalāṭu makaḷir puṇipōlap paṟṟi nīrāṭutaṟkuriya tuṇimi nalkutal nōkki avaṉ kūṭṭattit tōṭṭuṇi eṉṟār. vēṉiṟ paruvattup peyyum maḻi āṇṭiṉ mutaṉmaḻi yātaliṉ talippeyal eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉāṟ peruki varum puṉal teḷiviṉṟik kalaṅki varutal paṟṟic cempuṉal eṉṟār. āṭi yeṉṉum ceyteṉeccam kāraṇapporuṭṭu. tavanaṉi eṉṉum uric coṟkaḷ, “palacol oru poruṭkurimi tōṉṟiṉum” 1 eṉpataṉāl mikutipporuṭku urimi peṟṟu. vantaṉa.
taṉ kaṇ civantiruttal talimakaḷ pulattaṟku ētuvām eṉṟu karutit talivaṉ tāḻttu vantāṉāyiṉum, ataṉi uṇarnta avaḷ, avaṉ maṟippāṉ eṉa uṭkoṇṭu, pulakkuvēm allēm poyyātu urimō eṉṟum, tāṉ pulavāmi tōṉṟap parattiyip pārāṭṭi, nalattaki makaḷirkku eṉṟum, avarōṭu puṉalāṭiyataṉit tāṉ aṟintami eytat tōṭṭuṇiyāki eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
putuppuṉal eytuṅkāl kātalaṉuṭaṉ kūṭiyāṭutaṟku ikkaṟpukkālattēyaṉṟik kaḻinta kaḷavukkālattum viruppam mikavuṭiyaḷā yiruntamiyiṉ, atu tōṉṟat talippeyaṟ cempuṉal eṉac ciṟappittāḷ. kaḷavuk kālattil, “kāntaḷañ ciṟukuṭik kuvi pēṇātu, arimatar maḻikkaṇ civappa nāḷip, perumali nāṭaṉ mārpu puṇiyāka, āṭukam vammō kātalan tōḻi............... koṭipaṭa miṉṉip pāyiṉṟāl maḻiyē 1” eṉat talivi kūṟutal kāṇka. parattiyarōṭu puṉalāṭiya nikaḻcciyi nī maṟikkiṉum niṉ kaṇkaḷ maṟi yāvāyc civantu tōṉṟi yāṉ aṟiyak kāṭṭi viṭṭaṉa eṉṟaṟkut tavanaṉi civantaṉa makiḻna niṉ kaṇṇē eṉṟāḷ. “nīr nīṭu āṭiṉ kaṇṇum civakkum 2” eṉpar cāṉṟōr.
meyppāṭu : vekuḷi. payaṉ : pulattal.
9. pulavi virāya pattu
itaṉkaṇ varum pāṭṭukkaḷ pattum, taliviyum tōḻi yum parattiyum pulantu kūṟuvaṉavum vāyiṉ maṟuttuk kūṟuvaṉa vum, piṟavumāy viravivarutaliṉ, iḵtu ippeyariṉip peṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
	81.	kurukuṭit tuṇṭa veḷḷakaṭ ṭiyāmi
arippaṟi viṉiña ralkumicik kūṭṭum
malaraṇi vāyiṟ poyki yūranī
eṉṉi nayantaṉe ṉeṉṟiniṉ
maṉiyōḷ kēṭkiṉ varuntuvaḷ peritē.
taṉṉik koṭumi kūṟiṉāḷ talimakaḷ eṉpatu kēṭṭa paratti, talimakaṉ vantu taṉmēl aṉpuṭimi kūṟiṉāṉāka, avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
kurukuṭit tuṇṭa yāmi miccili viṉiñar uṇaviṟ kūṭṭum ūra eṉavē, nāṅkaḷ nukarntu kaḻitta mārpi nukarvāḷ eṉṟu talimakaḷip paḻittavāṟām.
uri :
kurukukaḷ uṭittu uṇṭu kaḻitta veḷḷiya vayiṟṟiṉi yuṭiya yāmi yiṟicciyiṉi, arip paṟiyi muḻakkutali yuṭiya uḻavar tamatu mikkavuṇavōṭu kūṭṭiyuṇṇum malarāl aḻakuṟṟa tuṟiyiṉi yuṭiya ūraṉē, nī eṉṉi virumpiṉēṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉi; maṟṟu, itaṉi niṉ maṉivi kēṭpiṉ poṟātu mikavum varuntuvaḷkāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
kuruku - kokku, nāri mutaliya puḷḷiṉaṅkaḷ. akaṭu - vayiṟu. arippaṟi, aritta ōciyiṉiyuṭiya paṟi. nellari yum uḻavar vayaṟkaṇ viḷinta nelli aṟukkap pukumuṉ avvayaliṟ kūṭamittu vāḻum puḷḷiṉaṅkaḷ muṉṉaṟintu ētamiṉṟi nīṅkuvatu kuṟittu ip paṟiyōciyi eḻuppuvatu paṇṭit tamiḻar marapu. “kaḻicuṟṟiya viḷikaḻaṉi, arip paṟiyāṟ puḷḷōppuntu”1 eṉa māṅkuṭikiḻārum, “veṇ ṇel arinar taṇṇumi verīip. paḻaṉap palpuḷ iriya”2 eṉap paraṇarum kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. alkumici - mikka uṇavu; “arilivar puṟṟattu alkiri nacii”3 eṉa varumāṟṟāl uṇarka. iṉi, “cila nāḷikku iṭṭu vit tuṇṇum uṇavu” eṉpar puṟanāṉūṟṟup paḻiya vurikārar. piṟa uyirkaḷ uṇṭu kaḻitta miccili makkaḷ uṇṇum muṟi, “ōypacic cennāy uyaṅkumari tolicci, ārntaṉa oḻinta miccil cēynāṭṭu, aruñcuram celvōrkku valci yākum”4 eṉa varutalālum teḷiyappaṭum. poykikkut tuṟi vāyil pōṟaliṉ, vāyil eṉṟār. eṉṟi: ṟakaram ūrnta muṉṉili nikaḻkāla muṟṟuviṉi.
talimakaḷ taṉṉip puṟaṉurittāḷ eṉpatu kēṭṭup pulanta paratti ap pulavi tīrumāṟu talivaṉ tāṉ avaḷi iṉṟiyamiyāmi kūṟiṉāṉāka, ataṉi mēṟkoṇṭa apparatti, eṉṉi nayantaṉeṉ eṉṟi eṉa avaṉ kūṟiya ataṉiyē koṇṭeṭuttumoḻintāḷ. talivaṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiyataṉāl perumakiḻvu koṇṭavaḷ talivi kēṭṭup poṟātu varuntavēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttiṉāl niṉ maṉiyōḷ kēṭkiṉ varuntuvaḷ peritē eṉṟāḷ. “koṭumi yoḻukkam kōṭal vēṇṭi, aṭimēl vīḻnta kiḻavaṉi neruṅkik, kātal eṅkiyar kāṇiṉ naṉṟu” eṉat talivaṉ parattimi kūṟit talivi pulantāḷāka, atu taṉṉip puṟa ṉurittatākak koṇṭu paratti at taliviyi ikaḻva tākiya kuṟipput tōṉṟa varutaliṉ, itu “pullutal mayakkum pulavik kaṇṇum”1 eṉṟa cūttirattu, “illōr ceyviṉi ikaḻccikkaṇ” nikaḻum kūṟṟu vakiyākum.
kurukukaḷ uṭittu uṇṭu kaḻitta yāmimiccili viṉi ñar tam uṇaviṟ kūṭṭi uṇpar eṉṟatu, nāṅkaḷ nukarntu kaḻitta niṉ mārpiṉi nukarvāḷ niṉmaṉiyōḷ eṉa uḷ ḷuṟuttu urittavāṟu. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ : taliviyi ikaḻtal.
iṉi, viṉiñar nalkumici eṉṟa pāṭam, piṟarkku nalki yuṇṇum uṇavu eṉṟavāṟu. pāttūṇ vāḻkki ellā makkaṭkum potuvākaliṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār eṉka. eṉavē, niṉ mārpu taṉakkēyaṉṟi emakkum urittātali aṟiyātu pulakkiṉṟāḷ niṉmaṉiyōḷ eṉap paratti kūṟiṉāḷām.
	82.	vekuṇṭaṉa ḷeṉpa pāṇaniṉ ṟalimakaḷ
makiḻnaṉ mārpi ṉaviḻiṇar naṟuntārt
tātuṇ paṟavi vantem
pōtār kūnta liruntaṉa veṉavē.
maṉivayiṟ pukunta pāṇaṟkut talimakaṉ kēṭkumāṟṟāl talimakaḷ colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
ituvum poṟātavaḷ nī īṇṭu varutal poṟāḷ, kaṭitiṟ celka eṉpatām.
uri :
pāṇa, makiḻnaṉatu mārpiṉkaṇ uḷḷa muṟukku aviḻnta pūṅkottukkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa naṟiya māliyil tēṉi yuṇṭa vaṇṭiṉam pōntu ematu malaraṇi kūntalil taṅkiṉa eṉṟu avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kūṟak kēṭṭalum niṉ taliviyākiya paratti vekuṇṭaṉaḷ eṉṟu palaruṅ kūṟā niṉṟaṉar; ituvum poṟātāḷ, nī īṇṭu varutali aṟiyiṉ iṟappavum vekuḷuvaḷ, ākaliṉ, kaṭitiṟ celvāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
malararumpu piṇittu muṟukkiyatu pōṟaliṉ, malarntavaḻi ataṉi muṟukku aviḻnta vāypāṭṭāl, aviḻiṇar eṉ ṟār; “piṅkāṟ koṉṟi meṉpiṇi aviḻa”1 eṉac cāṉ ṟōr vaḻaṅkumāṟu kāṇka. tātuṇ paṟavi, vaṇṭiṉam; “tātuṇ paṟavi pētuṟal añci”2 eṉpa. ēṉip puḷḷiṉaṅ kaḷilum tēṉ uṇpaṉa uḷavāyiṉum, tātuṇ paṟavi yeṉpatu vaṇṭiṉattiyē kuṟippatu, pāṉmipaṟṟiya vaḻakku eṉa aṟika. tēṉ tēṭi uṇṇum puḷ tēṉciṭṭu 3 eṉa vaḻaṅkum. taliviyatu illiṉkaṇ nikaḻntatu uṇarntu parattiyarkkuk kūṟuvōr avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉārē yākaliṉ, atu varuvittu urikkappaṭṭatu; “cāṉṟōṉ eṉak kēṭṭa tāy” 4 eṉpa taṟkup parimēlaḻakar kūṟiya uri kāṇka. niṉ talimakaḷ eṉṟatu parattiyi iḻittaṟ kuṟippiṟṟāy uyarcoṟ kiḷavi. ituvum poṟātāḷ eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa eñci niṉṟaṉa.
makiḻ ceyyum tēṉi yuṇṇum vaṇṭiṉa mākaliṉ, talivaṉ tāriluḷḷa tēṉi uṇṭataṉōṭu amiyātu, em kūntaṟ pōtukaḷiluḷḷa tēṉi virumpi vantiruntaṉa eṉpāḷ tātuṇ paṟavi eṉṟum, em pōtār kūntal eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. talimakaṉ tāriṉi aviḻiṇar naṟuntār eṉṟum, taṉ kūntalip pōtār kūntal eṉṟum ciṟappittataṉāl, talivaṉ tāriṟ pūkkaḷ malarntu tēṉkamaḻa iruntamiyum, tam kūntaliṟ pūkkaḷ malarum cevviyavāy iruntamiyum cuṭṭi ṉāḷ. itaṉāṟ payaṉ, mayakka mikutiyāl ivvēṟṟumi kāṇamāṭṭāta vaṇṭiṉ ceykiyip poruḷākak koṇṭu vekuḷutal naṉṟaṉṟu eṉap parattiyip paḻittavāṟu; “tātuṇ vēṭkiyiṟ pōtu terintu ūtā vaṇṭu”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr vaṇṭiṉ iyalpu viḷaṅkak kūṟiyavāṟu aṟika.
talivaṉ tāriṉatu malarttēṉi uṇṭa vaṇṭu, talivi kūntalil aṇinta pōtukaḷ malarum cevvi nōkki iruntaṉa eṉat taṉakkup pāṅkāyiṉār collak kēṭṭa paratti taṉpāl iṉpam nukarnta talivaṉ taṉ maṉiyakam pukkut talivi pulavi nīṅkum cevvi nōkkiyiruntāṉ eṉa uṭ koṇṭu vekuṇṭāḷāka, ataṉi uṇarnta talivi poṟātu avaṉip paḻittum pāṇaṉi maṟuttum nikaḻntavāṟu kūṟi vekuṇṭaṉaḷ eṉṟum, ataṉip palarum kūṟāniṟpar eṉṟaṟku eṉpa eṉṟum eṭuttu moḻintāḷ. poyyuri moḻiyum iyalpiṉaṉākiya pāṇaṉ tāṉ kūṟuvataṉiyum avvāṟē koḷvaṉ eṉṉum karuttāl taṉ kūṟṟiṉ vāymi piṟaṅka, eṉpa eṉap piṟarmēl vittu moḻikiṉṟāḷ eṉka. meyp pāṭu : vekuḷi. payaṉ : vāyiṉ maṟuttal.
iḵtu, “avaṉaṟivu āṟṟa aṟiyumākaliṉ”2 eṉṟa cūttirattu, “vāyiliṉ varūum vaki” eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkum.
	83.	maṇantaṉi yaruḷā yāyiṉum pipayat
taṇantaṉi yāki yuymmō nummūr
oṇṭoṭi muṉki yāyamun
taṇṭuṟi yūraṉ peṇṭeṉap paṭaṟkē.
varinta aṇumikkaṇṇē talimakaṟkup puṟat toḻukkam uṇṭākiyavaḻi ataṉi aṟinta talivi avaṉōṭu pulantu colliyatu.
uri :
ūra, emmi maṇantukoṇṭa nī emakku niṉṉuṭiya aruḷi neṭitu ceyyātoḻiyiṉum, numatu ūriluḷḷa oḷḷiya toṭiyaṇinta muṉkiyiṉiyuṭiya parattiyar palarum taṇṭuṟi yūraṉākiya niṉakkup peṇṭu eṉak kūṟap
paṭum ciṟappup peṟumāṟu mella mella nīṅki oḻukuvāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
aruḷutal - taliyaḷittal. piya piya eṉṟa aṭukkup pipaya eṉa marūu vāyiṟṟu; mella mella eṉpatu poruḷ; “paṭarum pipayap peyarum”1 eṉpa. uytal, īṇṭu oḻukutaṉ mēṟṟu. taṇṭuṟiyūraṉ peṇṭu - taṇṇiya tuṟiyiṉi yuṭiya ūraṟkuriya kāmak kiḻattiyar; itu “kūṟṟuavaṇ iṉmiyiṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟiya” koṇṭukūṟṟu. talivaṉik kūṭi viḷiyāṭi iṉpuṟum iyipu paṟṟip parattiyarit talimakaṟku āyam eṉṟār. eṉappaṭaṟku eṉpatu, “urupēṟṟu niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyarōṭu oppumi yuṭiyatāyiṉum, urupum peyarum eṉṟākātu, pakuppap piḷavupaṭṭu iciyātu oṉṟu paṭṭicittalāl”2 ataṉiṉ vēṟākiya ceyaṟkeṉṉum viṉiyeccamāyt taṇantaṉiyāki eṉpataṉōṭu muṭintatu.
varinta aṇimikkaṇṇē talivaṉpāl puṟat toḻukkam uṇṭāyiṉami aṟintukoṇṭa talivi, maṇantaṉi aruḷāy eṉṟum, piriviṉṟi emmoṭu uṭaṉ uṟintu aruḷāyāyiṉum, nī neṭitu pirintuṟiyiṉ niṉatu kāṭciṉ pamum peṟal iṉṟi varuttam mikku uṟivatu eṉakku iṉṉā tākaliṉ. mella mellap pirintuṟivati vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ eṉpāḷ, pipayat taṇantaṉiyāki uymmō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ; “tuṉitīr kūṭṭamoṭu tuṉṉā rāyiṉum, iṉitē kāṇunark kāṇpuḻi vāḻtal”3 eṉap piṟarum kūṟumāṟṟāl aṟika. taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyum cuṭṭi nummūr eṉṟāḷ. maṇantavarip piriviṉṟik kūṭiyiruntu makiḻātu nāḷum palappala makaḷirik kūṭuvatu, ap palarum niṉpeṇṭir eṉappaṭuvatu oṉṟiyē karutiyateṉpatu niṉ oḻukkattāl viḷaṅkiṟ ṟeṉpāḷ, taṇṭuṟiyūraṉ peṇṭu eṉappaṭaṟku eṉṟāḷām. āyamum eṉṟa ecca ummi, niṉ peṇṭu eṉappaṭumāṟu eṉṉi maṇantatēyaṉṟi, oṇṭoṭi muṉki āyattiyum avvaṇṇamē ceytu oḻukukiṉṟāy eṉpatu eytuvittatu. parattiyari oṇṭoṭi muṉki āyam eṉac ciṟappittataṉāl, avarkaḷ peṇṇiyal poṉṟiyē yuṭiyarāvaraṉṟi, empōl naṉṟi cāṉṟa kaṟpuṭiyarātal ilar eṉṟāḷām.
iḵtu “avaṉaṟivu āṟṟa aṟiyumākaliṉ”1 eṉṟa cūttirattu, “cellāk kālic celkeṉa” viṭuttaṟ kaṇ talivi nikaḻt tum kūṟṟu vakiyākum. meyppāṭu: vekuḷi. payaṉ : pulattal.
piya eṉṟum pāṭam uṇṭu.
	84.	ceviyiṟ kēṭpiṉum colliṟantu vekuḷvōḷ
kaṇṇiṟ kāṇi ṉeṉṉā kuvaḷkol
tiit taṇkayam pōlap
palarpaṭin tuṇṇuniṉ paratti mārpē.
parattiyar maṉikkaṇ taṅkip puṇarccikkuṟiyōṭu vāyil vēṇṭi vanta talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.
uri :
makiḻna, naṟiya pūkkaḷ aṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya makaḷir paṭintāṭum tiit taṇkayam pōlap palar muyaṅki iṉpam nukarum niṉmārpiṉic ciṟappittup piṟar kūṟat  taṉ ceviyiṟ kēṭpiṉum colliṟantu ciṉaṅ koḷḷum ivaḷ, paratti ceyta kuṟiyoṭu tōṉṟum ataṉit taṉ kaṇkaḷālē kāṇpāḷāyiṉ eṉṉāvaḷō? aṟiyēṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
colliṟantu vekuḷalāvatu collāṭaṟ kiyalāc ciṉam mikutal; collāṭutaṟku nāveḻāta attuṇi aḷa viṟanta ciṉam koḷḷutal. aimpāl - kūntal; aivaki oppaṉi uṭimipaṟṟi itu peyarāyiṟṟu. avi, muṭi koṇṭi kuḻal paṉicci curuḷ eṉa varum. “neyyiṭi nīvi maṇiyoḷi viṭṭaṉṉa, aivaki pārāṭṭiṉāy maṟṟu em kūntaṟ. ceyviṉi pārāṭṭiṉiyō aiya”2 eṉṟum, “aimpāl vakutta kūntal”3 eṉṟum varuvaṉa kāṇka. tiit taṇkayam, tittiṅkaḷil makaḷirāṭum  taṇṇiya kuḷam; teḷinta nīr taṇṇitākaliṉ teḷivum koḷka. kārkālattup perumiyāl nīr perukik kalakkamuṟṟa kayam, tit tiṅkaḷil naṉku teḷintu taṇṇi tātalāl, teṇṇīrk kayameṉṉātu, tiittaṇkayam eṉ ṟār. “tiit tiṅkaḷ taṇkayam pōlak, koḷakkuṟi paṭāk kūḻuṭi viyaṉakar” 1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. “uṇṭaṟ kuriya allāp poruḷi, uṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalum marapē”2 eṉpataṉāl, muyaṅkip peṟum iṉpa nukarcciyi, uṇṇum eṉṟār. paratti mārpu, paratti ceyta kuṟi kiṭantu viḷaṅkum mārpu. mārpu ceviyiṟ kēṭpiṉum vekuḷvōḷ, kaṇṇiṟ kāṇiṉ eṉṉākuvaḷ kol eṉa iyiyum.
makaḷirāṭum taṇkayam, avaraṇinta kōtiyum kuṅkumac cāntum neyyum viravit tōṉṟutalpōla, niṉ mārpum parattiyiṉ muyakkattāl cāṭiya cāntum vāṭiya kōtiyum uṭittāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉpāḷ, niṉ paratti mārpē eṉṟār; “kuvavumuli cāṭiya cāntiṉi, vāṭiya kōtiyi”3 eṉa varutal kāṇka. iḷiyarum mutiyarum eṉap palarum āṭum kayampōla, niṉṉik kūṭum parattiyarum avvakippaṭṭa palar eṉṟaṟkut taṇkayam pōlap palar paṭintuṇṇum mārpu eṉṟāḷ; “vaṇkayam pōlum niṉ mārpu”4 eṉṟu piṟa cāṉṟōrum kūṟiṉar. tittiṅkaḷil iḷa makaḷir nīrāṭi nōṟpatu marapākaliṉ, tiit taṇkayam eṉac ciṟappittāḷ; “iḻiyaṇi yāyamoṭu takunāṇ taṭiit, tiit tiṅkaḷ taṇkayam paṭiyum, peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ”5 tāyarukā niṉṟu tavattin nī rāṭutal”6  “tiyilnī rāṭiya tavam”7 eṉa varumāṟu kāṇka.
niṉ mārpiṉ ciṟappiṉip piṟar pukaḻntu kūṟak kēṭṭa tuṇiyāṉē poṟātu kaḻiciṉaṅkoṇṭu collāṭa iya lātu kiyaṟṟu niṟpavaḷ, cāṭiya cāntum vāṭiya kōtiyum koṇṭu tōṉṟum niṉ mārpiṉik kāṇiṉ uyirvāḻāḷ eṉpāḷ eṉṉākuvaḷ kol eṉat tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. ceviyil, kaṇṇil eṉa niṉṟa vitappu moḻikaḷ, muṟiyē kēṭṭal kāṇṭal mutaliya ceyal vakikaḷil avaḷ eytum maṉavēṟu pāṭṭiṉ kaṭumi uṇartti niṉṟaṉa. meyppāṭu : vekuḷi. payaṉ : vāyil maṟuttal.
paranta mārpē eṉṟum pāṭa muṇṭu.
	85.	veṇṇutaṟ kampu ḷarikkuraṟ pēṭi
taṇṇaṟum paḻaṉattuk kiḷiyō ṭālum
maṟuvil yāṇar malikē ḻūranī
ciṟuvari ṉiṉiya ceyti
nakārō perumaniṟ kaṇṭici ṉōrē.
talimakaṉ parattiyarmēl kātal kūrntu neṭittuc 1 celvaḻi maṉiyakam pukuntāṉākat talivi kūṟiyatu.
paḻiya uri :
kampuṭpēṭi cēvaloḻiyak kiḷiyuṭaṉē ālum ūra eṉṟatu, kiḷiyuṭaṉē vāḻkiṉṟa emakku niṉṉiṉ nīṅkiya melivu uḷatākak kūṟukiṉṟēmallēm, niṉ kulattoḻukkattukkut 
takātu eṉak kaḻaṟiyatām. “tāypōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻīik kōṭal, āymaṉik kiḻattikkum uritteṉa moḻipa”2  eṉṟatūum it tiṟaṉnōkki eṉak koḷka eṉpatu.
uri :
veḷḷiya taliyiṉiyuṭiya kampuṭkōḻiyiṉ aritta kuraliyuṭiya pēṭi kuḷirnta naṟuviya paḻaṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ēṉip puḷḷiṉaṅkaḷōṭu ālum kuṟṟamillāta putu varu vāy mikka ūraṉē, nī ciṟuvarkaḷip pōlap piṉṉiruntu varuntattakuvaṉavāya ivaṟṟic ceykiṉṟā yākaliṉ, peruma, niṉṉik kaṇṭōr ic ceyalpaṟṟi nakutalic ceyyārō? kūṟuka eṉṟavāṟu.
kampuṭkōḻiyiṉ tali veṇṇiṟamuṭiyatākaliṉ, veṇṇutaṟ kampuḷ eṉṟār; veṇṭalik kampuḷ eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu. arikkural, aritta ōciyiṉiyuṭiya kural; iḵtu “arikural”1 eṉṟu pāṭamum “arinta kural” eṉa uriyum kūṟappaṭuvatu muṇṭu. maṟuvil yāṇar, kuṟṟamillāta putuvaruvāy; kuṟṟamiṉmiyā, nāṭōṟum makkaḷukkup putumiyiṭattu viḻivum atu paṟṟiya muyaṟciyum peruki varutaliṉ apperukkattiṟ kēṟpa varuvāy kuṟivuṟa vārāmi. ciṟuvariṉ eṉpuḻi aintāvatu oppup poruṭṭu. iṉiya piṉṉiruntu eṇṇi iraṅkattakka icceyalkaḷ: “eṟṟeṉṟu iraṅkuva ceyyaṟka”2  eṉap periyōr teruṭṭupa. ivaṟṟic ceytavaḻi, ellārum ikaḻtalpaṟṟi, ivi vilakkappaṭuvaṉa vāyiṉa. kaṇṭiciṉōr viṉimutaṉ mēṉiṉṟa viṉiyā laṇiyum peyar.
ciṟuvar, tam ceykikaḷi mēl viḷivatu niṉiyātu ceytalpōla, nīyum, niṉ perumikkuriya aṟamum poruḷum piṟa takavuṭimiyum muṉṉar nōkkip piṉṉarc ceyvati viṭuttup piṉṉiruntu varuntattakka parattimi mēṟkoṇṭu yām tamittu varunta viṭukiṉṟaṉi eṉpāḷ, ciṟuvariṉ iṉiya ceyti eṉṟāḷ. “viḻiyā vuḷḷam viḻiyu māyiṉum eṉṟum, “kēṭṭavi tōṭṭi yāka mīṭṭāṅku, aṟaṉum poruḷum vaḻāmi nāṭit, taṉtakavuṭimi nōkki maṟṟataṉ, piṉṉā kummē muṉṉiyatu muṭittal, aṉiya periyōr oḻukkam”3 eṉavum, “ceytu piṉ ṉiraṅkā viṉiyoṭu, meyyala perum paḻi eyti ṉēṉē”4 eṉavum cāṉṟōr tōḻi kūṟṟil vittut terittiruttal kāṇka. “ciṟiyariṉ” eṉṉātu ciṟuvariṉ eṉṟāḷ, tāypōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻīit kōṭaṟ kuriya takuti paṟṟi. uḷḷuṟiyāl, talivi tāṉ taṉittiruntu varuntum cey tiyik kuṟittamiyiṉ iṉiya eṉac cuṭṭiyoḻintāḷ. talivaṉ parattimi, piṉ avaṉ tāṉē niṉintu varuntu taṟkum iṭamātaluṭiya teṉpatu, “kiḻavōṉ iṟantatu niṉii āṅkaṇ, kalaṅkalum uriyaṉ”5 eṉa āciriyar kūṟu māṟṟāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
kaṇṭōr naṉku matittaṟkuriya perumip paṇpum peruñ ceyalu muṭiyāy eṉpāḷ peruma eṉṟum, eṉavē, perumikku māṟāṉa ivvoḻukkam alarum eḷḷaṟpāṭum payantu kaṇṭōr nakumāṟu ceykiṉṟatu eṉpāḷ, nakārō perumaniṟ kaṇṭiciṉōr eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. ciṟuvar ceyal iḷamipaṟṟiyum icceyal eḷḷal paṟṟiyum nakikkap paṭutaliṉ, nakārō eṉa etirmaṟi vāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟiṉāḷ.
ivvāṟu talivaṉpāl tōṉṟiya ceyalpaṟṟik kaḻaṟik kūṟiṉāḷāyiṉum, talivi avaṉatu kūṭṭam paṟṟip piṟanta vēṭki mikutiyāl mayaṅkik kūṟutaliṉ, amivatāyiṟṟu; “tāypōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻīik kōṭal, āymaṉik kiḻattikkum uritteṉa moḻipa, kavavoṭu mayaṅkiya kāliyāṉa ”1 eṉa āciriyar kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. ciṟuvariṉ iṉiya ceyti eṉṟatu, aḻivil kūṭṭattu avaṉ pirivāṟṟāmi - nakārō perumaniṟ kaṇṭiciṉōr eṉṟatu puṟañcol māṇākkiḷavi. ēṉi meyppāṭu: perumitam. payaṉ : vāyil nērtal.
maṟivil yāṇar malikē ḻūraniṉ ciṟuvariṉ iṉiya ceyti eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu. taṭuttaṟ kiyalāta putu varuvāyiṉi yuṭiya ūraṉē, niṉ ciṟuvarip pōla iṉivaṉa ceyti eṉpatu poruḷām. eṉ ciṟuvaṉi nōkki, “empōlak kiyāṟuṭiyavar illallāṟ cellal” eṉac ciṟittavaḻiyum, avaṉ, ataṉik kinnīvik, “kuṉṟa viṟuvarik kōṇmā ivarntāṅkut tanti viyaṉmārpiṟ” pāyntu eṉpāl nillā toḻukiyatupōla, nīyum, puṟattoḻukutal vēṇṭā eṉac cūḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ ciṟippiṉum, ciṟippaṭātu oḻukukiṉṟaṉi eṉṟu talivi kūṟiyavāṟām. ceytiyi eṉṟa pāṭam ceyti kaḷi yuṭiyāy eṉṟavāṟu.
	86.	veṇṭalik kurukiṉ meṉpaṟi viḷikkural
nīḷvaya ṉaṇṇi yimiḻu mūra
emmiva ṇalkuta laritu
nummaṉi maṭantiyoṭu talippey tīmē.
“putalvaṉ kūṟiya māṟṟam talimakaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kūṟak kēṭṭāṉ” eṉpatu aṟinta paratti ataṟkup pulantu talimakaṟkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
kurukiṉ pārppu aḻikkum kural vayal naṇṇi imiḻum ūra eṉṟatu, niṉ putalvaṉ kūṟiviṭuttal nī kēṭṭami1 cēri yellām aṟintatu, niṉṉāl maṟittal aritu eṉpatām. pārppiṉam mellitākap paṟattalpaṟṟi meṉpaṟi eṉṟu āku peyarāṟ kūṟiyateṉak koḷka.
uri :
veḷḷiya taliyiṉiyuṭiya kurukiṉattiṉ pārppuk kaḷatu aḻippiṭukural nīṇṭa vayalkaḷi aṭintu olikkum ūraṉē, iṉi nī emmi aruḷutal muṭiyātāki yāl niṉ maṉiyi aṭintu aṅku niṉ maṉiyāṭṭi yuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpuṟuka eṉṟavāṟu.
veṇṭalik kuruku, kampuṭ kōḻiyumām. paṟi - ciṟaku. “veṇpaṟi nāri”2 eṉa varutal kāṇka. īṇṭu, iḵtu aṭiyaṭutta ākupeyarāyp pārppiṉattiṉ mēlatāyiṟṟu. ivaṇ, parattimaṉi. taṉ āyatti uḷappaṭutti em eṉṟum, taliviyiyum putalvaṉiyum uḷappaṭutti num eṉṟum urittāḷ. maṭanti, makappēṟṟukkuriya paruvattiyuṭiya peṇ. talippeytal, orucol : talikkūṭal, talip pirital eṉṟāṟ pōla.
talivaṉ iṉit taṉ maṉiyakam pōntu taṉṉi aruḷu vatu aritu eṉap paratti moḻintataṟkuriya ētuvi uḷ ḷuṟuttu urittamiyiṉ, vāḷā nalkutal aritu eṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭatu. putalvaṉpāl avaṉ meytīṇṭi uṭaṟkiṉpamum, coṟkēṭṭuc cevikkiṉpamum, taḷarnaṭi kaṇṭu kaṭkiṉpamum iṭiyaṟātu peṟutaliṉ taṉṉi nayantu varutal kūṭā teṉpāḷ, paratti em ivaṇ nalkutal aritu eṉṟāḷ. nalkutal eṉavē, tāṉ avaṉi iṉṟiyamiyāmi peṟavit tāḷām. maṉikkuriyaḷāya talimakaḷ putalvaṉip payantu polikiṉṟāḷ eṉpatupaṭa num maṉi maṭanti yoṭu eṉṟum, maṉi aṭintavaḻi, peṟutaṟkariya putalvaṟpēṟṟāl niṉṉiyum niṉ kuṭiyiyum viḷakkamuṟuttum taliviyip putalvaṉtāy eṉap pulli makiḻka eṉpāḷ talippeytīmē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. itaṉāṟ parattipāl poṟāmi tōṉ ṟiṟṟu. maṭanti yeṉṟatu, putalvaṟ pēṟṟāl mutumi yuṭi yāḷ eṉa ōrāṟṟāl taliviyi ikaḻntavāṟumām.
kurukiṉ meṉpaṟi ceyta viḷikkural nīḷvayal naṇṇi imiḻkiṉṟa teṉavē, niṉ putalvaṉ colli viṭṭa māṟṟam ic cēriyiluḷḷār yāvarum aṟiyappaṭṭatu eṉṟāḷām; ākavē, ap putalvaṉi uḷḷi nī niṉ vaḷamaṉi cellutal orutali eṉpatām. vayal naṇṇi imiḻkiṉṟa teṉṟataṉāl, ataṉi illi yeṉa maṟittalum niṉakkuk kūṭātu eṉṟa vāṟām. meyppāṭu : vekuḷi. payaṉ : vāyil maṟuttal.
itu, “pullutaṉ mayakkum”1 eṉṟu cūttirattu, “illōr ceyviṉi ikaḻccik kaṇṇum” eṉpuḻi nikaḻum parattiyar kūṟṟuvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkum. illōr eṉṟa paṉmiyāl, vaṉpuṟi vāyilākiya putalvaṉum taliviyum koṇṭu putalvaṉ kūṟṟit taṉ pulavikku ētuvākkiyum, maṭantiyeṉat taliviyi ikaḻntum kūṟiṉami koḷka.
emmivaṇ nalkutal aṟitum eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu. makaṉ viṭu māṟṟam kēṭṭoḻukum niṉakku avaṉpālum avaṉ tāypālum aṉpu celvataṉṟi, empāl cellāmi iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa teṉpatupaṭa aṟitum eṉṟāḷ eṉka. eṉavē, nalkutal aṟitum  eṉpatu kuṟippu moḻiyām.
	87.	pakaṉṟik kaṇṇip pallāṉ kōvalar
karumpu kuṇilā māṅkaṉi yutirkkum
yāṇa rūraniṉ maṉiyōḷ
yāriyum pulakku memmimaṟ ṟevaṉō.
talimakaḷ taṉṉip puṟaṅkūṟiṉāḷ eṉak kēṭṭa kātaṟ paratti, avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpat talimakaṉōṭu pulantu colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
karumpu kuṇilā māṅkaṉi utirkkum ūra eṉṟatu, yāṅkaḷ paḻittēmeṉṟu avaṭku iṉiyacoṟ kūṟi avaḷ eṅkaḷip paḻittuk kūṟum coṟkaḷi niṉakku iṉiyavākap peṟuvāy eṉṟavāṟu.
uri :
pakaṉṟip pūvāl toṭukkappaṭṭa kaṇṇiyic cūṭiya pala āṉirikaḷiyuṭiya āyarkaḷ karumpik kuṟuntaṭi yākak koṇṭu māviṉ kaṉikaḷi utirkkum putu varuvā yiṉiyuṭiya ūraṉē, niṉ maṉivi yāvariyum pulantu kūṟum iyalpiṉaḷākaliṉ emmip pulattaṟku eṉṉi?
pakaṉṟi, civatippūṇṭu. kilukiluppic ceṭiyaṉṟu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum1 peruṅkiyāl eṉa aṭiyārkku nallārum2 kūṟuvar. itu nīṇṭa iliyum veṇmiyāṉa pūvum uṭiyatu. itaṉ pū vaṭivil vaḷḷam pōṉṟiruttaliṉ, veḷḷiya pāṇṭili itaṟku uvamam kūṟuvar, ip pūviṟku maṇam kiṭiyātu. itu maruta nilattiṟ kuriyatu. “veḷḷitaḻp pakaṉmati yuruviṟ pakaṉṟi māmalar”3 “pāṇṭil oppiṟ pakaṉṟi malarum”4 “pakaṉṟi vāṉmalar paṉiniṟintatupōl, pālpey vaḷḷam cālki paṟṟi”5 “pērilip pakaṉṟip potiyaviḻ vāṉpū, iṉkaṭuṅ kaḷḷiṉ maṇamila kamaḻum”6 eṉpaṉa mutalākac cāṉṟōr kūṟumāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka. maṇamila tāyiṉum itaṉāl kaṇṇiyum māliyum toṭuttu aṇital paṇṭiyōr marapu. “pakaṉṟik kaṇṇip paḻiyar makaḷir”1 eṉṟum, “cillē rāḷar paṉittuṟip pakaṉṟip pāṅkuṭit teriyal...... cūṭi”2 eṉṟum varuvaṉa kāṇka. kuṇil - kuṟuntaṭi; “kaṉṟu kuṇilāk kaṉiyukutta māyavaṉ ”3 eṉapuḻippōla. iḵtu akattuṟip pāṭṭākaliṉ, itaṉkaṇ mulli nilattuk kuriya kōvalar ceyti kūṟappaṭṭatu; “avvam makkaḷum vilaṅku maṉṟip, piṟavavaṇ variṉum tiṟavatiṉ nāṭit, tattam iyaliṉ marapōṭu muṭiyiṉ, antiṟan tāṉē tuṟiyeṉap paṭumē”4 eṉavarum tolkāppiyattiṟkup pērāciriyar kūṟum uri kāṇka.
pulattaliṉṟi maṅkalavakiyāl talivi etirēṟṟuk kōṭaṟ kuriya “piṉmuṟi yākkiya perum poruḷ vatuvi”5 makaḷiri yum avaḷ pulakkum pāṉmiyaḷ eṉpāḷ, niṉmaṉiyōḷ yāriyum pulakkum eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, avaḷ emmip pulattal collāmalē viḷaṅkum eṉṟaṟkup paratti  emmimaṟ ṟevaṉō eṉṟāḷ.
karumpiṉ tuṇṭattik kuṟuntaṭiyākak koṇṭu kōvalar māviṉ tīṅkaṉiyi utirppar eṉṟatu, yām kūṟum coṟ pakutikaḷit taliviyip paḻittuk kūṟiyaṉavāka avaṭkuk kūṟi avaḷatu pulavi tīrttuk kūṭṭam peṟṟu makiḻvāy eṉṟavāṟām. pulavi tīrkkumiṭattu, talivikkut talimakaṉ iṉcoṟ kūṟalum avaḷ parattiyip paḻittuk kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi ēṟṟu appulavi tīrat takuvaṉa ceytalum uṇmiyiṉ, “yāṅkaḷ paḻittēmeṉṟu avaṭku iṉiya coṟkaḷik kūṟi avaḷ eṅkaḷip paḻittuk kūṟum coṟkaḷi niṉakku iṉiyavākap peṟuvāy” eṉap paḻiyavuri kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu. meyppāṭum payaṉu mavi.
itu “pullutal mayakkum”6 eṉṉum cūttirattu “illōr ceyviṉi ikaḻccikkaṇ” paratti nikaḻttum kūṟṟu vaki yākum.
	88.	vaṇṭuṟi nayavarum vaḷamalarp poykit
taṇṭuṟi yūraṉi yevvi yemvayiṉ
varutal vēṇṭutu meṉpa
tollēm pōlyā matuvēṇ ṭutumē.
talimakaṉi nayappittuk koḷkiyil viruppillātāḷ pōla avvāṟu kōṭaliyē virumpuvāḷ atu taṉakku muṭiyā teṉat talimakaḷ puṟaṉurittā ḷeṉak kēṭṭa paratti avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉārkkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
ivvāṟu eṉṉik kūṟuki tavirāḷāyiṉ, ataṉi muṭiyac ceytu viṭukiṟēṉ eṉpatām. vaḷḷiya tuṟikaḷiṉkaṇṇē ellārum koḷḷum vaṇṇam nayantu pūkkiṉṟa malarkaḷi yuṭiya poykiyūraṉ eṉṟatu, makaḷirellārkkum potup paṭṭiruppāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
uri :
vaṇṭukaḷ uṟiyum yāvarum virumpukiṉṟa vaḷaviya malarppoykiyiṉ taṇṇiya tuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya ūraṉi yām emmiṭamē varutali vēṇṭukiṉṟēm eṉṟu evvi colluvataṉip puṟattē virumpātēm pōlak kāṭṭi akattē ataṉi virumpi oḻukukiṉṟēm eṉṟavāṟu.
vaṇṭukaḷ - vaṇṭiṉam; caṅkumām. vaṇṭuṟi vaḷamalarp poyki, nayavarum vaḷamalarp poyki eṉa iyiyum. vaṇṭuṟi eṉpataṟku vaḷḷiya tuṟiyeṉavum, nayavarum vaḷamalar eṉpataṟku ellārum nayantu koḷḷum vaṇṇam pūkkiṉṟa vaḷaviya malar eṉṟum uḷḷuṟutta karuttukkēṟpap paḻiyavurikārar kūṟukiṉṟār. evvi - em taṅki; num taṅki - nuvvi yeṉavum, tam taṅki - tavvi yeṉavum varutalpōla; “nummiṉum ciṟantatu nuvvi yākumeṉṟu, aṉṉi kūṟiṉaḷ”1 eṉavum, “niṉ tavviya rāvōr tāriyum vīriyum”2 eṉavum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkumāṟu kāṇka. “evvik kevaṉperi taḷikkum”1 eṉpataṟkum ituvē kūṟikkoḷka. ollēm pōl eṉṟatu uḷḷum puṟamum ovvāmi uṇara niṉṟatu; “melliya iṉiya kūṟaliṉ yāṉaḵtu ollēṉ pōla uriyāṭu valē”2 eṉap piṟa cāṉṟōrum ip poruṇmi tōṉṟa urikkumā ṟaṟika. tollēm pōl eṉṟu koḷvāru muḷar. pirinili ēkāram vikārattāl tokkatu. piṟavellām eccavakiyāṟ peyturikkappaṭṭaṉa.
talivaṉ taṉpālē varutal vēṇṭumeṉap paratti mika vēṇṭi niṉṟāḷāyiṉum, talivi kūṟṟi uṭaṉ paṭātāḷ pōla oḻukutaṟku uriya ētuviṉi uḷḷuṟikkaṇ kūṟi viṭṭamiyiṉ, evvi emvayiṉ varutal vēṇṭutum eṉpatu ollēm eṉa vāḷātu moḻintāḷ. karuttu vakiyāl uṭaṉpāṭuṇmiyiṉ taliviyi evvi yeṉṟum, av vuṭaṉpaṭāmiyum piṟarkkut tōṟṟuvittaṟkē eṉpatu eyta ollēm pōl eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷāyiṟṟu.
talivi kūṟṟit tāṉ uṭaṉpaṭātāḷpōla oḻukuta lāvatu, talivaṉ yāvarmāṭṭum puṇarvu pirivukaḷāl iṉpa mum iṭumpiyum nikaḻttutaliṉ, talivi emmip pulattal kūṭātu; avaḷ koḻunaṉē avaṭku varutta meytuvik kiṉṟāṉ eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa kūṟutal. “vāṉam vēṇṭā vaṟaṉil vāḻkki”3 eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉuḷ, “ūraṉōṭu eḻunta kuviyō peritē, naṭpē, koḻuṅkōl vēḻattup puṇituṇi yākap, puṉalāṭu kēṇmi aṉittē” eṉavum, “avaṉē...... taṇṇaṟuñ cāntaṅ kamaḻum tōḷmaṇantu, iṉṉum piṟaḷva yiṉāṉē” eṉavum, ivaṟṟi aṟiyātu “maṉiyōḷ, emmoṭu pulakkum eṉpa” eṉavum, “uritiṉiṉ, yāmtaṉ pakiyē mallēm cērntōr. tirunutal pacappa nīṅkum, koḻunaṉē cālumtaṉ uṭaṉuṟi pakiyē” eṉavum paratti talivaṉatu naṭpip paḻittu avaṉ taṉpālē varutali nayavātāḷ pōla avaṉip paki eṉak kūṟiyatu pōlvaṉa eṉak koḷka.
ippōḻtu, puṟattē uṭaṉpāṭiṉmiyum akattē uṭaṉpāṭum koṇṭu oḻukat tuṇinta paratti, talivi kūṟiyatu taṉ māṭṭāmi kūṟit taṉṉi avaḷ paḻippatu pōṟaliṉ yām atu vēṇṭutumē eṉṟāḷ. ceykutum eṉṉātu, vēṇṭutum eṉṟataṉāl, “avaḷ (talivi) iv vāṟu kūṟuvatit tavirāḷāyiṉ, yāṉ palarum aṟiyat talivaṉ empālē varat takuvaṉavaṟṟic ceytuviṭat tuṇintēṉ” eṉ ṟāḷām. aḵtāvatu, yāṉ talivaṉik koṇṭu kivalip pal eṉavum, avaṉ eṉvayiṉ varumāṟu ceyyēṉāyiṉ iṉṉaḷāvēṉ eṉavum piṟavum kūṟit tarukkutal. “maṇkaṉi muḻavoṭu makiḻmikat tūṅkat, taṇṭuṟi yūraṉem cēri vanteṉa......... āṉātu kaḻaṟupa eṉpa avaṉpeṇṭir.......... ciṟipaṟintu uriic ceṅkuṇak koḻukum, antaṇ kāviri pōlak, koṇṭu kivalittal cūḻnticiṉ yāṉē” eṉavum, “tuṟikē ḻūraṉōṭu āvatāka, iṉi nāṇ uṇṭō, varukatil amma em cēricēra, varivēy uṇkaṇavaṉ peṇṭir kāṇat, tārum tāṉiyum paṟṟi...... tōḷkan tākak kūntaliṟ piṇittuavaṉ mārpu, kaṭikoḷḷē ṉāyiṉ ārvuṟṟu. irantōrkku īyātu īṭṭiyōṉ poruḷ pōl, parantu veḷippaṭā tāki, varuntuka tillayāy ōmpiya  nalaṉē”1 eṉavum varuvaṉa parattiyiṉ tuṇipuri vakiyiṉi uṇarttu vaṉavām.
ellārum nayantu koḷḷum vaṇṇam pūkkiṉṟa malarkaḷi yuṭiya poykiyūraṉ eṉṟatu, taliviyum yāmumākiya makaḷir palarum virumpi nukarum vaṇṇam nayantu pōntu iṉpam nalkuvāṉ eṉat talivaṉatu potuppaṇpiṉi uṇarttiyavāṟu. ākavē, talivaṉ makaḷirpalarkkum potup paṭa oḻukukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟami peṟappaṭum. ivvāṟu kūṟi yoḻukiya taṉṉit talivi puṟaṉurittāḷ eṉappoṟātu paratti neṭumoḻi nikaḻttum uḷḷattaḷāyt taliviyiṉ pāṅkāyiṉārkkuc collukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ emvayiṉ eṉṟum yām eṉṟum paṉmi vāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟiṉāḷ. itu “pullutal mayakkum”1 eṉṟa cūttirattu, “eṇṇiya paṇṇiyeṉ ṟivaṟ ṟoṭu piṟavum” eṉpuḻip “piṟavum” eṉpataṉāl koḷḷap
paṭum. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ : taliviyi ikaḻtal.
vaḷarmalar eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu; maṟṟu, vaḷartaṟ ṟoḻil malarukkātal, poykikkātal iyiyāmiyiṉ atu pāṭamaṉmi yaṟika.
	89.	amma vāḻi pāṇa vevvik kevaṉperi taḷikku meṉpa paḻaṉattu
vaṇṭutā tūtu mūraṉ
peṇṭeṉa virumpiṉ ṟavaṭaṉ paṇpē.
talimakaṉ talimakaḷippaṟṟi oḻukāniṉṟāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭa kātaṟparatti avaṉ pāṇaṉukkuc colluvāḷāy avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
avaṉ āṅku oḻukukiṉṟatu, avaḷ paṇpuṭimiyallatu vēṟu kāraṇam illi eṉa ikaḻntu kūṟiyavāṟām.
uri :
pāṇa, itaṉik kēḷ: paḻaṉaṅkaḷil malarntuḷḷa pūkkaḷil vaṇṭu tēṉiyuṇṇum ūraṉ em taṅkiyākiya talimakaḷip peritum taliyaḷit toḻukukiṉṟā ṉeṉa avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār collukiṉṟa teṉṉi? avaḷit taṉakkup peṇṭeṉak karuti avaṉ virumpi yoḻukuvatu avaḷ taṉ maṉimakaḷām paṇpuṭimiyē yoḻiya vēṟilli; itaṉi avarkaḷ aṟintilar pōlum eṉṟavāṟu.
peritaḷikkum eṉpatu pāṅkāyiṉār kūṟṟik koṇṭu kūṟiyatu. evaṉ eṉpatu “aṟiyāp poruḷvayiṟ ceṟiyat tōṉṟum” viṉāviṉ kiḷavi; īṇṭu, iḵtu avarkaḷ kūṟṟi maṟukkum kuṟippuṇara niṉṟatu. kūṟuvōr avarākaliṉ, avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār eṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu; ēṉiya ecca vakiyāṟ peṟappaṭṭaṉa. virumpiṉṟu, kālaṅ kāṭṭum toḻiṟpeyar. kāraṇam kāriyamāka upacarikkappaṭṭatu. piṟa nalaṅkaḷi maṟuttup paṇpiṉiyē vaṟpuṟuttutaliṉ ēkāram tēṟṟam. vāḻi : acinili.
talivaṉ parattiyarkkut taliyaḷi ceytoḻukutali, uḷḷuṟiyāṟ kuṟikkiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ ataṉik koḷḷātu uritta taliviyiṉ pāṅkāyiṉār kūṟṟi maṟuttaṟku, evvikku evaṉ peritu aḷikkum eṉpa eṉṟum, avaṉatu oḻukkattiṉkaṇ parattipāṟ ceyyappaṭum taliyaḷiyiṉum talivipāṟ ceyyappaṭuvatu ciṟitu peritāyt tōṉṟiṉ, ataṟkuk kāraṇam iḵteṉpāḷ ūraṉ peṇṭeṉa virum piṉṟu avaḷtaṉ paṇpē eṉṟum coṉṉāḷ. parattimi tōṉṟiṉum, atupaṟṟi avaṉi veṟātu puriyaṟam teḷintu avaṉpāl aṉpu toka niṟṟal īṇṭup paṇpāvatu eṉa aṟika; “takavuṭi maṅkiyar cāṉṟāṇmi cāṉṟār, ikaḻiṉum kēḷvari yētti yiṟiñcuvar”1 eṉṟum, “māya makiḻnaṉ parattimi, nōvēṉ tōḻi kaṭaṉnamak keṉavē”2  eṉṟum cāṉṟōr urikkumāṟu kāṇka.
potuvākiya paḻaṉaṅkaḷiṟ pūttirukkum malariluḷḷa tēṉi vaṇṭu uṇṭu makiḻum eṉṟataṉāl, taliviṉ potumakaḷirāṉa parattiyiṟ pirintu iṉpa nukartalip pulappaṭuttā ḷāyiṟṟu. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ : pāṅkāyiri maṟuttal.
vēṇṭeṉa virumpiṉṟu eṉṟa pāṭattukkut talivaṉ māṟātu vēṇṭuka eṉa vāyilviṭuttu virumpi oḻukuvatu eṉa urikka. avaṉ parattimiyāṟ pulantu vēṟupaṭṭāḷi atu nīkki ēṟṟukkoḷḷumāṟu vēṇṭutalāṟ kāmam ciṟattaliṉ, atu ceyvatum takumeṉa vaṟika. “alantāri allalnōy ceytaṟṟāl tammip, pulantārip pullā viṭal”3 eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvap peruntakiyār aṟivuṟuttumāṟu kāṇka.
	90.	makiḻnaṉ māṇkuṇam vaṇṭukoṇ ṭaṉakol
vaṇṭiṉ māṇkuṇam makiḻnaṉkoṇ ṭāṉkol
aṉṉa tākalu maṟiyāḷ
emmoṭu pulakkumavaṉ putalvaṉ ṟāyē.
talimakaṉ taṉ maṉikkaṭ cellāmal tāṉ vilakku kiṉṟā ḷākat talimakaḷ kūṟiṉāḷ eṉpatu kēṭṭa kātaṟ paratti, talimakaṉ kēṭkumāṟṟāl pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.
uri :
makiḻnaṉuṭiya māṇpuṟṟa kuṇaṅkaḷi vaṇṭukaḷ aṭintaṉavō? avaṟṟiṉ māṇ kuṇaṅkaḷi avaṉ aṭintaṉaṉō? ceyaloṟṟumiyāl vēṟupaṭuttu aṟiyakkūṭāmiyiṉ, acceyal avaṟku iyalpākalum ataṉāl avaṉi vilakkuta lākāmiyum aṟiyāḷāy avaṉ putalvaṟkut tāyākiya talivi yām avaṉit taṉ maṉikku ēkāvaṇṇam vilakkukiṉṟēm eṉa emmōṭu pulakkiṉṟāḷ. iḵtōr aṟiyāmi iruntavāṟu eṉṉē eṉṟavāṟu.
makiḻnaṉ māṇkuṇam, nalam putiyarāya makaḷiriyē kūṭiyoḻukutal. vaṇṭiṉ māṇkuṇam, ellāppūviṉum ceṉṟu tēṉuṇṇutal. ēṟpuṭiya coṟkaḷ ecca vakiyāl varuvittuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. acceyal paṟṟi avaṉip piṟar vilakkalākāmiyum eytuvittaliṉ ākalum eṉpuḻi ummi eccap poruḷatām. aṟiyāḷ: muṟṟeccam pulakkum eṉṉum viṉimutal viṉi koṇṭatu.
nalam putiyaḷāyc ciṟakkum taṉpāl piriviṉṟik kūṭi iruntu iṉpanukarcci eytum talivaṉceyal tēṉuṭip putumalarit tērntu tēṉ uṇṇum vaṇṭiṉ ceyali nikarttaliṉ, makiḻnaṉ māṇkuṇam vaṇṭukoṇṭaṉa kol eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, paratti taṉ nalattik kuṟippāṟ ciṟappittuk kūṟiṉāḷāyiṟṟu. tātuṇṇum vēṭkiyāl putuppū eṉṟātal paḻampū eṉṟātal nōkkātu ellāp pūviṉum ceṉṟu vaṇṭiṉam ūtutalpōla, nalam putiyaḷāya eṉṉik kūṭalēyaṉṟi mutiyaḷāya taliviyiyum kūṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉpāḷ, vaṇṭiṉ māṇkuṇam makiḻnaṉ koṇṭāṉkol eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, taṉ iḷamiyum taliviyiṉ mutumiyum kūṟiṉāḷāyiṟṟu. vaṇṭiṉam ellāp pūviṉum ūtum eṉpa taṉi, “tātuṇ vēṭkiyiṟ pōtuterin tūtā, vaṇṭō raṉṉaavaṉ taṇṭāk kāṭci”1 eṉa varumāṟṟāl uṇarka.
putunalam keḻumiya makaḷiriyē nāṭi iṉpa nukarcci eytum talivaṉ kuṇam ellāp pūviṉum oppa ūtum vaṇṭiṟkiṉmi yiṉ, vaṇṭukoṇ ṭaṉakol eṉṟum, vaṇṭiṉampōlat talivaṉ iḷiyariyum mutiyariyum oppak kūṭutaliṉ aḵtu avaṟku iyalpaṉṟu eṉpatu paṭa, makiḻnaṉ koṇṭāṉ kol eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ; “pukaṉṟa uḷḷamoṭu putuvōr cāyaṟku, akaṉṟa kiḻavaṉ ”2  eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiya ṉārum, “ūraṉ putuvōr mēvalaṉ”3 eṉa in nūlāciriyarum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, talivaṉ putunalamakaḷiriyē nāṭumāṟu aṟika. “avaṉē, neṭunīrp poyki naṭunāḷ eytit, taṇkamaḻ putumalar ūtum, vaṇṭeṉa moḻipa makaṉeṉ ṉārē”4 eṉavarum āciriyar marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār pāṭṭum ik karut tukkaṭkut tuṇiyātal kāṇka.
ik kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, putunalamuṭiya makaḷirik kūṭi neṭitu iruttal talivaṟku iyalpākaliṉ ataṉit talivi aṟintilaḷ eṉpāḷ, aṉṉatākalum aṟiyāḷ eṉṟāḷ. iḷamipparuvam kaḻiyuṅkāṟum ac ceyal avaṟku vilakkap paṭāmiyum aṟintilaḷ eṉpāḷ, ataṉi ummiyāl eytuvittāḷ.
“piṉmuṟi yākkiya”5 eṉṟa cūttirattuk “kiḻavōṉ iṟantatu niṉii āṅkaṇ, kalaṅkalum uriyaṉ eṉmaṉār pulavar” eṉṉum pakutiyil, kalaṅkalum eṉpuḻi ummi “etir maṟiyākalāṉ (parattimi nīṅki) mīḷāmiyum urittāyiṟṟu; eṉṉi? iḷamipparuvam kaḻiyāta kālattu akkātal mīḷā tākaliṉ” eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urippatu kāṇka.
makiḻnaṉ māṇkuṇam, vaṇṭiṉ māṇkuṇam eṉpuḻi māṇpukaḷ muṟiyē pukaḻcciyum, ikaḻcciyum kuṟittu niṉṟaṉa.
talivi, makiḻnaṉ kuṇacciṟappum vilakkalākāmiyum nōkkātu avaṉi yām vilakkukiṉṟēm eṉa emmoṭu vaṟitē pulakkiṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ, emmoṭu pulakkum avaṉ putalvaṉ tāyē eṉṟāḷ. talimakaṟkuriya talivi paratti mutaliya peṇṭirip putalvaṉtāy eṉap potuvākak kuṟittal marapāyiṉum, īṇṭu makappayantu mēṉi mutirntu nalam mutiyaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉpāḷ taliviyip putalvaṉtāy eṉṟāḷ. “putalvaṉtā yeṉa....... em mutumi eḷḷal”1 eṉap paraṇar, putalvaṉ tāy eṉpatu pulattaṟkuriya colleṉa viḷakkiyavāṟu aṟika. “niṉmakaṉ tāyātal purivatu”2 eṉat taliviyi makaṉtāy eṉak kuṟikkumāṟu kāṇka. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ : taliviyi ikaḻtal.
10. erumippattu
itaṉkaṇ varum pāṭṭukkaḷ pattiṉum erumiyākiya karupporuḷē ciṟanteṭuttu moḻiyappaṭutaliṉ iḵtu ip peyariṉip peṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
erumiyiṉam ulakeṅkum paraviyirukkiṉṟatu; eṉiṉum, nāṭutōṟum avvan nilattut taṭpaveṭpa nilikaṭ kēṟpa avaṟṟiṉ uṭalamippum kōṭukaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyum amintiruk kiṉṟaṉa. namatu innilavulakil ciṟantu viḷaṅkum nakaraṅkaḷ yāvum paṇṭik kālattil ivverumi yiṉaṅkaḷiṉ uḻippuc ciṟappāl vaḷam peṟṟaṉa eṉpar. piṟa tuṇik karuvikaḷi ākkikkoḷḷum aṟivunalam makkaḷiṭit tōṉṟutaṟkumuṉ ūrtikaḷākavum uḻavut tuṇiyākavum utaviya paḻamperumi erumiyatē.
airōppāvil ippoḻutu vāḻum erumiyiṉam palavum pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉpē āciya nāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟa vaṟṟiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ eṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. mēṉāṭukaḷil erumiyiṉamum kutiriyiṉamum tōṉṟi mak kaṭku vēṇṭiya utaviyiṉip puriyat toṭaṅkutaṟku muṉ āṉēṟum āṉiriyumē ataṉi āṟṟi vantaṉa. piṉṉarē erumiyiṉam ceṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ niliyil niṉṟu uḻutal vaṇṭiyiḻuttal cumikoṇṭu cēṟal mutaliya ceyalkaḷic cevvē ceytu pōntaṉa.
uyirnūl aṟiñar, ivverumiyiṉam palavum paṇṭi yil nam nāṭṭiṟṟāṉ tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. amerikka nāṭṭiṟ kāṇum erumiyiṉam (Bison) uṇmi erumi yiṉamākā. āyiṉum avi uḻavu mutaliyavaṟṟic ceytalum, pāl veṇṇey iṟicci tōl mutaliyavaṟṟi utavutalum ceykiṉṟaṉa.
erumiyiṉam uṭaluḻippāl kaḷippu mikka vaḻic cēṟṟu nīriṟ paṭintu kiṭattal iyalpu. itaṉi aṟintē nam nāṭṭup paṇṭic cāṉṟōr, “taṭamarup perumip piṇarccuval irumpōttu, maṭanaṭi nārip palliṉam iriya, neṭunīrt taṇkayam tuṭumeṉap pāyntu, nāṭṭoḻil varuttam vīṭac cēṭciṉi, iruḷpuṉi marutiṉ iṉṉiḻal vatiyum ”1 eṉavum, “valimiku muṉpiṉ aṇṇal ēeṟu, paṉimalarp poykip pakalcela maṟuki, maṭakkaṇ erumi māṇnāku taḻīip, paṭappi naṇṇip paḻaṉattu alkum”2 eṉavum kūṟuvārāyiṉar. veyil avir veppam mikka nam nāṭṭil erumikkuc cēṟṟiṟ paṭiyum ivviyalpu ottatāyiṉum, kuḷirmikka mēṉāṭukaḷil itu vēṇṭāvaṉṟē! annāṭṭiṉum erumikaḷ itu cey talik kāṇpōr, iḵtu ivaṟṟiṉ mutaṉmi iṉattiṉ paḻiya iyaṟkit toṭarpu eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar.
āciya nāṭṭil, intiyā parmā malēyā mutaliya pakuti kaḷil vāḻum erumiyiṉam orē iyalpum paṇpum uṭiyaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. āppirikka nāṭṭuk kāṅkō erumiyum, cutaṉa 3 nāṭṭu erumiyum, mēli āppirikka erumiyum niṟattil vēṟupaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuṟ kāṅkō cemmi niṟamum, cutaṉam cāṇa niṟamum1 mēliyāppirikka nāṭṭavi mikka cemmiyum piṟavum kalanta niṟamum peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. nam nāṭ ṭavi karuntāḷum ceṅkaṇṇum valiya kōṭum meyvaṉmi yum peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi nam tamiḻāciriyaṉmār ciṟappuṟa vitantu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. “karuntāḷ erumi” eṉa innūluḷḷum, “kaḻunīr mēynta karuntāḷ erumi ”2 eṉa naṟṟiṇiyiṉum kūṟappaṭum. “neṟimarup perumi nīla virumpōttu,” “karuṅ kōṭṭu erumic ceṅkaṇ puṉiṟṟā,” “cēṟṟunili muṉiiya ceṅkaṇ kārāṉ” eṉavum ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum mēṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟi valiyuṟuttuvaṉavākum.
erumiyiṉattuḷ makkaṭ payiṟciyil vārāta kāṭ ṭerumi kaḷ, payiṟciyil vantuḷḷa ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉum mikka vaṉmi yuṭiyaṉavām. payiṟcipeṟṟa erumik kōṭukaḷiyē `irumpi yaṉ ṟaṉṉa karuṅkōṭ ṭerumi,’ `maḷḷa raṉṉa taṭaṅkōṭ ṭerumi’ eṉac ciṟappittum, meyvaṉmiyi, `karuṅkōṭ ṭerumi kayiṟuparintu acii,’ `ceṅkaṇ kārāṉ, ūrmaṭi kaṅkuliṉ nōṉṟaḷi parintu, kūrmuḷ vēli kōṭṭiṉ nīkki’ eṉa eṭuttu moḻipaveṉiṉ, kāṭṭerumikaḷiṉ vaṉmi mikuti collavēṇṭā vāyiṟṟu. kāṭṭerumik kaṭā oṉṟu mūṉṟu arimākkaḷōṭu pōr ceytatākak kaṇṭōr kūṟi yirukkiṉṟaṉar. vilaṅku vēṭṭiyiṟ kitērntavarkaḷ, arimāviṉ āṟṟaliṉum vekuṇṭu pāyum erumikkaṭāviṉ āṟṟalē peritum añcat takuvateṉak kaṭṭurikkiṉṟaṉar. ceṉṟa cila āṇṭukaṭku muṉ tilli nakarattiṉ (Delhi) arukil nikaḻnta pukivaṇṭi vipattiṟku ōr erumiyē kāraṇam eṉak kūṟappaṭṭatu.
paḻaṅkālattil erumikaḷ iravuppōtil iri mēy talum, pakaṟpōtil ōyntiruppatum ceytaṉa; av virāk kālaṅkaḷil, arimākkaḷ ulavat toṭaṅkiyatum ivi avviyalpi māṟṟip pakaṟpōtil mēyvatum iravil ōyvukōṭalum ceyvaṉavāyiṉa. paṇṭip payiṟci vātaṉi yāṟṟāṉ, ippōtum cila erumikaḷ irākkālaṅ kaḷil kayiṟu parintuceṉṟu mēyal ārkiṉṟatum, iravil mēyntu pakaṟ pōtil nīrnilikaḷiṟ paṭintu kiṭattalum ceykiṉṟaṉapōlum. “paṉimalarp poykip pakalcela maṟuki maṭakkaṇ erumi māṇāku taḻīip, paṭappi naṇṇip paḻaṉat talkum” eṉa uvarkkaṇṇūrp pullaṅkīraṉār urippatu kāṇka.
iṉi, erumiyiṉ āṇiṉi, kaṭā, pōttu, ēṟu eṉavum, peṇṇerumiyiṉi, nāku, peṭi eṉavum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvar. “maṭakkaṇ erumi māṇnāku taḻīi”1 eṉavum, “erumip peṭi”2 eṉavum varutal kāṇka. piṟa vellām payiṉṟa vaḻakkiṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ āṇerumi uḻavu, cumi tāṅkic cēṟal mutaliyavaṟṟic ceyyum. peṇṇerumi pālum neyyum tayirum vaḻaṅkum.
erumippālik kōṭaṟporuṭṭu ataṉ kaṉṟiṉi maṉiyakattē tūṇiṟ kaṭṭi vippar. taṉ kaṉṟiṉi aritiṉ nīṅki mēyaṟku ēkum erumi, āṅkut taṉ kātaṟkaṉṟiṉi uḷḷiyuḷḷik kaṉikkum; atupōtu ataṉ maṭi curakkum; pāl tāṉē coriyum. ūrk kuṟumākkaḷ (ciṟuvarkaḷ) viṭiyaṟ kālattē erumiyiṉi mēyttaṟku ōṭṭic celvar. cel vōr, avaṟṟiṉ mutukilēṟi amarntu celvar. itaṉi āciriyar pūtaṉṟēvaṉār eṉpār, “maṉṟa erumi malartalik kārāṉ, iṉṟīm pāṟpayaṉ koṇmār kaṉṟuviṭṭu, ūrkkuṟu mākkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭu kaḻiyum perumpular viṭiyal”3 eṉṟaṉar. mēlē ivarntu cellum ciṟuvarkaḷiyum, avarkaḷic cumantēkum erumikaḷiyum kaṇṭa maturi marutaṉ iḷa nākaṉār eṉṉum nallicippulavar, erumiyit tuṟukal pōlavum ciṟuvarkaḷi ataṉmēl irukkum manti pōlavum uvamittu, “tirimarup perumi, mayirkkaviṉ koṇṭa māt tōl irumpuṟam, ciṟutoḻil makāar ēṟic cēṇōrkkut, tuṟukal mantiyiṉ tōṉṟum ūraṉ”4 eṉa nakiccuvi payappak kūṟiṉar.
uḻavukkum cumi tāṅkaṟkum kaṭākkaḷip payaṉ koḷvōr avaṟṟiṟku vikkōli uṇavākat taruvar. “uḻuta nōṉpakaṭu aḻitiṉ ṟāṅku”1 eṉa varutal kāṇka. aḻi - vikkōl.
“accoṭu tākkip pāruṟṟu iyaṅkiya, paṇṭic cākāṭṭu āḻcci colliya, varimaṇal ñemarak kaṟpaka naṭakkum, perumitap pakaṭṭukkut tuṟiyum uṇṭō”2 eṉa varuvataṉāl erumikkaṭā vaṇṭi īrttaṟkup payaṉpaṭumāṟu aṟiyappaṭum. iṉṟum, nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷil erumikkaṭāk kaṭkuk kaḻuttil maṇikaṭṭi vaṇṭiyiṟ pūṭṭit toḻil ceyvittal kaṇkūṭu. maṇikaṭṭum ic ceyal paṇṭum irunta teṉpatu, “tirimarup perumi iruṇiṟa miyāṉ, varumiṭaṟu yātta pakuvāyt teṇmaṇi, pulampukoḷ yāmattu iyaṅku toṟicikkum”3 eṉavarum kuṟuntokip pāṭṭālum aṟiyap paṭukiṟatu. toḻil muṭintavaḻi ivaṟṟik kaḻaṉikaḷil mēya viṭṭu nīrnilikaḷiṟ kiṭappittal paṇṭum iṉṟum uḷḷa ceyal. meṉpulatti uḻuta uḻavar erumikaḷi vaṉpulattu mēyaviṭuvar eṉap puṟappāṭṭu 4 oṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
	91.	neṟimarup perumi nīla virumpōttu veṟimalarp poyki yāmpaṉ mayakkuṅ
kaḻaṉi yūraṉ makaḷivaḷ
paḻaṉa vetiriṉ koṭippiṇi yalaḷē.
kuṟivēṇṭip piṉṉiṉṟu vanta talimakaṟkut tōḻi, “ivaḷ iḷiyaḷ viḷivilaḷ” eṉac cēṭpaṭuttatu. 5
paḻiya uri
nāṟṟam koḷḷappaṭāta karumpiṉ pūvāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa neṭiya māliyi yuṭiyaḷ eṉpataṉāl pēti eṉṟavāṟu aṟika. erumippōttu veṟimalarp poyki āmpal mayakkum ūr eṉṟatu, nalla taṉmiyi ārāyātu keṭukkum ūrātalāl, niṉakku īṇṭu varutal poruntātu eṉpatām. paḻaṉavetir eṉpatu karumpu.
uri :
muṭaṅkiya kompukaḷiyuṭiya erumiyiṉ kariya periya āṇ maṇamuṟṟa pūkkaḷ niṟinta poykikkaṇ uḷḷa ām palic citikkum kaḻaṉikaḷi yuṭiya ūraṉukku makaḷākiya ivaḷ paḻaṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa karumpiṉ pūvāl toṭukkappaṭṭa nīṇṭa māliyiṉi yuṭiyaḷāvaḷ kāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
neṟi - muṭakkam; “vāṭiya neṟikoḷ varikkuṭar”1 eṉpataṉ urikāṇka. erumiyiṉ maruppu ōṅki nillātu mey mukaṅkaḷiṉ neṟiyē muṭaṅki vaḷartaliṉ neṟimaruppu eṉap paṭṭatu pōlum. nīlam - karumi; “tirimarup perumi iruḷniṟa miyāṉ”2 eṉpa vākaliṉ, nīlam īṇṭuk karumiyāyiṟṟu. “pōttuṅ kaṇṭiyum kaṭuvaṉum piṟavum, yātta āṇpāṟ peyareṉa moḻiya”3 eṉṟataṉāl, erumiyiṉ āṇ pōttu eṉappaṭṭatu. “arimala rāmpal mēynta neṟimaruppu, īrntaṇ erumic cuvalpaṭu mutupōttu”4 eṉap piṟarum kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. vetir eṉṉum viṉi vēṟupaṭāp palaporuḷ orucol, paḻaṉavetir eṉak kiḷakkap paṭṭamiyiṉ, karumpiṉ mēlatāyiṟṟu. “viṉi vēṟu paṭāap palaporu ḷorucol, niṉiyuṅkālik kiḷantāṅ kiyalum”5 eṉpatu tolkāppiyam; īṇṭu, mutaliṟ kūṟum ciṉiyaṟi kiḷaviyāyk karumpiṉ pūvic cuṭṭiṟṟu.
talimakaḷik kuṟinayappittut taṉṉiṉākiya kūṭṭam eytuvikkum tōḻi talivaṉ nīṭṭiyātu teruṇṭu varital vēṇṭi uḷḷuṟiyāl kāppumikutiyum taliviyatu arumi yum uṇartti veḷippaṭiyāl avaḷatu iḷamip paruvattup pētimiyiṉik kāṭṭic cēṭpaṭukkiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ taliviyip paḻaṉa vetiriṉ koṭippiṇiyalaḷ eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, ciṟappillāta karumpiṉ pūvāl piṇiyal toṭukkum ittuṇip pētimiyuṭiyāḷ niṉ kuṟiyip pōkkumāṟu illi eṉṟum, nīyē teruṇṭu nīṭṭiyātu varital vēṇṭum eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷām. “naṟuntaṇ takaram vakuḷam ivaṟṟi, veṟumputalpōl vēṇṭātu vēṇṭi eṟintuḻutu, centiṉi vittuvār taṅki piṟar nōykku, nontiṉiya vallaḷō nōkku”1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
erumip pōttup poykikkaṇ āmpaliṉ nalaṅ karutātu ataṉic citikkum  eṉṟataṉāl, niṉ varavāl viḷiyum naṉmiyi ārāyātu ivvūravar niṉakku ētam ceyvar eṉṟavāṟām. ākavē, itu taliviyatu arumi kuṟikkumukattāṟ kāppumikuti urittu varivu kaṭāvum nōkka muṭittātal aṟika. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ : talivaṉ teruṇṭu varivāṉāvatu:
paḻanal vetiriṉ eṉṟa pāṭattukkup paḻamiyāṉa nalla karumpiṉpū eṉka. eṉiṉum, itu palaporuṭkuriya potumi nīṅkik karumpiṟkē yuriya ciṟappup poruṇmi viḷaṅka nillāmiyiṉ cīriya pāṭam aṉmi yaṟika.
	92.	karuṅkōṭ ṭerumic ceṅkaṭ puṉiṟṟāk kātaṟ kuḻavik kūṟumuli maṭukkum nunti nummūr varutum
oṇṭoṭi maṭanti niṉṉiyām peṟiṉē.
“niṉakku varintutarutaṟkuk kuṟi niṉtamar aṅku vantu kūṟāmiyē,” eṉat tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷākat talimakaḷ mukanōkki ivaḷ kuṟippiṉāṟ kūṟiṉāḷeṉpatu aṟinta talimakaṉ, “varivu māṭcimippaṭiṉ nāṉē varuval” eṉat tali makaṭkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
erumip puṉiṟṟāt taṉ kuḻavikku ūṟumuli maṭukkum eṉṟatu, avaḷ poruṭṭu uṟṟārpakkal tāṉ peṟuvaṉavum kūṟiyavāṟu.
uri :
periya kompiṉiyum civanta kaṇṇiṉiyum īṉṟa aṇimiyiṉiyumuṭiya tāyerumi, taṉ aṉpuṭiya kaṉṟukkuc curakkum mulitantu pālūṭṭum niṉ tantiyatu ūrkku, oḷḷiya vaḷi aṇinta maṭantiyākiya niṉṉi yām peṟutal kūṭumāyiṉ yāṉē varukuvaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
karumi, perumi; niṟappaṇpumām. erumi civanta kaṇṇuṭiyatu eṉpatik “karuṅkōṭṭuc ceṅkaṇ erumi ”1 eṉapataṉālum aṟika. taṉ kaṉṟiṉ niṉivu taṉ uḷḷattu eḻunta tuṇiyāṉē oḷḷiya  maṭicurantu pāl mikac coriyum vaḷam ciṟanta tākaliṉ, erumiyiṉ maṭiyi, ūṟumuli eṉac ciṟappittār. puṉiṟṟā muli maṭukkum ūr, nunti nummur eṉa iyiyum. uraṉuṭimi paṟṟik kūṟaliṉ, varutum eṉap paṉmi vantatu.
pallāṟṟāṉum cēṭpaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāl arumi ceytu ayarkkappaṭṭut taliviyip peṟalarumi niṉintu varuntumāṟu tōṉṟa niṉṉi yām peṟiṉē eṉṟum, peṟutal varivumāṭcimip paṭutalātaliṉ, atu nikaḻnta viṭattut taṉ tamar varavu tāḻppiṉum tāṉ tāḻātu varuvatu viḷaṅka, nunti nummūr varutum eṉṟum talimakaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. meyppāṭu: perumitam. payaṉ : varivu.
iṉi, pērāciriyar, “kiḻavōṟ kāyiṉ uraṉoṭu kiḷakkum, ēṉōrk kellām iṭamvari viṉṟē”2 eṉpuḻi, itaṉik kāṭṭi, “tāy pōṉṟu nummit taliyaḷippal eṉat talimakaṉ talimi tōṉṟa uraṉoṭu kiḷantavāṟu kāṇka” eṉpar. paḻiyavurikārar, “erumip puṉiṟṟāt taṉ kuḻavikku ūṟumuli maṭukkum eṉṟataṉāl, avaḷporuṭṭut tāṉ uṟṟār pakkal peṟuvaṉavum kūṟiyavāṟu” eṉpar. ivviru varuḷ, pērāciriyar kūṟuvatu, “varivuṭaṉ paṭutalum āṅkataṉ puṟattum”1 eṉpuḻi varivuṭaṉpaṭṭa talivaṉ nikaḻttum kūṟṟuvakiyilum, paḻiyavurikārar kūṟuvatu, “varital vēṇṭit tōḻi ceppiya, puritīr kiḷavi pulliya etir viṉkaṇ” talimakaṉ nikaḻttum kūṟṟuvakiyiṉum aṭaṅku māṟu kāṇka.
aruṅkōṭṭerumi eṉṟa pāṭattukkup pakiyiṉam neruṅkutaṟkariya kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya erumi eṉka.
	93.	eruminal lēṟṟiṉ mēya larunteṉap
pacumō rōṭamō ṭāmpa lollā
ceyta viṉiya maṉṟa palpoḻil
tātuṇa veṟukkiya vākiyivaḷ
pōtaviḻ mucci yūtum vaṇṭē.
muyakkam peṟṟavaḻip piṟanta veṟināṟṟattāl paṇṭiyaḷa vaṉṟi vaṇṭukaḷ moyttaṉavāka, “itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ ?” eṉṟu viṉāviya cevilit tāykkuk kūṟuvāḷ pōṉṟu talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi colliyatu.
uri :
aṉṉāy, pala poḻilkaḷilum malarntuḷḷa pūkkaḷiṉ tēṉākiya uṇavi veṟuppavuṇṭu, ivaḷatu malarnta pūvaṇinta kūntali mūci muralum vaṇṭiṉam, erumiyiṉ nalla ēṟṟiṉam mēyntu viṭṭataṉāl, paciya ceṅkaruṅ kāliyum āmpalum poruntāvāyiṉa; avi tēṉ īṭṭuva tākiya viṉi muṟṟiṉa, ataṉāl ivvāṟu moyppa vāyiṉakāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
veṟukkiyavāki, mucciyūtum vaṇṭu, ēṟṟiṉam mēyal arunteṉa, mōrōṭamōṭu āmpal ollā : avi ceyta viṉiya maṉṟa eṉa iyiyum. pōttu mutaliyaṉa pōla, ēṟu ēṟṟi eṉpaṉavum “yātta āṇpāṟ peyareṉa moḻipa”2 vākaliṉ, erumiyiṉ āṇ ēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. uḻutoḻiṟkum, vaṇṭiyiḻuttaṟkum payaṉpaṭum ciṟappuṭimiyiṉ nallēṟ ṟiṉam eṉṟār. ārnteṉa eṉpatu arunteṉa eṉa vantatu. mēyal ārnteṉpatu oru collāy mēyntu eṉṉum  poruḷatāyiṟṟu. ceyteṉa eṉṉum viṉiyeccam muṭikkum collāl uṇarttappaṭum toḻiṟkut taṉ mutaṉilit toḻil kāraṇam eṉpatupaṭa varum iyalpiṟṟākaliṉ, vaṇ ṭiṉam ollāmikku mēyalarunteṉa eṉṟatu ētuvāyiṟṟu. mōrōṭam, ceṅkaruṅkāli. itaṉpū mikka maṇamuṭiyatu; ippūvi ēṉi naṟumaṇa mikka pūkkaḷōṭu viravic ceppiṉuḷ aṭittuvittalum paṇṭiyōr marapu : “mutirā vēṉil etiriya atiral, parārip pātirik kuṟumayir māmalar, naṟumō rōṭamoṭu uṭaṉeṟin taṭicciya ceppu”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟiyavāṟṟāl iḵtu uṇarappaṭum. im mōrōṭam mārōṭa meṉak kuṟiñcippāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu; “kulli piṭavam ciṟumā rōṭam”2 eṉa varutal kāṇka. ceyta viṉi, ceytu muṭikkap peṟṟa viṉi; eṉavē, nikaḻvil avviṉiyiṉmi kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. maṉṟa, teḷivup poruṭṭāya iṭic col. palpoḻil eṉpatu viṉippaṭu tokuti yaṉmiyiṉ muṟṟummi vārātāyiṟṟu. pōtaviḻ mucci ūtum eṉṟata ṉāl, aviḻnta pōtiluḷḷa tēṉiyum uṇṇātu veṟitē mūci muraṉṟami peṟṟām.
kaḷavu neṟiyil talimakaṉik kūṭiyoḻukum talivi kūntalil vaṇṭiṉam mikutiyāka moyttatu ceviliyiṉ karuttil ārāycci piṟappikkavē, avaṭkut tōḻi pala poḻilkaḷukkum ceṉṟu āṅku malarntu tēṉ nirampi niṟkum pūttoṟum tēṉi veṟukka vuṇṭu avaṟṟi nītta vaṇṭiṉam ivaḷ kūntalil moyttu muralukiṉṟaṉa eṉpāḷ, palpoḻil tātuṇ veṟukkiya vāki ivaḷ pōtaviḻ mucci ūtum eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, poḻiṟ pūkkaḷi veṟuttamiyāl vaṇ ṭiṉam maṇaṅkaruti ivaḷ pōtaviḻ mucciyiṉi nayan taṉavē yaṉṟip piṟitilli eṉac cevilikku viṭiyiṟutta vāṟāyiṟṟu. “aṟukāṟ paṟavi yaḷavila moyttaliṉ, kaṇkō ḷāka nōkkip paṇṭum, iṉiyi yōeṉa viṉaviṉaḷ yāyē”1 eṉap piṟāṇṭum cevili viṉaviyavāṟu kāṇka. aṟṟēl, mōrōṭa mum, āmpalum ittakiya maṇaṅkamaḻvaṉa uḷavē eṉiṉ, aviyum erumiyiṉ ēṟṟiṉam mēyntu oḻintamiyiṉ ilavāyiṉa eṉpāḷ, erumi nallēṟṟiṉam mēyalarun teṉa, pacumōrōṭamoṭu āmpal ollā eṉṟāḷ. mōrōṭam makaḷir kūṟtaliṉ maṇaṅkamaḻum eṉpatu, “naṟumō rōṭamō ṭuṭaṉeṟin taṭicciya, ceppiṭan taṉṉa nāṟṟam tokkuṭaṉ, aṇiniṟaṅ koṇṭa maṇimaruḷ aimpāl, taḷar naṟuṅ katuppu”2* eṉavarum naṟṟiṇip pāṭṭālum, āmpaliṉ maṇamum attakiyatē eṉpatu, āmpalnāṟum tēmpoti naṟuviri”3 eṉavarum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷiṉ pēruriyālum iṉitu uṇarappaṭum.
eññāṉṟum tēṉ nāṭi īṭṭalē iṭiyaṟāta viṉiyāka vuṭiya vaṇṭiṉam, aḵtu oḻintu vāḷātu muralumāṟu illiyē eṉa eḻum viṉāviṟku viṭi kūṟuvāḷāy avi av viṉiyi muṟṟa muṭittu viṭṭaṉa eṉpāḷ ceyta viṉiya maṉṟa eṉṟāḷ. “koṅkutēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpi”4 eṉṟataṉāl, vaṇṭiṉam tēṉ nāṭum viṉiyiṉi yuṭiya eṉpatum, muṟṟāta viṉi ceyta viṉi eṉappaṭāmi yiṉ, viṉi muṟṟiyavāṟum aṟintukoḷka.
erumiyiṉ ēṟṟiṉam mēyntamiyāl vaṇṭiṉam mōrōṭamum āmpalum ollāvāyiṉa eṉṟatu, talivaṉōṭu kūṭiyataṉāl talimakaḷpāl uṟṟa vēṟupāṭṭiṉi avaḷtamar uṇarntu iṟceṟittamiyum, avi tātuṇa veṟukkiyavāki ivaḷ pōtaviḻ mucci yūtum eṉṟatu, ayalār tamakkuriya ceyalmēṟ cellātu ivaḷatu vēṟupāṭṭiṉic cūḻntu alar kūṟāniṟpar eṉpatum uḷḷuṟuttu urittavāṟu. meyppāṭu : perumitam. payaṉ : cevili aiyam tīrttalum, tōḻi kuṟippāl varivukaṭātalumām.
eruminal lēṟiṉa mēya larunteṉa eṉṟa pāṭattukku, ēṟṟiṉam ēṟiṉam eṉa vantatāka urittuk koḷka. pōtaviḻ mūccūy ūtum vaṇṭu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, pōtu aviḻntu nekiḻntu pataṉaḻiya ūtum vaṇṭu eṉṟu urittukkoḷka. ūytal, pataṉaḻital.
	94.	maḷḷa raṉṉa taṭaṅkōṭ ṭerumi
makaḷi raṉṉa tuṇiyoṭu vatiyum
niḻaṉmuti rilañcip paḻaṉat tatuvē
kaḻaṉit tāmari malaruṅ
kaviṉpeṟu cuṭarnutal tanti yūrē.
variviṭi vittup pirinta talimakaṉ mīḷkiṉṟāṉ colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
erumi tuṇiyoṭu vatiyum eṉṟatu varin teytiya vaḻit talimakaḷoṭu tāṉ ōḻukum iṉpa voḻukkattiṉi niṉintu kūṟiyavāṟu. kaḻaṉit tāmari malarum eṉṟatu av viṭattut taṉṉik kaṇṭu makiḻvār mukamalarcci kūṟiyavāṟām.
uri :
aḻakupeṟṟa oḷivīcum neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiyāṭkut tantiyiṉatu kaḻaṉikkaṇ tāmari malarum ūr, vīrar  pōṉṟa periya kompiṉiyuṭiya erumi avartam makaḷir pōṉṟa tuṇiyākiya peṇṇerumiyōṭu taṅkum niḻal cūḻnta nīrnilikku arukēyuḷḷa paḻaṉat tiṭattākum. eṉṟavāṟu.
erumikku vīrar uvamiyātal, “kārccē ṇikanta kari maruṅkiṉ nīrccērntu, erumi yeḻilēṟu eṟipavar cūṭic, cerumiku maḷḷariṟ cemmākkum”1 eṉṟum, “īrntaṇ erumic cuvalpaṭu mutupōttu........ mutūrp, pōrceṟi maḷḷariṟ pukutarum ūraṉ”2 eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟumāṟṟāl teḷiyap paṭum. āṇ erumikku maḷḷarik kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ tuṇikku avartam makaḷiri uvami kūṟiṉār. mutirtal, cūḻtal; “pārmutir paṉikkaṭal”3 eṉṟāṟ pōla. taṭaṅkōṭu, uriccol muṭipu. paḻaṉam, ūravarkkup potuvākiya nilam eṉpar.
taṉakkuc ciṟantāḷākiya talimakaḷit talimakaṉ niṉik kuntōṟum, avaḷatu tirunutal avaṉ neñciṭit tōṉṟi aḻakum oḷiyum tikaḻa niṉṟu taṉṉi avaṉ kaṇṇiṟ kāṇum pōḻtiṉum mikku viḷaṅkutaliṉ, kaviṉpeṟu cuṭarnutal eṉac colli iṉpuṟṟāṉ. vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻip pirintāṉ oruvaṉ, viṉi muṭiyāmiyiṉ pācaṟikkaṇ iruntu tāṉ kuṟitta paruvavaravu kaṇṭu āṟṟātu taṉ taliviyi niṉintāṉāka, avaṉ uḷḷattum avaḷatu nutal tōṉṟutaliṉ, aḵtu, “ilaṅku nilaviṉ iḷampiṟi pōla....... kaviṉpeṟu cuṭar nutal”4 eṉṟu avaṉāl ciṟappikkappaṭṭa vāṟaṟika. itu, “paṇpiṟ peyarppiṉum”5 eṉṟa cūttirattu “āṟṟiṭi yuṟu talum” eṉṟataṉi iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāl, “variviṭi vittup pirintāṉ āṟṟiṭi varuttamuṟṟuk kūṟuvaṉavum” koḷḷappaṭum eṉṟu koṇṭu amittuk koḷḷappaṭum. ataṉuḷ, variviṭivittup pōkiṉṟāṉ kūṟuvaṉavum mīḷkiṉṟāṉ kūṟuvaṉavum eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ itu varintu eytiyavaḻit talimakaḷōṭu tāṉ oḻukum iṉpa oḻukkat tiyum, variviṉkaṇ kāṇpōr makiḻumāṟṟiyum uḷ ḷuṟuttuk kūṟutaliṉ mīḷkiṉṟāṉ kūṟṟāyiṟṟu.
variviṭivittup poruḷvayiṟ piripavaṉ, atu muṟṟātavaḻi ataṉāṉām payaṉi niṉintu taṉṉuḷ makiḻtal āḷviṉi mikka avaṉ talimippaṇpukku iḻukkātaliṉ, avvāṟaṉṟi, atu muṟṟiyatum ataṉāl eytaluṟum payaṉum uṭaṉeytiyatu pōṟaliṉ, taṉṉuḷ makiḻntu tāṉ peṟa virukkum iṉpa mikutiyiṉi niṉintu uvakiyuṟuvāṉ, maḷḷar aṉṉa taṭaṅkōṭṭerumi, makaḷir aṉṉa tuṇiyoṭu vatiyum eṉṟu viyantāṉ. niḻalmutir ilañcip paḻaṉattatu eṉṟatu, talivi maṉiyiṉ aruḷum poruḷum ciṟappittavāṟu.
itukāṟum piṟar aṟivuṟutal añci maṟintu oḻukiyatu pōlātu kaṇṭōr makiḻntu mukamalarcci eytumāṟu oḻukuvatu niṉintu uvakimikutiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu eytak kaḻaṉit tāmari malarum eṉṟāṉ. talivikku ūr taṉṉūrē yāyiṉum, avaḷ vāḻum pakutiyi, avaṭku ayaṉmi tōṉṟat tantiyūr eṉṟāṉām. iṉit taṉ varivu etirkoṇṭu makaṭkoṭi nērum urimiyuṭiyavaṉ avaḷ tantiyākaliṉ tanti yūr eṉṟāṉ eṉṟumām. meyppāṭu : uvaki. payaṉ : makiḻtal.
tiṟaṅkōṭu eṉṟa pāṭattukku maḷḷar pōlat toḻiṟ kūṟupāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya kōṭu eṉka. toḻiṟ kūṟupāṭu, kolivaki.
ivi nāṉkum marutattuk kaḷavu.
	95.	karuṅkōṭ ṭerumi kayiṟuparin tacii
neṭuṅkatir nelli ṉāṇmēya lārum
puṉaṉmuṟ ṟūraṉ pakalum
paṭarmali yarunōy ceytaṉa ṉemakkē.
uṇṭikkālattu maṉikkaṇ varutalum curuṅkip parattiyiṭat taṉāyt talimakaṉ oḻukiyavaḻi avaṟku vāyilāy vantārkkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
erumi kayiṟṟip parintu pōy nāṇmēyal ārum eṉṟatu, vilakkuvārkku aṭaṅkātu puṟattoḻukkam virumpuvāṉ eṉṟa vāṟu.
uri :
periya kompukaḷiyuṭiya erumikaḷ iravil tām kaṭṭappeṟṟirukkum kayiṟṟi aṟuttuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu neṭiya katirkaḷiyuṭiya nelliṉi nāḷuṇavāka uṇ ṇum nīr niṟinta vayal cūḻnta ūraṉ, pakaṟkālattum paṭarmali yarunōyiṉi emakkuc ceytu oḻukukiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ, avaṉ poruṭṭu vāyil vēṇṭuvatu eṉṉi eṉṟavāṟu.
parital, aṟuttukkōṭal; “cēṟṟunili muṉiiya ceṅkaṇ kārāṉ, ūrmaṭi kaṅkuliṉ nōṉṟaḷi parintu, kūrmuḷ vēli kōṭṭiṉ nīkki”1 eṉac cāṉṟōr urikkumāṟu kāṇka. nāṇmēyal viṭiyaṟkālattiṟ peṟum mēyccal; nāḷiri, nāḷuṇavu eṉṟāṟ pēla. “iruveti rīṉṟa vēṟṟalik koḻumuḷi, cūṉmutir maṭappiṭi nāṇmēyal ārum”2 eṉap periyōr vaḻaṅkumā ṟaṟika. parattimiyāl iravuppōtil vārāmiyē yaṉṟi, uṇṭikkālamākiya pakaṟpōtilum talivaṉatu vārāmi uṇartti niṟṟaliṉ ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu. muṟṟutal, cūḻtal. paṭarmali arunōy, pala kālum niṉippeḻutalāl  uḷatākum poṟuttaṟkariya nōy. itu cāṉṟōr urikaḷil “naṉṉutaṟ pacanta paṭarmali yaru nōy”3 eṉavum, “paṭarmali evvamoṭu mātiran tuḻii ”4 eṉavum varutal kāṇka.
tōḻi mutaliya akampukum marapiṉ vāyilkaḷ vilakkavum aṭaṅkātu puṟattoḻukkamē virumpit talimakaṉ iraviṟ pirintoḻukiyataṉāl, paṭarmikuntu tāṉ varunti iruntataṉi uḷḷuṟiyāṟ kuṟittavaḷ, “iravukkālattu avaṉ vārāmi yāl eḻunta varuttatti uṇṭikōṭaṟkup pakaṟkālattu varutalāl āṟṟiyiruntēṉ; iṉi atuvum oḻintamiyāl āṟṟēṉāyiṉōṉ” eṉpāḷāy, pakalum paṭarmali aru nōy ceytaṉaṉ eṉa iṉintu kūṟiṉāḷ. vārāmiyākiya viṉi avaṉpāl nikaḻntatāyiṉum, ataṉ payaṉāy viḷiyum tuṉpam iravum pakalum muṟukip perukip poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpattip payappatāyiṟ ṟeṉṟaṟkup paṭarmali arunōy eṉṟum, atu tāṉum avaṉ vantavaḻi arukutalum vārāvaḻip perukutalum ceytaliṉ annōykku mutal avaṉē eṉpāḷ ceytaṉaṉ eṉṟum avaṉ viṉiyāṟ kūṟiṉāḷ. vārā vaḻi, avaṉ taṉakkuc ceyta taliyaḷi niṉivil eḻuntu varuttuva tōṭu paratti mutalāyiṉārkkuc ceytalāl poṟāmi tōṟṟi mikka nōyiṉi viḷivippat tāṉ āṟṟāḷāvatu kaṇṭu tōḻi mutalāyiṉārum āṟṟātu mika varuntukiṉṟami yiṉ tāṉ āṟṟāmi mītūrntami tōṉṟat talivi, emakkē eṉa eṭuttu moḻintāḷ.
iravil erumi kayiṟṟip parintupōy nelliṉ nāṇ mēyal ārum eṉṟataṉāl, talivaṉ iravuppōtil vārātu vilakkuvār collum koḷḷātu puṟattoḻukiyatu kūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu. neṭuṅkatir nel eṉac ciṟappittatu, parattiyiṉ nalattip pukaḻvatu pōlap paḻittavāṟu. parattiyiṉ nalatti nāṇ mēyal eṉṟamiyiṉ an nalattiṉ putumi cuṭṭi ṉāḷām; ākavē, ivvāṟu yāvar vilakkiṉiyum kikaṭantu ceṉṟu putunala makaḷirik kūṭiyuṟipavaṉ, emmi niṉintu varumāṟu illi yākaliṉ avaṉ poruṭṭu nīvir vāyil vēṇṭuvatu eṉṉi? eṉa maṟuttavāṟu. iḵtu aḻivil kūṭṭattu avaṉ pirivu āṟṟāmi. ēṉi meyppāṭu : vekuḷi. payaṉ : vāyiṉ maṟuttal.
	96.	aṇinaṭi yerumi yāṭiya vaḷḷal maṇiniṟa neyta lāmpaloṭu kalikkum
kaḻaṉi yūraṉ makaḷivaḷ
paḻaṉa vūraṉ pāyaliṉ ṟuṇiyē.
parattiyar palarōṭum oḻukutalkaṇṭu poṟātirunta tali
makaḷ talimakaṉ maṉikkaṭ pukuntuḻi uṭaṉpaṭutal kaṇṭa vāyilkaḷ tammuḷḷē colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
erumi uḻutu uḻakkiya aḷḷaṟkaṇṇē neytalum āmpa lum kalikkum eṉṟatu, talimakaṟku vēṇṭuvaṉa purikiṉṟa ilvāḻkkik kaṇṇē tām peṟukiṉṟa ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟu.
uri :
aḻakiya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya erumi uḻakkiya cēṟṟiṉkaṇ nīlamaṇiyiṉ niṟattiṉiyuṭiya neytalum āmpalum taḻik kum kaḻaṉikaḷiyuṭiya ūraṉukku makaḷākiya ivaḷ paḻaṉaṅ kaḷiyuṭiya ūraṉākiya talimakaṟkup pāyal iṉtuṇivi āyiṉāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
“varimaṇal ñemarak kaṟpaka naṭakkum, perumitappakaṭu 1” eṉac cāṉṟōr ciṟappikkum aḻaku uṭimiyiṉ aṇinaṭi yerumi eṉṟār. aḻaku - īṇṭup perumitam. erumi uḻakkiya vayal cēṟuṟṟu uḻutatupōla vaḷampeṟutaliṉ, neytalum āmpalum taḻippa vāyiṉa eṉa uṇarka. neytal nīlamaṇipōlvatu eṉpatu, “curumpuṇa malarnta peruntaṇ neytal, maṇiyēr māṇalam”2 eṉa varutalālum tuṇiyappaṭum. ūraṉmakaḷ eṉpatu “atuveṉ vēṟṟumi uyartiṇit toki”yātaliṉ, nāṉkaṉurupu viritturikkappaṭṭatu; ēṉi yiṭaṅkaḷiṉum itaṉi urittuk koḷka. pāyal iṉ tuṇi, kūṭṭattiṟku iṉiya tuṇi, “pāyal iṉṟuṇiyākiya paṇittōḷ, tōki māṭciya maṭanti”3 eṉap piṟāṇṭum varumāṟu kāṇka. pāyam, uṟakkamumām; “paṉṉāḷum pāyal peṟēeṉ paṭarkūrntu”4 eṉṟāṟ pōla.
parattiyar palaruṭaṉ kūṭi oḻukum pāṉmiyaṉ eṉṟaṟkup paḻaṉavūraṉ eṉṟum, av voḻukkattāṟ poṟātu vēṟu paṭṭiruntāḷ avaṉ varutalum pulatta liṉṟik kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭu amintamiyiṉ pāyal iṉṟuṇi eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
tām vāḻum aḷḷaṟkaṭ pukuntu tammi mayakki uḻakkiya erumikkut tāmē kalittup payaṉpaṭum neytalum āmpalum uḷḷa kaḻaṉiyūraṉ makaḷ eṉṟatu, talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkattāl taṉṉik kalakki nutal pacantu mēṉi vēṟupāṭu eytuvittāṉā kavum, talivi avaṉ pukkavaḻi etirēṟṟu vaḻipāṭu purintami kaṟpu nalattiṟku ētuvāki niṉṟatu. itu, “kaṟpuṅ kāmamum”1 eṉpuḻik kūṟappaṭum kiḻatti māṇpukaḷuḷ naṟpāloḻukkam.
iṉi, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, “itu kaṟpuk kūṟiyatu” eṉṟum, ivvāṟu vāyilkaḷ tammuṭ kūṟiyatu, “vāyil ucāvē tammuḷum uriya”2 eṉpataṉāl amiyum eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iṉip paḻiyavurikārar. erumiyiṉ ceytiyāl, āmpalum neytalum taḻikku meṉṟatu, vāyil peṟum ciṟappukkuṟittu niṉṟatu eṉpatu kāṇka. meyppāṭu : viyappu. payaṉ : tammuḷ makiḻtal.
aṇiniṟa verumi eṉṟa pāṭattukku aḻakiya niṟam uṭiya erumi yeṉṟu poruḷkūṟik koḷka.
	97.	pakaṉṟi vāṉmalar miṭinta kōṭṭik
karuntā ḷerumik kaṉṟu verūum
poyki yūraṉ makaḷivaḷ
poykip pūvi ṉaṟuntaṇ ṇiyaḷē.
puṟattoḻukkam iṉṟiyē  yirukkavum uḷateṉṟu pulanta talimakaḷip pulavi nīkkiya talimakaṉ puṇarcciyatu iṟutik
kaṇ taṉṉuḷḷē colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
tāyiṉuṭiya pakaṉṟimalar miṭinta kōṭṭik kaṉṟu verūum eṉṟatu, taṉ tōḷil aṇinta māliyip piṟi toṉṟaṟ kaṇinta māli yeṉak karutiṉāḷ eṉpatām.
uri :
pakaṉṟiyiṉ veḷḷiya malarmiṭinta kariya tāḷkaḷi yuṭiya tāyerumiyiṉ kōṭṭik kaṇṭu ataṉ kaṉṟu añcum poyki yūraṉukku makaḷākiya ivaḷ, poykik kaṇ malarnta āmpaṟpūviṉum kuḷircciyiyuṭiyaḷāvaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
vāṉmalar, vāliya malar; “vāṉcuti vaṇṇam koḷal”1 eṉṟāṟpōla. miṭital, neruṅkac cūṭutal. erumi pakaṉṟi miṭinta kōṭṭavāy varumeṉpataṉi, “erumic cuvalpaṭu mutupōttu........... kurūukkoṭip pakaṉṟi cūṭi mūtūrp, pōrcceṟi maḷḷariṟ pukutarum”2 eṉpataṉālum, atu karun tāḷatu eṉpatu kāṭciyaḷaviyāṉēyaṉṟi, “kaḻunīr mēynta karuntāḷ erumi”3 eṉavarum cāṉṟōr uriyālum uṇarap paṭum. “āmpal malariṉum tāṉ taṇṇiyaḷē ”4 eṉṟu cāṉ ṟōr kūṟutaliṉ, poykippū eṉṟatu āmpalāyiṟṟu. aintāvatu uṟaḻviṉkaṇ vantatu. taṭpatti uṇarntu makiḻntu kūṟaliṉ, ēkāram tēṟṟam. kaṉṟu verūum ūraṉ, poyki yūraṉ eṉa iyiyum.
taṉpāṟ puṟattoḻukkam iṉṟākavum, uḷateṉak karutip pulanta taliviyit teḷiyat takuvaṉa kūṟik kūṭiṉāṉākaliṉ, ataṉip pakaṉṟi vāṉmalar miṭinta kōṭṭik karuntāḷ erumik kaṉṟu verūum eṉa uḷḷuṟuttu urittāṉ. veyil teṟum vempakaṟ pōtiṉkaṇ poyki nīr puṟattē ciṟu vemmiyum akattē taṭpamum uṭiyatātal pōlat tāṉ puṟattoḻukiya kālattut talivi, puṟattē vekuṇṭāḷ pōlavum akattē aṉpumikkum oḻukaṟpālaḷ eṉpāṉ poykiyūraṉ makaḷ eṉṟum, aḵtilvaḻi eñ ñāṉṟum taṭpaṅkuṉṟāta poykippūppōl taṇṇiyaḷātaṟ kuriyāḷ, ataṉiṉum mikka taṭpamuṭiyaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉpāṉ poykip pūviṉum naṟuntaṇṇiyaḷē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. puṇarccikkaṇ akamum puṟamum vemmiyiṉṟi mikak kuḷirntu iṉpam ceytalpaṟṟi, naṟuntaṇṇiyaḷ eṉṟāṉ; paṇṭu kaḷaviṟ puṇarcci eytiyapōtum, “oṭuṅkī rōti oṇṇutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ, naṟuntaṇ ṇīraḷ ”1 eṉṟu tōḻiyum kūṟiṉāḷ.
poyki eṉṟatu, kuṭimicciṟappiṉic cuṭṭiyatāyiṉ, iṉṉā ceytārkkum iṉiyavē ceytoḻukum cālpuṭimi viḷakkiyatākak koḷka. ivvāṟu taliviyit talivaṉ pukaḻtal vēṭkimikutiyāl nikaḻtaliṉ amiyum eṉpar; “nikaḻtaki maruṅkiṉ vēṭki mikutiyiṉ, pukaḻtaki variyār kaṟpi ṉuḷḷē”2 eṉpa.
tāyerumiyiṉ kōṭṭiṟ kiṭanta pakaṉṟi malarik kaṇṭu ataṉ kaṉṟu vēṟupaṭa niṉintu veruviyatu pōla, taṉ tōḷiṟ kiṭanta māliyip puṟattē piṟar aṇinta teṉap piṟaḻa uṇarntu pulakkiṉṟāḷ eṉṟāṉām. iḵtu uḷḷuṟi vaki aintaṉuḷ nakiyiṉpāṟpaṭum. meyppāṭu : uvaki. payaṉ makiḻtal.
	98.	taṇpuṉa lāṭun taṭaṅkōṭ ṭerumi
tiṇpiṇi yampiyiṟ ṟōṉṟu mūra
oṇṭoṭi maṭamaka ḷivaḷiṉum
nuntiyum yāyuṅ kaṭiyarō niṉṉē.
puṟattoḻukkam uḷatākiya tuṇiyāṉē pulantu vāyil nērāta talimakaḷ koṭumi talimakaṉ kūṟak kēṭṭa tōḻi avaṟkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
ivaḷiṉum nuntiyum yāyuṅ kaṭiyarō eṉṟatu, niṉṉiṭattuk kuṟṟam uḷatākiyavaḻik kaḻaṟuṅkāl ivaḷiṉum kaṭiyarō nunti yum yāyum eṉṟavāṟu; avariṉum kaṭumiyāṟ kūṟutaṟ kuriyāḷ ivaḷ eṉpatām.
nīrāṭum erumi palarum ēṟutaṟkuriya ampipōlat tōṉṟum eṉṟatu, palarkkum uriyiyāvi yeṉap pulantāḷ eṉpatām.
uri :
kuḷirnta nīriṉkaṇ āṭum periya kompiṉiyuṭiya erumi tiṇṇitāyp piṇikkappaṭṭa ampipōlat tōṉṟum ūraṉē, niṉpāṟ kuṟṟamuḷatāya viṭattu atu paṟṟi niṉṉik kaṭiyuṅkāl niṉ tantiyum tāyum oḷḷiya toṭiyum maṭappamu muṭiya ivaḷiṉum kaṭumi yuṭiya rāvarō? kūṟuka eṉṟavāṟu.
tiṇ piṇi yampi, aciviṉṟit tiṇṇitāyp piṇikkap paṭṭa ampi. ampi - tōṇi. nīraliyāl acitaliṉ nīkku taṟkut tiṇṇitāyp piṇittal vēṇṭutaliṉ, tiṇpiṇi yampi eṉṟāḷ. erumikku ampi uvamiyātal, “mari yēṟṟiṉ mēlirun tāṭit tuṟiyampi, yūrvāṉpōl tōṉṟu mavaṉ”1 eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. ivaḷiṉum eṉpuḻi uṟaḻcci, tanti tāyariṉum mikkuṟṟa ciṟappiṉi yuṭiyāḷ eṉpatu tōṉṟa niṉṟatu. acciṟappu, tantitāy eṉṟa iruvar kaṭaṉum tāṉ oruttiyē āṟṟutal. kuṇanāṭi makiḻtaliṉum kuṟṟa nāṭik kaṭintu tūymi ceytalil ciṟantamiyiṉ, tantiyi muṟkūṟiṉār. “cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantikkuk kaṭaṉē”2  eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. niṉ ṉeṉpuḻi iraṇṭāvatu tokkatu; “aiyuṅ kaṇṇum allāp poruḷ vayiṉ, meyyurupu tokā iṟuti yāṉa ”3 eṉa āciriyar kūṟiyavāṟu aṟika.
