uḷmelivu nikaḻntaviṭattum, ataṉip puṟattē tōṉṟā vaki maṟittal āṇmip paṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟāka, av vāṟaṉṟit tōḻiyiṭic ceṉṟu kuṟiyuṟutal paṟṟip paṇpum, av vuḷḷamelivāl uṟakka miṉmiyiṉ pāyalum kūṟi ivaṟṟik koṇṭaṉaḷ eṉṟāṉ. koṭuppak koḷvaḻik, koṇṭaṉaḷ eṉṟal ākāmiyiṉ, kavarntu koṇṭami tōṉṟak koṇṭaṉaḷ eṉa vāḷātu kūṟi, kūṟumati tavaṟē eṉṟataṉāl apporuṇmi eyta vittāṉ. paṇpum pāyalum taṉakkē uriyavāyiṉum, avaḷ virumpiyavaḻi avi koḷḷappaṭutalum paṭāmiyum uṭiya eṉpatupaṭap paṇpum pāyalum koṇṭaṉaḷ eṉṟataṉāl tāṉ avaḷ varippiṉa ṉāyatum peṟappaṭum. paṭavē, taṉpāl kuṟṟam nikaḻiṉ, ataṉik kāṭṭit tirutti avaṟṟi mīṭṭum peṟuvittal muṟi eṉṟaṟkuk kūṟumati tavaṟē eṉṟāṉ.
matiyuṭampaṭuttal vēṇṭit tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟukiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ, avaḷi, toṭiyum alkulum mēṉiyu muṭiyāy eṉac ciṟappittāṉ.
oruvar varippiṉarāy oruvar niṉṟu paṇipuriyumiṭattu, avarpāl tavaṟilvaḻi, avar avaruṭiya paṇpum pāyalum keṭap paṇipurivittal ākātu eṉpatoru nayam tōṉṟiya vāṟu kāṇka.
itaṉāl, talimakaḷmuṉ niṉṟu tāṉ kuṟiyirattaṟku iṭam kāṭṭit taṉ kuṟiyit tōḻi uṇartaṟku iyipupaṭak kuṟiyuṟumāṟṟi īṇṭu viḷaṅkak kūṟiṉāṉ eṉa aṟika.
āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, “meytoṭṭup  payiṟal 1” eṉṟa cūttirattut tōḻi kuṟiyavaṭ cārtti meyyuṟak kūṟalum” eṉpataṟku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉar. veḷippaṭi yum kuṟippumākiya akkūṟṟuvaki iraṇṭaṉuḷ itu veḷip paṭak kūṟiyatu.
	177.	tavaṟila rāyiṉum paṉippa maṉṟa
ivaṟutiri tiḷikkum iṭumaṇal neṭuṅkōṭṭu
muṇṭaka naṟumalar kamaḻum
toṇṭi yaṉṉōḷ tōḷuṟ ṟōrē.
itu talimakaḷum tōḻiyum oruṅku niṉṟuḻi, “ivaḷ eṉṉi varuttutaṟkuc ceyta tavaṟu eṉ?” eṉṟu viṉāviya talimakaṟkut tōḻi nakiyāṭic colliyatu.
paḻiya uri1:
tōḷuṟṟōr eṉṟatu, tōḷi etirppaṭṭōr eṉṟavāṟu. nīraṟā nilattu muṇṭaka naṟumalar kamaḻum toṇṭi aṉṉōḷ eṉṟatu ivaḷ nām aṇuki nukartaṟkariyaḷ eṉṟavāṟu. itaṉ iṟicci........
uri :
oṉṟaṉi yoṉṟu mēṟpaṭ ṭeḻum ali poruta lāl koḻittiṭappaṭṭa maṇal paranta uyarnta kariyiṉi yuṭiya tuṟikkaṇ nīrmuḷḷiyiṉ naṟiya malarkaḷiṉ maṇam kamaḻum ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya toṇṭinakar pōlvāḷatu tōḷi etirppaṭṭōr tavaṟu oṉṟum ceytilarāyiṉum teḷivāka naṭukkamuṟṟu varuntuvar; ākaliṉ, yāṉ kūṟumā ṟeṉṉi? eṉṟavāṟu.
ivaṟutal, oṉṟaṉi yoṉṟu mēṟpaṭṭeḻutal. iva ṟaṉmi yeṉpataṟkup parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟum uri 2 kāṇka. ivaṟutiri tiḷikkum iṭumaṇal neṭuṅkōṭu eṉṟataṟku, nīraṟāmiyāl ali vantu kalakka ataṉāl alippuṇṭu kari keṭātavāṟu putumaṇal iṭappaṭṭu uyarnta kariyiṉi yuṭiya tuṟi eṉṟu urippiṉum amiyum. tōḷuṟṟōr eṉṟatu tōḷik kūṭiṉōr eṉṟu poruḷkōṭal ami yātu eṉṟaṟkup paḻiya urikārar “tōḷi etirp paṭṭōr” eṉṟu kūṟuvatu kāṇka. paṉittal, paṇpum pāyalum iḻantu varuntutal.
talimakaṉ tōḻiyi matiyuṭampaṭukkum karuttiṉāl avaḷum taliviyum oruṅku niṉṟaviṭattup pōntu, paṇpum pāyalum iḻantu tāṉ paṉikkumāṟu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, tōḷuṟ ṟōr eṉṟu talivaṉatu talippeytuṭimi tāṉ uṇarnta vāṟu tōḻi pulappaṭuttiṉāḷ eṉa aṟika. tāṉ eytiya varuttattukku ētuvākat tāṉ ceyta tavaṟu yātu eṉṟu viṉāviya talivaṟku, tavaṟilvaḻiyum paṉittal tōḷuṟṟōr iyalpākaliṉ, tavaṟṟiṉ uṇmiyiṉmikaḷ ārāytal vēṇṭā eṉpāḷ, tavaṟila rāyiṉum paṉippa eṉṟum, tavaṟilār paṉittal yāṅkaṉam eṉak kaṭāveḻuntu coṟpal kāmi nōkki, maṉṟa eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “kūṟumati tavaṟē 1” eṉa vēṇṭi niṉṟāṟku aḵtu uṇṭeṉṟēṉum, iṉṟeṉṟēṉum kūṟātu, tavaṟila rāyiṉum paṉippa maṉṟa eṉa muṭintatu muṭittalākap potuvāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, “niṉpāl tavaṟili yāyiṉum tavaṟu uṇṭu pōlum eṉak karutip paṉittal niṉmāṭṭē amintatu” eṉṟāḷām. itaṉāl, tōḻimāṭṭu naki tōṉṟiṟṟeṉa aṟika. koḷaṟkarumiyum perukiya tiruvuṭimiyum paṟṟik kaṇṭōr paṉittal iyalpē eṉṟaṟku avviraṇṭumuṭiya toṇṭi nakari uvamittāḷ.
itaṉ iṟiccipporuḷ paḻiyavurippakutiyil citintu viṭṭatu. nīr iṭiyaṟātu cūḻnta maṇaṟkōṭṭil niṟkum muṇṭakam malarntu naṟumaṇam kamaḻvatu pōla, ariya kāvalaminta kaṭimaṉikkaṇ peru nalam tikaḻat talimakaḷ viḷakkamuṟuvatum, an naṟumaṇam kaṇṭōr uḷḷattik kavaṟṟiṉum, koḷḷaṟ karitāyatu pōla, ivaḷ nalattāl nī paṉittu varuntiṉum koḷḷaṟ kariyaḷ eṉat tōḻi uḷḷuṟi yāl arumi ceytu ayarvittami kāṇka. itu cēṭpaṭi. muṇṭakam neytalnilak karupporuḷ. pāṭṭiṭi vanta karupporuḷi iṟicci eṉpatum vaḻakku. “iṟiccip poruḷvayiṉ ceyyuḷuṭ kiḷakkum iyaṟ peyarkkiḷavi 2” eṉṟum, “aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicciyuṭ cuṭṭalum 3” eṉṟum āciriyar kūṟuvatu kāṇka. āyiṉum, pāṭṭiṭip poruṭku ottu muṭiyum poruḷ uḷḷuṟiyuvamam eṉavum, ap poruṭkup puṟamāy vēṟupaṭak kāṇpatu iṟicci eṉavum vēṟupāṭu terintukoḷka. naṟumaṇam kamaḻum neṭuṅkōṭṭu muṇṭakam orukāl neruṅkiṉum muḷḷuṭimiyāl koḷaṟku aritāmāṟu pōla, uṭaṉuṟiyiṉum eṉṉāl ivaḷi uṭaṉpaṭuvittal iyalātu eṉat tōḻi kūṟiyatāka iṟicci peṟappaṭumāṟu kāṇka.
	178.	tōḷum kūntalum palapā rāṭṭi
vāḻta lollumō maṟṟē ceṅkōṟ
kuṭṭuvaṉ toṇṭi yaṉṉa
eṟkaṇṭu nayantunī nalkāk kālē
itu, talimakaṉ tōḻiyi irantu kuṟiyuṟuvāṉ colliyatu.
uri :
cevviya muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya kuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṟ kuriya toṇṭinakar pōlum eṉṉi, eṉ kuṟi mikka niliyiṉik kaṇṭum nī virumpi aruḷāyāyiṉ, ivaḷuṭiya tōḷum kūntalum pārāṭṭi vāḻum il vāḻkki eṉakku iyalumō? iyalātu kāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
irantu kuṟiyuṟutaṟkaṇ, irattal eṉpatu kuṟiyuṭiyār ceyyum ceyki ceytoḻukuvatu. kuṟiyuṟutal eṉpatu ikkuṟi iṉṟiyamiyāṉ eṉpatu paṭap pacin toḻukuvatu. ivi ceytoḻukal uyarnta talimakaṟku ilakkaṇameṉa vaṟika. “puṇarnta piṉṟi āṅṅaṉam oḻukātu, paṇinta moḻi yāṉ tōḻi tēettu, irantukuṟi yuṟutalum kiḻavōṉ mēṟṟē1” eṉpatu viti. maṟṟu: acinili. cevviya kōlpōṟaliṉ, muṟimi, ceṅkōl eṉappaṭṭatu. toṇṭi kuṭṭuvaṟ kurittātal, “veṇkōṭ ṭiyāṉi viṟaṟpōrk kuṭṭuvaṉ, teṇṭirip parappiṉ toṇṭi 2” eṉpataṉālu maṟika. cēra nāṭu, kuṭṭanāṭu, kuṭanāṭu, poṟināṭu eṉap pala nāṭukaḷākap pirintu taṉittaṉiyē cērar kuṭiyil tōṉṟiya aracarkaḷāl āṭci ceyyappaṭiṉum, avaruḷ vaḻimuṟit tōṟṟattāl uriyavaṉ muṭivēntaṉāka, ēṉiyōr avaṉ vaḻiyaracāy āṭcipurivar; kuṭṭuvaṉ muṭivēntaṉāyiṉ, kuṭakkōvum, irumpoṟiyum vaḻiyaracāvar; kuṭakkō muṭikkuriyaṉā yiṉ kuṭṭuvar kōvum poṟiyar kōvum vaḻiyaracāvar. tantikkuppiṉ avaṉ mutaṉmakaṉ, avaṟkuppiṉ avaṉ mutaṉmakaṉ aracuk kuriyaṉāka varum muṟi 1 tamiḻar aracu muṟiyaṉṟu; atu vaṭavar aracumuṟi. immuṟi teriyāmal teṉṉāṭṭu varalāṟu kāṇpōr palar taṭumāṟukiṉṟaṉar. niṟka, innūlāciriyar kālattil kuṭṭuvaṉ cēramāṉāy maṇimuṭiyum eḻumuṭi keḻīiya tiruvum uṭiyaṉāy viḷaṅkiṉamiyiṉ, poṟi nāṭṭut toṇṭiyic ceṅkōṟ kuṭṭuvaṉ toṇṭi eṉṟu ciṟappittār eṉa aṟika. eṟkaṇṭu eṉṟatu āṟṟalāl avaṉatu kuṟimikka nilimi uṇara niṉṟatu.
illiruntu nallaṟam puriyumiṭattut talimakaḷik kūṭun toṟum “aṟitō ṟaṟiyāmi 2” pōlap puttiṉpam tōṉṟak kaṇṭu makiḻvāṉ avaḷ nalam pārāṭṭal iyalpākaliṉ, tōḷum kūntalum pala pārāṭṭi eṉṟāṉ. kūṭṭattiṟ kūntaliṉum tōḷ ciṟantamiyiṉ, tōḷi muṟkūṟiṉāṉ. “uvaviṉi vāḻiya neñcē miyaṟa, vikucuṭar viḷaṅkum vāṉṟōy viyaṉakarc, cuṇaṅkaṇi vaṉamuli nalampā rāṭṭit tāḻiruṅ kūntalnaṅ kātali, nīḷami vaṉappiṉ tōḷumā raṇintē 3” eṉpataṉālu maṟika. talimakaḷik kūṭi vāḻum vāḻkkiyi yiṉṟit tāṉ amiyāmi tōṉṟa niṟṟaliṉ, eṟkaṇṭu eṉṟum, avaḷit tāṉ peṟaṟkaṇ avaṭkuc ciṟantāḷāya tōḻiyiṉ uṭampāṭilvaḻip peṟalaritu eṉpāṉ nayantu eṉṟum, nalkākkālē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. vāḻtal ollumō eṉṟataṉāl, nī nalkākkāl yāṉ uyirvāḻtalum kūṭumō eṉpatu kuṟippāl tōṉṟak kūṟiṉami peṟṟām.
parivuṟṟu meliyum eṉṉik kaṇṭum eṉṟatu, tali makaṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya tuṉpattup pulampal. vāḻtal ollumō eṉṟatu, “nalattaka nāṭiṉ kalakkamum atuvē4” eṉpuḻip peṟṟa kalakkam.
iṉi, “meytoṭṭup payiṟal 1” eṉṟa cūttirattu, “aṟintō ḷayarppiṉ avvaḻi maruṅkiṟ, kēṭum pīṭum kūṟalum” eṉpuḻi itaṉi aṭakkuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. avar karuttuppaṭi, tōḷum kūntalum pārāṭṭutal nalkiyavaḻi eytum pīṭum, vāḻtal ollumō eṉṟatu nalkāvaḻi eytum kēṭum eṉak koḷka. iḷampūraṇar tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟum pakutikku utāraṇamākkuvar.2
	179.	nalkumati vāḻiyō naḷinīrc cērppa alavaṉ tākkat tuṟiyiṟāp piṟaḻum iṉṉolit toṇṭi yaṟṟē niṉṉala tillā vivaḷciṟu nutalē.
itu, kuṟiyiṭattu vantu nīṅkum talimakaṉit tōḻi etirp paṭṭu varivukaṭāyatu.
paḻiya uri :
alavaṉ tākkat tuṟi iṟāp piṟaḻum eṉṟatu, iv voḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉākiya ñāṉṟu ivaḷ uyir vāḻāḷ eṉpatām.
uri :
periya kaṭaṟkariyiyuṭiya cērppaṉē, niṉṉi iṉṟiyamiyāta ivaḷatu ciṟiya nutal, karikkaṇ vāḻum alavaṉ tākkutalāl tuṟikkaṇ vāḻum iṟāmīṉ puraḷum iṉiya oliyiṉiyuṭiya toṇṭinakar pōlvatākaliṉ, nī ataṉ oḷi keṭāmi aruḷvāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
naḷi - perumi. tuṟiyiṟā eṉṟataṟ kēṟpak kari vāḻalavaṉ eṉpatu peyturikkappaṭṭatu. piṟaḻum oli, piṟaḻutalāl eḻum oli yeṉka. niṉṉalatillā eṉpatu “nāṇu naṭpum illōrt tēriṉ, yāṉala tilliyiv vulakattāṉē” eṉpuḻip pōla, allatil eṉṉum vāypāṭṭiṉkaṇ vantatu.
kuṟiyāvatu, “talimakaṉ tōḻiyi irantu kuṟiyuṟṟup piṉṉiṉṟa viṭattu, avaṉi āṟṟuvikkum cila coṟ colli āṟṟuvittut talimakaṉik kuṟi nayappittavāṟṟāṉ niṉṟa tōḻiyip piṉṉum talimakaṉ etirppaṭum; etirppaṭ ṭāṉi iṉṉaviṭattu vā eṉṟu kuṟippiṉā luṇarttum” iṭam. ivviṭattu ivar iraviṉum pakaliṉum vantu kūṭuvar. “kuṟiyeṉap paṭuvatu iraviṉum pakaliṉum, aṟiyat tōṟṟum āṟṟa teṉpa. 1” īṇṭu āṟṟateṉpatu avaṉ varutaṟkuriya neṟiyiṭam eṉṟaṟku. ivaṟṟuḷ, iravukkuṟi maṉiyakattē orucār eṉṟum, pakaṟkuṟi maṉiyiṉ puṟattē makaḷir ceṉṟu payilum ellikkaṇ uḷḷatōr iṭam eṉṟum kūṟupa.
varivukaṭātalāvatu, varital vēṇṭumeṉa niṉikku māṟu talivaṉi muṭukutal. ituvum kuṟippiṉāl varivu kaṭātalum, veḷippaṭiyāl varivukaṭātalum eṉa iru vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kuṟippāl varivu kaṭāvuvaṉa, “poḻutum āṟuṅ kāppumeṉṟu ivaṟṟiṉ, vaḻuvi ṉākiya kuṟṟam kāṭṭa lum, taṉṉi yaḻitalum  avaṇū ṟañcalum, iraviṉum paka liṉum,  nīvā eṉṟalum, kiḻavōṉ taṉṉi vāral eṉṟalum, naṉmiyum tīmiyum piṟitiṉik kūṟalum, puripaṭa vanta aṉṉavi piṟavum, varital vēṭkip poruḷa eṉpa 2” eṉa āciriyar kūṟuvar. “kāmam mikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷaviyum, kāppuc ciṟi mikka kiyaṟu kiḷaviyum, āṟupārt tuṟṟa accak kiḷaviyum, iraviṉum pakaliṉum nīvaru keṉṟalum, kiḻavōṉ ṟaṉṉi vāra leṉṟalum, taṉṉuṭ kiyā ṟeytiṭu kiḷaviyum, aṉṉa marapiṟ piṟavun tokii, taṉṉi yaḻinta kiḷavi yellām, varital vēṭkip poruḷa eṉpa 3” eṉa iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷ uṭiyārum kūṟiṉar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār. “vēṭki maṟuttuk kiḷantāṅ kurittal, marīiya maruṅkiṉ uritteṉa moḻipa 4” eṉṟatu veḷip paṭiyāl varivu kaṭātal eṉa urippar iḷampūraṇar. ivar karutumāṟu vēṭkimaṟuttal eṉṟatu “vēṭkip poruḷāṟ kūṟutali maṟuttup paṭṭāṅkuk kūṟutal” eṉpatu; “māmali nāṭa varintaṉi koṇmō 5” eṉṟāṟpōla. kaḷavoḻuk katti nīṭṭippiṉ viḷiyum ētam āyntu talimakaṉi virintu varintu koḷḷumāṟu ūkkuvatu aṟivum aṟamum aṉpum uruvākak koṇṭa tōḻikkuk kaṭaṉ. akkaṭaṉi āṟṟu miṭattut taṉatu nili talimakaṉatu taliminiliyi nōkkat tāḻntatākaliṉ, avaṉatu uḷḷam varintu kōṭaṟkuc cellu māṟu veḷippaṭiyāka muṭukutal nērmiyākāmiyiṉ toṭakkattil kuṟippākavum, piṉṉar veḷippaṭiyākavum ceyvaḷ. atu paṟṟiyē varivu kaṭātal iruvakittāyiṟṟu. kuṟippākat tōḻi kūṟuvatit talimakaṉ uṇarvāṉāyiṉum, kāmañ ciṟappatu karutit talimakaṉ varitali nīṭṭippaṉ. nīṭṭittal avaṉatu talimiyiṉ māṇpu eṉa aṟika.
uḷḷuṟiyāl talimakaḷatu āṟṟāmi mikuti kūṟu kiṉṟamiyiṉ nalkumati eṉṟum, nalkiyavaḻi avviruvarum tuyppa niṟkum iṉpattiṉ ciṟappu nōkki, vāḻiyō eṉṟam, uṟṟār uṟātār ākiya irutiṟattārkkum oppa vaḻaṅkum taṇṇaḷiyuṭimi karuti, naḷinīrc cērppa eṉṟum, iṉ ṉoliyāl viḷakkamuṟum toṇṭiyaṟṟē eṉṟami yiṉ, ivaḷatu nutalum taṉ nalloḷiyāl viḷakka muṟutaliṉṟip pacantatu eṉṟum, taṉakkuṟṟa varuttam kūṟit tēṟṟutaṟkuriya tāyarum taṉṉiyarum tōḻiyarum piṟarum uṭiyaḷāyiṉum, avarāl tīrkkappaṭātu niṉṉi iṉṟiyamiyā ḷāyiṉāḷ eṉṟaṟku niṉṉalla tillā ivaḷ eṉṟum, oḷiyiḻantu pacantu tōṉṟutaliṉ ciṟunutal eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. nutal ciṟitāyiṉum, pacantu varutta muṟuvittaliṟ peritāmeṉpatu kuṟippu. “ciṟunutal pacantu peruntōḷ cāay 1” eṉṟār piṟarum.
niṉṉala tillā ivaḷ eṉṟatu aḻivil kūṭṭattu aṟaṉaḷit turittal. ciṟunutal toṇṭi yaṟṟākaliṉ, nalkumati eṉṟatu āṅkuneñcaḻital; variyātu nīṭṭit tavaḻi, ic ciṟu nutalum pacaliyāl oḷikeṭṭup puṟattārkku ivvoḻukkattip pulappaṭukku meṉa añci neñcaḻin turikkiṉṟamiyiṉ, nalkutal varitalātaliṉ, itu varital vēṭkip poruḷatām eṉa aṟika.
alavaṉ tākkutalāl tuṟikkaṇ vāḻum iṟāl mīṉ piṟaḻum eṉṟatu ivaḷ nutal pacattalāl, puṟattārkku iv voḻukkamuṇmi pulaṉām; ākiyavaḻi ivaḷ perunāṇiṉa ḷātaliṉ uyirvāḻāḷ eṉṟavāṟu. ituvē paḻiyavuri kārarkkum karuttātal kāṇka.
	180.	ciṟunaṉi varintaṉi koṇmō perunīr
valivar tanta koḻumīṉ valcip
paṟitapu mutukuru kirukkum
tuṟikeḻu toṇṭi yaṉṉaviva ṇalaṉē.
itu, tāḻttu variyak karutiya talimakaṉit tōḻi neruṅkik kaṭitiṉ variya vēṇṭumeṉac colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
valivar tanta koḻumīṉ valcikkaṇṇē paṟattal keṭṭa mutukuruku iṟukkum eṉṟatu, notumalarkku makaṭ pēca vantiruk kiṉṟa cāṉṟōr uḷar eṉṟavāṟu.
ivipattum colvakiyāl toṭarcci peṟutalē yaṉṟik kiḷavi vakiyāl toṭarcciyuṭiyavāṟum aṟika.
uri :
paratavar kaṭaliṉiṉṟum koṇarnta koḻuviya mīṉākiya uṇaviṉ poruṭṭup paṟattalvali yiḻanta mutirnta kuruku taṅkiyirukkum tuṟi poruntiya toṇṭinakar pōlum ivaḷi mikak kuṟukiya pōtiṉkaṇ varintu ivaḷatu nalatti nukarvāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
ciṟunaṉi - mikak kuṟukiya pōtu “ciṟunaṉi nī tuñci ēṟpiṉum 1” eṉpataṉālum ipporuṇmi tuṇiyappaṭum. tāṉum uṇa vātalaṉṟi ēṉi nel mutaliyaṉa peṟutaṟkum poruḷātaliṉ, mīṉik koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟār. nel mutaliya uṇavup poruḷiṉum mīṉuṇavu uṭalōmpuvārkkuc ciṟantatu eṉpa vākaliṉ, koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟāreṉiṉumām. “iruñcēṟ ṟaḷḷal nāṭpulam pōkiya, koḻumīṉ valcip puṉṟalic ciṟā ar 1” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr vitantōtutal kāṇka. paṟitapu mutukuruku eṉṟatu, paṟattal vali yiḻantu mutumiyāl taḷar vuṟṟa nāriyi. paṟi, paṟattal. “paṟivalan tappiya pital nāri” “paṟitapu nāri 2” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkumāṟu kāṇka.
talimakaṉ tāḻttu variyak karutiṉāṉ eṉpatu uṇarnta tōḻi, uḷḷuṟiyāl notumalar varivu kuṟittu viṭutta cāṉṟōr varaviṉi uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, veḷippaṭiyāl, ciṟitu tāḻkkiṉum ētam peritām eṉṟaṟkuc ciṟunaṉi eṉṟum, kāvalarumiyum iṟceṟippum mikkiruttaliṉ koṇṭutalik kaḻital mutaliya muyaṟcikaḷ payaṉpaṭā eṉṟaṟku, varintaṉi koṇmō eṉṟum, kāmañ ciṟattaṟporuṭṭu virinta variviṉik karutiṟṟili yeṉiṉum, tāḻttavaḻi ivaḷ nalam keṭutal orutali yeṉa yāppuṟuttaṟku, varintaṉi eṉṟu oḻiyātu koṇmō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. itu “puripaṭa  vanta3” tāyiṉum varitaṟ poruṭṭātaliṉ amiyum eṉa vuṇarka.
paratavar koṇarnta koḻumīṉ valciyiṉ poruṭṭup paṟitapu nāri taṅkiyirukkumāṟu pōla, talimakaḷ vikiya emmaṉiyiṭattu vēṟṟuvarivu kuṟittuc cāṉṟōr vantuḷar eṉpatu uḷḷuṟi. paḻiya urikārarkkum ituvē karut tātal kāṇka.
mutumi kaṇṭu iraṅkik koḻumīṉ valciyiṉi valivar eṟiyār eṉpataṉi aṟintuvittum. kuruku avvalcip poruṭṭu iruntatāka, nī varital vēṇṭi muyalāmi eṉṉi eṉa ippāṭṭiṉ poruṭpuṟattē iṟicci tōṉṟumāṟu kāṇka.
ciṟunaṇi yeṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu. āyiṉum, “niṉ aṇi māṇ ciṟupuṟam kāṇkam ciṟunaṉi, ēkeṉa ēkal nāṇi 4” eṉṟum, “pirintōr uḷḷupu niṉital, ciṟunaṉi āṉṟikam eṉṟi tōḻi 5” eṉṟum, “peritamarn tiyinta kēṇmi ciṟunaṉi, ariya pōlak kāṇkuveṉ 1” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr payila vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, ciṟunaṉi eṉṟa pāṭamē cīriteṉa aṟika.
19. neytaṟpattu
ippattiṉkaṇ varum pāṭṭuttōṟum neytalākiya karupporuḷ ciṟantu niṟṟaliṉ, iḵtu ippeyariṉip peṟuva tāyiṟṟu.
neytal, nīrppū vakiyuḷ oṉṟu. perumpālum itu kaṭaṟkarippakutiyil uḷḷa nīrnilikaḷil mikku iruppatāl, annilappakuti itaṉāl neytal eṉṟa peyar eytiṟṟu. kaṭalum kaṭalcārnta pakutiyum “perumaṇal ulakam 2” eṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭiṉum, cāṉṟōr palarum perumaṇal ulakam eṉṉātu neytaliyē ciṟappāka eṭuttu neytal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyiruppa toṉṟē itaṉ nalattukkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum.
inneytal, tāmari āmpal mutaliyavaṟṟip pōlak koṭiyum iliyum uṭiyatu. perumpālum itu kaḻik karikaḷil perukak kāṇappaṭiṉum, mulli marutam kuṟiñci mutaliya pakutikaḷilum nīrnili maruṅkil kāṇappaṭuvatu uṇṭu. itaṉ ili, yāṉikkaṉṟiṉ kātupōlum vaṭiviṉiyuṭiya tātal kaṇṭa cāṉṟōr, “irumpiṭi...... neytaṟ pācaṭi puriyum añcevip, pitalaṅ kuḻavi taḻīi.... vikum 3” eṉṟaṉar. “neytalmukiḻt tuṇiyavām kuṭumi 4” eṉṟaliṉ, itaṉ arumpu paṇṭi paḷḷic ciṟuvariṉ kuṭumipōl irukku meṉat terikiṟatu. neytaṟpūviṉ itaḻ mēṟpuṟattu aṭippakuti muṟṟum kaṟuttu makaḷir kaṇpōl irukkum. “nīṉaṟu neytaliṟ polinta uṇkaṇ 5” eṉṟum, “kaṇṇē roppiṉ kamaḻnaṟu neytal 6” eṉṟum aṟiñar urippatu kāṇka. itaṉ niṟatti viyanta paṇṭic cāṉṟōr, “maṇikkalat taṉṉa māyitaḻ neytal 7” eṉṟum, “maṇiniṟa neytal 8” eṉṟum pārāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. itaṉkoṭi nīṇṭu āmpaṟṟaṇṭu pōla uḷḷē puḻiyuṭiyatu.
neytaliṉ niṟamum nīlamalariṉ niṟamum okkumāyiṉum, nīlattiṉ itaḻ muḻutum nīlaniṟaṅ koṇṭiruppatu vēṟupāṭu. aṉṟiyum nīlam kāliyil malarvatu; neytal vikaṟiyil malarvatu; “vikaṟi malarum neytal 1” eṉa innūlē kūṟuvatu kāṇka. tāmari pōla māliyil itaḻkuvivatum inney taṟku  iyalpu. neytaṟpūviṉ itaḻ, vaṭivil vēlpōla irukkum. neytaliṉ pūviyē taṉippaṭa vittut toṭut taṇivatu paṇṭiyōr vaḻakkaṉṟu; itaṉuṭaṉ vēṟu pala niṟamuṭiya pūkkaḷiyum taḻikaḷiyum viravi uṭipōlat toṭuttaṇital iḷamakaḷir ceyal vakiyākum. makaḷir ivaṟṟik kūntaṟ piṉṉaliṟ cūṭikkoḷvatum, āṭavar cerunti mutaliya malarkaḷōṭu cērat toṭuttut tārāka aṇivatum vaḻakkam. viḷiyāṭṭu makaḷir tām iḻikkum maṇaṟciṟuvīṭṭil inneytaṟ pūkkaḷic corintu kōlañ ceytu iṉpuṟuvar. itaṉi maturi marutaṉ iḷaṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum cāṉṟōr, “oṇṇutal makaḷir ōṅkukaḻik kuṟṟa, kaṇṇē roppiṉ kamaḻnaṟu neytal, akalvaric ciṟumaṉi aṇiyum tuṟiva 2” eṉṟu pukaḻntōtukiṉṟār. ivvāṟu pulavar palarum pārāṭṭippāṭum pukaḻpeṟṟa inneytaliṉ ciṟappu, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil parantu kiṭattali nōkkātu, nīlōṟpalam eṉa vaṭavar kūṟum nīlamalariyum itaṉiyum oṉṟākac cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. “pallitaḻ nīlamoṭu neytal nikarkkum 3” eṉpataṉik kaṇṭēṉum avarkaḷ iraṇṭum vēṟuvēṟu vakiyiṉa eṉṟu teḷivārkaḷāka.
	181.	neyta luṇkaṇ nēriṟip paṇittōḷ
poyta lāṭiya poyyā makaḷir
kuppi veṇmaṇaṟ kuravi niṟūun
tuṟikeḻu koṇkaṉ nalkiṉ
uṟiviṉi tammaviv vaḻuṅka lūrē.
iḵtu ikkaḷavoḻukkam neṭitu celliṉ,  ivvūrkkaṇ alar piṟakkumeṉṟu añciyirunta talivi, “talimakaṉ varintu koḷḷat tuṇintāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻikkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
itu ciṟippuṟam. makaḷir kuppi veṇmaṇaṟ kuravi niṟūum tuṟikeḻu koṇkaṉ eṉṟatu, makaḷir tam kātalaroṭu āṭum ivvūriṭattut taṉittal aritu eṉpatām.
uri :
neytalmalar pōlum miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum nuṇ ṇiya iṟi poruntiya vaḷiyaṇinta parutta tōḷiyu muṭiyarāyp poytal āṭiya iḷamakaḷir kuvinta veṇ maṇaṟkaṇ kuravi ayarum tuṟi poruntiya koṇkaṉ nammi variyumukattāl taṇṇaḷipurivā ṉāyiṉ, ala rākiya āravāratti yuṭiya ivvūr uṟitaṟku iṉitākum kāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
neytal - makaḷir kaṇṇiṟku uvamiyātal mēlē kūṟiṉām. poytal, iḷañciṟār peritum payiṉṟu viḷi yāṭum viḷiyāṭṭuvaki. “polantoṭip putalvaṉum poytal kaṟṟaṉaṉ 1” “poytal makaḷir kaṇkāṇa ikuttantu 2” “mīṉ muḷ ḷaṉṉa veṇkāṉ māmalar, poytal makaḷir viḻavaṇik kūṭṭum” “poyta lāṭip polikeṉa vantu 3” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar. “miyāṭa lāṭu maḻapulavar māṟeḻuntu, poyyāṭa lāṭum puṇarppi ṉavar 4” eṉpataṟkum, “poytal makaṉiyāyp piṟarmaṉip pāṭinī, eytiya palark kītta payam payak kiṟpatō 5” eṉpataṟkum muṟiyē parimēlaḻa karum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum uritta urikaḷāl itu kāmak kuṟip pillāta nōṉpākiya viḷiyāṭṭeṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. ataṉāl poytalāṭum iḷiya makaḷir maṟi pulappaṭāmal niṟuttum niṟiyiṉṟi, aṟintataṉi aṟintāṅ kurikkum vāymiyuṭimi paṟṟip poyyāmakaḷir eṉṟār eṉa vuṇarka. ivarkaḷi “maṟiyeṉal aṟiyā māyamil āyam1” eṉṟār piṟarum. kuravi - maṅkiyar eḻuvar kikōttāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu vaki. “kuravi yeṉpatu eḻuvar maṅkiyar, cennili maṇṭilak kaṭakak kikōttu, annilik koppa niṉ ṟāṭa lākum 2” eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. maṇaliṉkaṭ kuravi yayartal, “koṇṭa  liṭumaṇaṟ kuravi muṉiyiṉ 3”  eṉpataṉāl valiyuṟum. iṉitu eṉpuḻi ākkam vikārattāl tokkatu. itaṟku ētu, koṇkaṉ nalkiṉ eṉpatu. “ākkam tāṉē kāraṇa mutaṟṟē” “āvayiṟ kuṟippē ākkamoṭu varumē 4” eṉpaṉa viti. ākkam ciṟupāṉmi vārāmiyum uṇṭeṉpar cēṉāvariyar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, vārāvaḻit tokka teṉavē paṭum eṉpar. ivaṟṟuḷ nallatu koḷka. aḻuṅkal, ōci; īṇṭu, alar mēṟṟu. amma: uriyaci. 
kaṇṭatum kātal koṇṭu kāmam kiyikappak kūṭic ciṉṉāḷ kaḻintatum kinnekiḻttoḻiyum kantaruvam pōlātu, kaṇṭatum kāṭci vāyilākak karuttiṭi muḷikkum kātal, iṭantalippāṭu pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭaṅ kaḷāl vaḷarntu peruki iṭiyīṭukaḷālum, oruvaḻittaṇappu variporuṭ pirivu mutaliyavaṟṟālum muṟuki mutirntu, oruvar oruvari yiṉṟi uyirvāḻta lamiyāta niliyi eytuvippatu kaḷavoḻukka mākiya kātalaṟattiṉ iyalpu. annilimi eytuṅkāṟum tam talimippaṇpāl kaṭal pōṟ perukum kātal veḷḷattik kikaṭavātavāṟu niṟuttu neṟippaṭuttu māṟṟāl talimakkaḷ mēṉmi eytukiṉṟaṉar. kīḻmakka ḷallarākaliṉ, kaḷavukkālattē tammil tām tamittuk kūṭumiṭattu meyyuṟu puṇarcci eytuva tallatu makap pēṟṟukkuriya puṇarcci eytuvatilar. oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāta kāmacciṟappu eytiyatum, cāṉṟōr vāyilā ṉātal uṭaṉpōkkiṉālātal varintu koḷvar. kāmam ciṟappatu kuṟittut talimakaṉ variviṉkaṇ viriyātu kaḷavē virumpi oḻukiṉamiyiṉ nalkāṉāyiṉaṉ eṉa varunti yirun tāṭkut tōḻi avaṉ varitalit tuṇintāṉ eṉpati uṇarntu kūṟutaliṉ koṇkaṉ nalkiṉ eṉṟum, varunti yirukkumpoḻutellām alar eḻumō eṉa añciyiruntā ḷākaliṉ ivvaḻuṅkalūr eṉṟum, tōḻi kūṟṟāl avvaccam nīṅki eḻunta alar iṉṉum ettuṇi eḻiṉum eḻuka eṉat tuṇintaṉaḷ eṉpatu tōṉṟa uṟivu iṉitu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. uṟivu iṉitākāmik kuriya ētuviṉi aḻuṅkalūr eṉṟatu uṭkoṇṭu niṟṟal kāṇka.
poytal makaḷir ayarum kuravikkaṇ kāmakkuṟip piṉmiyiṉ akkuṟippiṉi yuṭiya kātalar kūṭiyāṭutal ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ paḻiyavuri kuṟikkum poruḷ ciṟakkavilli. poytalāṭiya makaḷir piṉṉum maṇaṟkaṇ kuraviyāṭum tuṟiva ṉākaliṉ, kaḷaviṉ kaṭ kūṭi namakkut taliyaḷiceyta avaṉ piṉpu varivuvaḻik kūṭutali virumpi ṉāṉ eṉṟāḷ eṉka.
	182.	neytal naṟumalar ceruntiyoṭu viriik
kipuṉi naṟuntār kamaḻu mārpaṉ
aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavu ḷallaṉ
peruntuṟik kaṇṭiva ḷaṇaṅki yōṉē.
itu talimakaḷ melivu kaṇṭu teyvattā lāyiṟṟu eṉat tamar niṉintuḻit tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.
paḻiya uri :
neytalum ceruntiyum viravik kūṟiyatu, avaṉatu celvac ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟu. peruntuṟik kaṇṭu ivaḷ aṇaṅkiyōṉ eṉṟatu, puṉaltaru puṇarcci.
uri :
periya tuṟikkaṇṇē ivaḷik kaṇṭu varuttam uṟuvittavaṉ taṭuttaṟkariya valiyiṉiyuṭiya kaṭavuḷallaṉ; neytaliṉ maṇa mikka malaric cerunti malaroṭu viravi aḻakuṟat toṭukkappaṭṭa naṟiya māliyiṉ maṇam kamaḻum mārpiṉiyuṭiya makaṉāvāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
neytal malar nīla niṟattiṉiyum, ceruntimalar poṉ ṉiṟattiṉiyum uṭimiyāl, maṇiyum poṉṉum viraviyatu pōl aḻakuṟutaliṉ, neytal naṟumalar cerunti yoṭu viriik kipuṉi naṟuntār eṉṟār. neytal nīlaniṟa muṭimi “nīṉiṟa neytal niriyitaḻ porunta 1” eṉṟum, “maṇiniṟa neytal 2” eṉṟum varuvaṉavaṟṟālum, cerunti. poṉṉiṟattatātal, “poṉṉaṭarn taṉṉa oḷḷiṇarc  cerunti 3” eṉa varutalālum uṇarappaṭum. “teyvañ cuṭṭiya peyarnilik kiḷavi, uyartiṇi maruṅkiṟ pālpirin ticikkum 4” eṉṟamiyiṉ, aṇaṅkiyōṉ eṉa uyartiṇi vāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟiṉār. “āvō vākum 5” eṉṟataṉāl, aṇaṅkiyōṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. ceruntiyoṭu eṉpuḻi oruviṉiyoṭu uyarpiṉ vaḻivantatu. uyarpu, poṉṉeṉac ciṟantu polital. teyvameṉat tamar karutiyataṉi maṟuttu makaṉ eṉat tuṇiyak kūṟutaliṉ kaṭavuḷallaṉ eṉṟār. “taṉmi cuṭṭalu muritteṉa moḻipa, aṉmik kiḷavi vēṟiṭat tāṉ 6” eṉpatu viti.
nīrttuṟikkaṇ aṇaṅku uṟiyum eṉṉum ulakuriyāl, tam makaḷ nīrāṭiyaviṭattu aṇaṅkuṟṟāḷ ātal vēṇṭum eṉat talimakaḷ mēṉi melivu kaṇṭu tamar niṉintu kūṟiya taṉik koṇṭukūṟutaliṉ, perun tuṟikkaṇṭu ivaḷ aṇaṅkiyōṉ eṉṟum, peruntuṟikkaṇ veṇmaṇaṟ parappil yām viḷiyāṭi niṉṟēmāka, neytaṟ pūk koṇṭu nummakaḷ taṉ pāvik kaṇiyat tantu nīṅkiṉā ṉōr naṟuntār kamaḻum mārpaṉ uḷaṉ; iḵtu aṟivaṟiyāk kālattu nikaḻiṉum, aṟivataṟiyum ikkālattuk “koṇṭārk kuriyar peṇṭir eṉpatum, uṟṟārk kuriyar poṟṟoṭi makaḷir eṉpatum” niṉintu, avaṉi vaḻipaṭātoḻital kulattukku vaṭuvā meṉak karuti vēṟupaṭṭāḷāka, ataṉi yaṟiyātu, perun tuṟik kaṭavuḷāl āyiṟṟeṉap piḻipaṭak karutiṉir eṉpāḷ, aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavuḷallaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ kaṭavuḷ eṉpataṉi maṟuttu makkaḷiṉattu makaṉ eṉṟaṟku ētuk kūṟuvāḷ, kaṭavu ḷāyiṉ, kiceyyappaṭā iyaṟki vaṉappum, iyaṟki maṇamum uṭiyaṉām; avaṉ aṉṉa ṉallaṉ eṉpāḷ, kipuṉi naṟuntār kamaḻum mārpaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
poṉṉeṉac ciṟantu neytaliṉum mikkup poliyum ceruntiyiṉi uyarpiṉ vaḻittāya oruviṉi yoṭuk koṭuttup pirittāṅku, muṟkūṟātu piṟkūṟiyataṉāl, avaṉ tantatu neytaṟpū eṉa aṟika. eṉavē, itaṉāl pūttaru puṇarcci yākiya ētīṭu kūṟiyavāṟum, mārpaṉ eṉac ciṟappittamiyāl, avaṉ mārpu kūṭiyatākiya uṇmi ceppiyavāṟumām. palarāṭu tuṟikkaṇ ivaḷoruttiyi varintu aṇaṅkiṟṟu eṉṟal nirampātu eṉṟaṟkup peruntuṟi eṉṟum, ivaḷ uṟṟa melivu tīrttaṟkut tammāl iyaṉṟaṉa ellām muyaṉṟum tīrāmi kaṇṭu mayaṅkiṉar eṉṟaṟku, aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum, “kipuṉin tiyaṟṟāk kaviṉpeṟu vaṉap 1” puṭimi kaṭavuṭku iyalpākaliṉ, aṉṉa ṉallāta talimakaṉi, kipuṉi naṟuntār kamaḻum mārpaṉ eṉṟum eṭuttuk kūṟiṉāḷ. iḵtu oruvakiyāl ēttalumām. ākavē, ivvaṟattoṭunili ētīṭum, uṇmi ceppalum, ēttalum viravi niṉṟavāṟu eṉa uṇarka.
iṉi, “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 2” eṉṟa cūttirattuk kaḷam peṟak kāṭṭiṉum eṉpuḻi, itaṉik kāṭṭi itu vēlaṉi muṉṉiliyākak kūṟiya teṉpar iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum. veṟiyāṭ ṭayarum vevvāy vēlaṉi muṉṉili yākak kūṟiyatāyiṉ, avaṉ murukeṉ ṟātal, kaḻaṅkiṟ ṟeḷika eṉṟātal kūṟuta laṉṟi, peruntuṟik kaṭavuḷ eṉap piṟaḻntu kūṟa liṉmiyiṉ, atu poruḷaṉṟu eṉpar.
	183.	kaṇaṅko ḷaruvik kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ
kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ nalvaya lūraṉ
taṇkaṭaṟ cērppaṉ pirinteṉap paṇṭiyiṟ
kaṭumpakal varuti kiyaṟu māli
koṭuṅkaḻi neytaluṅ kūmpak
kāli variṉum kaḷiñarō vilarē.
itu, variviṭi vittup pirintuḻi āṟṟāḷākiya talimakaḷ mālikkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
paṇṭiyiṟ kaṭumpakal varuti eṉṟatu, paṇṭu varum kālat tilē vantāṟ pōlap piṟarkkut tōṉṟak kaṭumpakaṟ kaṇṇē vārāniṉṟāy eṉṟavāṟu.
uri :
kiyaṟuvikkum mālippoḻutē, kūṭṭamāṉa aruvikaḷ pāyum kāṉam poruntiya nāṭaṉum ciṟiya kuṉṟu kaḷiyuṭiya nāṭaṉum, nalla vayalkaḷ poruntiya ūraṉum, taṇṇiya kaṭaṉilattiyuṭiya cērppaṉu mākiya talimakaṉ piritalāl neṟipiṟaḻtal amiyu māyiṉum, paṇṭē pōl iṉṟum kaṭumpakaṟkaṇṇē vārāniṉṟāy; aḵtu oḻintu vaḷinta kaḻikkaṭ pūtta neytal itaḻ kuviyak kāli variṉum varuka; variṉ niṉṉi vilakkuvār ilar eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ, kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, nalvayalūraṉ, kaṭaṟ cērppaṉ eṉṟatu, mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉṉum nāṉilavaḷamu muṭiyaṉ eṉṟaṟku. “nāṭa ṉeṉkō ūra ṉeṉkō, pāṭimiḻ paṉikkaṭaṟ cērppa ṉeṉkō 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. vaḷamillāta nilamākaliṉ, pāliyik kūṟārāyiṉār. pirinteṉa eṉṟatu, ceyteṉa veṉṉum viṉiyeccam; itu, “muṭikkuñ collāṉ uṇarttappaṭum toḻiṟkut taṉ mutaṉilittoḻil kāraṇam eṉpatupaṭavarum 2” eṉpa vākaliṉ, piritalāl eṉṟu urikkappaṭṭatu. “mellam pulampaṉ pirinteṉap, pallitaḻu ṇkaṇ pāṭol lāvē 3” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. veyiliṉ vemmi tikaḻum kālamākaliṉ, kaṭumpakal eṉṟār. kiyaṟumāli kiyaṟu vikkum māli; piṟaviṉip poruṭṭu; “kiyaṟṟa kaṅkulāṉ4” eṉṟāṟ pōla. ōkāram: pirinili ummi, mutalatu ici niṟiyum, piṉṉatu etirmaṟiyu mām. muṉṉatic ciṟappu eṉiṉumām, neytaṟku atu ciṟantamiyiṉ. neytaṟkuriyatu aṉmiyiṉ, māli kūṟiyatu tiṇimayakkam. “tiṇimayak kuṟutalum kaṭinili yilavē 1” eṉpatu viti.
taṉiyavari muṉivu ceyyum iyalpiṟ ṟākaliṉ, kiyaṟu māli eṉṟum, vikaṟikkaṇ malarum neytal māliyiṟ kūmputal iyalpātal paṟṟik koṭuṅkaḻi neytaluṅ kūmpa eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “vāliḻi makaḷir uyirpoti yaviḻkkum, kāli yāva taṟiyār, māli yeṉmaṉār mayaṅki yōrē 2” eṉṟum, “neytal kūmpa niḻalkuṇak koḻukak, kalcēr maṇṭilam 3” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟumā ṟaṟika.
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ, tāṉ avaṉit taṉittuk kūṭiyatu niṉintu kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ eṉṟum, kūṭiyōṉ pirintuḻit tāṉ pirivāṟṟi avaṉ col tēṟiyiruntami niṉintu kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ eṉṟum, kūṭṭa meytumiṭat tellām “muliyoṭu muṉintāṉ pōlavum, tōḷoṭu tuvaṇṭāṉ pōlavum” iṉpam ciṟakkumāṟu oḻukiya tiṟam niṉintu nalvaya lūraṉ eṉṟum, ivvāṟu oḻukiyōṉatu pirivāṟṟātu tāṉ alamarum vaṇṇam pirintataṉāl kalaṅki melintamiyiṉ, taṇkaṭaṟ cērppaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. kūṟavē, aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi iṉpavoḻukkam ciṟakka oḻukiyōṉ itupōtu atu kaḻintu tuṉpam ciṟakka oḻukukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu kūṟiṉāḷām.
iṉi, māliyē, paṇṭellām vantatu pōla niṉmuṟi piḻiyātu iṉṟum vantaṉi; niṉṉip pōl avarum paṇṭellām eṉakku iṉpamē ciṟakkumāṟu oḻukiyatu pōla muṟi piḻiyātu iṉṟu vantu oḻukuvārāyiṉ aḵtu amiyum. atu māṟi, tuṉpam ciṟakka oḻukukiṉṟār eṉṟaṟku, kaṭumpakal varuti eṉṟum, neṟipiḻiyārē neṟivaḻīiyiṉāri an neṟikkaṇ niṟuttum nīrmiyarāvar eṉṉum muṟimiyāl, talimakaṉ taṉ pirivāl neṟipiḻittāṉ eṉpāḷ, pirinteṉa eṉṟam, annīrmi piḻittamiyiṉ avar iṉi neṟivaḻu viṉum niṉṉi niṟuttum kaṭappāṭilar eṉpāḷ, kāli variṉum kaḷiñarō ilar eṉṟāḷ. iṉik kāli māli kaḷil arumpi malarum kāmanōyit tīrkkum kaṭappāṭuṭi yōṉ, avaṟṟip pōl oruniliyē cemmiyaṉāyiṉ tīrttal takum; paṇṭellām piriyā toḻuki, iṉṟu pirin tuṟivōṉ cemminili vaḻīiyiṉa miyiṉ, niṉṉāl eytum ivvaruttam kāliyē tōṉṟiṉum vilakkuvārilli eṉṟāḷ eṉṟumām.
kāliyil malartaṟ kuriya neytal malarātu kūmpumāṟu kāli variṉum eṉṟatu, taṉ varavu piḻittalēyaṉṟi, taṉ varavu kaṇṭu payappā ṭeytum piṟavum piḻikkumāṟu ceytal aṟaṉaṉ ṟeṉpatu muṭittaṟku. variṉum eṉpuḻi ummi, vārāmi muṭikku mukattāl avaḷatu āṟṟāmiyiṉ ciṟappuṇara niṉṟatu. itu tuṉpattup pulampal.
“variviṭi vitta kālattu varuntiṉum, variyā nāḷiṭi vantōṉ muṭṭiṉum, uriyeṉat tōḻikku urittaṟ kaṇṇum, tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamu muḷavē 1” eṉṟa viṭattu, “variviṭi vitta kālattu varuntiṉum” eṉpuḻit “tokutta moḻiyiṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal” eṉṟa tantira vuttiyāl itu talivi tāṉē kūṟum kūṟṟā yamintatu.
iṉik “kaṭumpakal varuntik kiyaṟu mā, māli neytalum kūmpak, kāli variṉum kaḷiñarō vilarē” eṉṟa pāṭat tukkuk kaṭumpakal muṟṟum varuntic ceyalaṟṟu iṉiyumpaṭi neytal kūmpa, mālippoḻutu kāli varuvatāyiṉum, emmit tuyariṉiṉṟum vilakkuvārō ilar eṉpatu poru ḷākak koḷka. koḷḷiṉ, itaṉik kāṭṭi, “paruva varaviṉ kaṇ mālippoḻutu kaṇṭu āṟṟāḷāya talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu; neytaṟkaṇ māli vantatu 2” eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu poruntumeṉa uṇarka.
	184.	neyta liruṅkaḻi neytal nīkki
mīṉuṇ kurukiṉaṅ kāṉa lalkum
kaṭalaṇin taṉṟava rūrē
kaṭaliṉum peritemak kavaruṭi naṭpē.
itu, vāyil vēṇṭi vantār, talimakaṉ aṉpuṭimi kūṟiyavaḻi vāyiṉ maṟukkum talimakaḷ colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
neytal nīkki mīṉuṇ kurukiṉam kāṉalil alkum eṉṟatu, potumakaḷiriṭattu ūṭal nīkki iṉpam nukarntu āṇṭut taṅkuvāṉ eṉpatām.
uri :
neytal nilattiluḷḷa periya kaḻiyiṟ pūtta neytali nīkki, ataṉ arukil mēyum mīṉiyuṇṇum kuruku taṉ iṉattōṭu kūṭik kāṉaliṉkaṇ taṅkum kaṭalāl aḻaku peṟṟatu avaruṭiya ūr; avaratu naṭpu emakku ak kaṭaliṉum peritā yiruntatu kāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
neytal, mutaliṟ kūṟum ciṉiyaṟi kiḷavi yāya ākupeyar. mīṉuṇkuruku mīṉkuttip paṟavi. iṉam - kūṭṭam kaṭal eṉpuḻi, mūṉṟaṉurupu tokkatu. cuṭṭum ākkamum varuvikkap paṭṭaṉa. “kaṟimiṭa ṟaṇiyalum aṇintaṉṟu 1” eṉṟār piṟarum.
uḷḷuṟiyāl talimakaṉatu puṟattoḻukkam poṟātu vāyil maṟukkum talimakaḷ, “kātalar kātal tāṉum kaṭaliṉum peritē 2” eṉṟa vāyilkaḷiṉ kūṟṟiṉit taḻīi mēṟkoṇṭu, an naṭpuk kaṭaliṉum peritātaṟku avarūr kaṭalaṇin tirukkum cārpuṭimiyē yaṉṟi empāṟ koṇṭa aṉpuṭimi kāraṇam aṉṟeṉpāḷ, kaṭalaṇin taṉṟavar ūrē eṉṟum, kaṭal avar ūriṟ peritāyiṉum ūrkkup payaṉpaṭātu aṇimāttiri yāyatu pōl avaratu naṭpu emakkuppayap pāṭiṉṟi yām mēṉi vēṟupaṭumāṟu ceyta teṉpāḷ, kaṭa liṉum peritu eṉa uṟaḻntu kūṟiṉāḷ. ākavē, aṇin taṉṟu peritu eṉpaṉa payappāṭiṉmiyum aṉpiṉmiyum tōṉṟa niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. iṉi iṟappap peritāyaviṭattu oruvar pāṅkil nillātu ellārkkum parantu niṟkum paṇpu peṟu taliṉ, avar naṭpu empāl akappaṭṭu nillātu, parattiyar palarkkum parantu payaṉpaṭuvatāyiṟ ṟeṉṟu mām.
ākavē, avaṉuṭiya ūr kaṭalaṇinta tākaliṉ, ataṉ kaṇ vāḻvārāya parattiyar, kaḻippūk kuṟṟum kāṉal alkik kuravi yayarntum, avaṉoṭu kūṭi makiḻvārāka, yām kaṭaliṉum peritāya naṭpuṭiyēm eṉa vāyilkaḷāṟ kūṟap paṭum kūṟṟumāttiriyē peṟṟē mallatu piṟitilli; iḵ taṉṟō avaṉuṭiya aṉpuṭimi yiruntavāṟu eṉa avaṉatu aṉpiṉmi cuṭṭi veḷippaṭiyāka vāyil maṟuttavāṟu uṇarka. iḵtu aḻivil kūṭṭattu avaṉ pirivāṟṟāmi.
neytali nīkki mīṉuṇṭa kuruku taṉ iṉattōṭu kūṭik kāṉaṟkaṇ taṅkum eṉṟatu, taṉ maṉiyiṉ nīṅkip parattiyar cērikkaṇ iṉpanukarnta talimakaṉ, avar kūṭṭattil tōyntu āṇṭē taṅkukiṉṟā ṉātaliṉ avaṉporuṭṭu vāyil vēṇṭuvatu eṉṉi eṉak kuṟippāy vāyil maṟutta vāṟu.
mīṉuṇ kuruku iḷaṅkāṉal alkum eṉpatu pāṭa māyiṉ, mīṉuṇṭa kuruku iḷiya maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta kāṉaṟ cōlikkaṇ taṅkum eṉa urittu, kāṉalil uḷḷa marañ ceṭikaḷiṉ iḷami kāṉaṟ kēṟṟappaṭṭatāka urikka.
	185.	alaṅkitaḻ neytaṟ koṟki muṉṟuṟi
ilaṅkumut tuṟikku meyiṟukeḻu tuvarvāy
arampō ḻavvaḷik kuṟumakaḷ
narampārt taṉṉa tīṅkiḷa viyaḷē.
iḵtu āyamakaḷiruḷ niṉṉāl nayakkappaṭṭāḷ yāvaḷ eṉa viṉaviya tōḻikkut talimakaṉ colliyatu.
uri :
acikiṉṟa itaḻkaḷ poruntiya neytal niṟinta koṟkinakariṉ muṉṟuṟikkaṇ peṟappaṭum viḷaṅkukiṉṟa muttup pōlum paṟkaḷ poruntiya civanta vāyiṉiyum, arattāṟ pōḻappaṭṭa aḻakiya vaḷiyiṉiyumuṭiya iḷiyavaḷ yāḻici pōlum tīviya coṟkaḷi yuṭiya ḷāvāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
alaṅkitaḻ, acikiṉṟa itaḻ. “alaṅkukulik kāntaḷ 1” eṉṟum, “alaṅkuciṉi 2”eṉṟum varutal kāṇka. koṟki, taṇṇāṉ poruni (tāmpiraparaṇi) kaṭaloṭu kalakku miṭattē irunta nakaram; ippōtu ciṟṟūrāy uḷatu. toṇṭi nāṭṭukkup pavattiriyum, cōḻanāṭṭukkup pūmpukārum, cēranāṭṭukku muciṟiyum pōlap pāṇṭi nāṭṭukkuk koṟki ciṟanta kaṭaṟkari nakaramāyc caṅkakālattē viḷakkamuṟ ṟiruntatu. piḷiṉi mutaliya mēṉāṭṭu yavaṉarkaḷiṉ kuṟippukkaḷil innakaramum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kalveṭṭuk kaḷum pōtiya aḷavu kiṭippa taṟku muṉ nam nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu eḻutiya āṅkilappulavar kēṭpōr nakikkum muṟiyil ceytikaḷ cila kuṟittuḷḷaṉar: pāṇṭināṭṭu vēntarkaḷ toṭakkattil koṟkiyiyē talinakarākak koṇṭiruntaṉa reṉṟum, piṉpē maturi avarkaṭkut talinakarāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum avarkaḷ kuṟittuḷḷaṉar3.  nalla kālamāka itu pōṉṟa ceytikaḷ pala āṅkilattilēyē iruntupōyiṉa. tamiḻil eḻutiyiruntāl iḷañciṟuvar palarum akkūṟṟi eḷḷi nakiyāṭiyiruppar. caṅka ilakkiya kālattēyē koṟkiyum maturiyum pāṇṭi nāṭṭukkut talinakaraṅkaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉpati, “pēricik koṟkip porunaṉ veṉvēl, kaṭumpakaṭṭu yāṉi neṭuntērc ceḻiyaṉ, malipuri neṭu nakark kūṭal 4” eṉavarum akappāṭṭē kūṟukiṉṟatu. iḵtu avar kaṭkut teriyāmiyāl piṉ vanta aṟiñar avarkaḷ kūṟṟi maṟuppārāyiṉar 5.
caṅkakālap pāṇṭiyarkkuppiṉ pāṇṭi nāṭu kaḷappira rāṭciyil cīraḻintu iṭikkālap pāṇṭiyarāṭciyil tali tūkkiṟṟu. ivaruḷ muṉṉōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭuṅkō 6 . ivaṉ kuṭanāṭṭu vēntar kuṭiyil tōṉṟiya kaṭuṅkōkkaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉaṉ. ataṉāl koṟkiyic cūḻntanāṭu kuṭanāṭu eṉṟum, kaṭuṅkōmaṇṭalam eṉṟum peyar peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. appakutiyiluḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ kaṭuṅkō maṇṭalamāṉa kuṭanāṭṭuk koṟki 1 eṉṟum, kirēkkar kuṟippukkaḷ “muttup paṭu peruntuṟi 2” eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. piṉvanta cōḻar kālak kalveṭṭukkaḷ, koṟkiyi irācarācap pāṇṭi maṇṭa lattuk kuṭanāṭṭuk koṟki 3 eṉṟum, uttama cōḻa vaḷa nāṭṭuk kuṭanāṭṭuk koṟkiyāṉa maturāntaka nallūr 4  eṉṟum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukkaḷāl koṟki nakaramē orukāl maturāntaka nallūr eṉṟum, piṟitorukāl maturōtaya nallūr 5 eṉṟum peyarpeṟṟami terikiṟatu.
koṟkinakaram, caṅka kālattē muttukkup peyar ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. “maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum, koṟkiyam peruntuṟi muttu 6” eṉavum, “viṉinavil yāṉi viṟaṟpōrp pāṇṭiyar, pukaḻmali ciṟappiṉ koṟki muṉṟuṟi, avirkatir muttamoṭu valampuri corintu 7” eṉavum “īṇṭunīr, muttuppaṭu parappiṉ koṟki muṉṟuṟi 8”  eṉavum cāṉṟōr pārāṭṭik kūṟuvar. mēṉāṭṭu yavaṉar kuṟippiṉum icceyti ciṟappākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.9 kaṭalil muttuk kuḷippōrkkuc cuṟāmīṉkaḷ10 peruntīṅku ceytoḻukiṉamiyiṉ, avaṟṟi vilakkik kaṭalaḻuvam pukuntu valampuri muttuk koṇarvatu koṟki nakarattē pērāra vāramāka irukkum eṉpārāy, “ilaṅkirum parappiṉ eṟicuṟā nīkki, valampuri mūḻkiya vāṉṟimiṟ paratavar, olitalip paṇilam ārppak kalleṉak, kalikeḻu koṟki etirkoḷa iḻi tarum 11” eṉṟu cēntaṉ kaṇṇaṉār ciṟappitturikkiṉṟār. ivvāṟu irupatu irupattumūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉē viḷaṅkiya muttuk kuḷikkum toḻil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nūṟ ṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ nilaviya nāyakkamaṉṉar āṭcivariyil ciṟanta aḷavil iruntirukkiṟatu. it toḻilāl pāṇṭināṭ ṭaracukku nalla varuvāyum iruntatu. muttukkuḷippārkku vitikkappaṭṭirunta vari calāpat tēvi eṉappaṭum eṉṟum, itaṉik kuṭanāṭṭuḷ vāḻpavar celutta vēṇṭum eṉṟum annāḷiya aracu āṇiyiṭṭuḷatu1 eṉa āṟṟūrk kal veṭṭu aḻakākak kūṟukiṟatu.
kuṟumakaḷ - iḷampeṇ. uṟikkum eṉpatu “āṟā ṟaviyum aṉṉa piṟavum 2” eṉpataṉāl amintu “tattam marapiṉ tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē 3” eṉpataṉāl uvamapporuḷ taruvatāyiṟṟu. narampu - yāḻnarampu. iṉcollukku narampiṉ iciyik kūṟutal marapu; “vaṭippuṟu narampiṉ tīviya moḻintē 4” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ talimakaṉ, talimakaḷiṉ meytoṭṭup payiṉṟum, iṭampeṟṟut taḻuviyum, iṭiyūṟu kiḷantum, nīṭuniṉin tiraṅkiyum kūṭutal uṟuttakāli, avaḷ kūṭṭattiṟku uṭampaṭumukattāl muṟuvalittami taṉ uḷḷattu nīṅkātu niṉṟu tikaḻtaliṉ, eyiṟukeḻu tuvarvāy eṉṟum, uṟutal mutaliya nāṉku puṇarcciyum peṟṟu, cevippuṇarcci viriviṟ peṟātu peyarttup peṟṟu makiḻnta ciṟapput tōṉṟa, tīṅkiḷaviyaḷē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. itaṉāl kaḷavuk kālattu meyyuṟu puṇarcci yeṉpatu meytoṭutal mutaliya aintumē yeṉa aṟika. “kaṇṭu kēṭṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyum aimpulaṉum oṇṭoṭi kaṇṇēyuḷ 5”  eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. piṟkālattavarāl ivvēṟupāṭu pakuttuk kāṭṭappaṭāmaṟ pōṉatu tamiḻiṉattiṉ tavakkuṟi.
itu “meytoṭṭup payiṟal 6” eṉṟa cūttirattut “taṇ ṭātu irappiṉum” eṉṟataṟku iraṭṭuṟa moḻital eṉṉum uttiyāl tavirā tirappiṉum eṉak koṇṭu ataṉpāl amikkappaṭum.
	186.	nāri nalliṉam kaṭuppa makaḷir
nīrvār kūnta luḷarun tuṟiva
poṅkukaḻi neyta luṟippa vittuṟip
palkāl varūun tēreṉac
cellā tīmō veṉṟaṉaḷ yāyē.
itu, pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ vantu nīṅkum talimakaṉit tōḻi ceṟippaṟivuṟīi varivu kaṭāyatu.
paḻiya uri :
nāriyiṉam kaṭuppa makaḷir nīrvār kūntal uḷarum tuṟiva eṉṟatu, tammiṭattuk kuṟṟam nīkkum makaḷiriyuṭiya tuṟiva eṉṟavāṟu.
uri :
nāriyiṉatu nalla kūṭṭatti yoppa makaḷir nī, oḻukuṅ kūntalip pularttum tuṟivaṉē, nīr mikuntuḷḷa kaḻikaḷil neytalkaḷ nīrttuḷikaḷic coriyum ittuṟik kaṇ, “pala kālum tēr vārāniṉṟatu” eṉak kūṟi, “iṉip puṟattē cellaṟka” eṉṟu emmuṭiya tāy iṟceṟittaṉaḷ. ākaliṉ, nī ēṟṟa toṉṟaṉic ceyvāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
vārtal - oḻukutal, uṟittal - tuḷittal, poṅku kaḻi eṉpuḻi, iṭattunikaḻ poruḷiṉ toḻil iṭattiṉmēl niṉṟatu. cellātīmō eṉṟatu ēvaṟ poruṇmikkaṇ vanta etirmaṟi muṟṟuviṉit tiricol. nī ēṟṟa toṉṟaṉic ceyka eṉpatu kūṟṟeccam. nāri nalliṉam kaṭuppa eṉṟār, tam ciṟakukaḷ nīrāl naṉintavaḻi, pulartaṟporuṭṭu utaṟi nāri vayiṟṟil aṭittukkōṭaṟ pōla, ōrimakaḷir tam kūntal naṉintavaḻi, pularat tuvaṭṭit tam vayiṟalittukkoṇṭu ēkutal paṟṟi. “nīralik kaliiya kūḻi vaṭiyāc, cāay avvayi ṟalippa uṭaṉiyintu, ōri makaḷirum ūreytiṉarē 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟumāṟṟāl aṟika.
ittuṟikkaṇ tēroṉṟu palakāl varūum eṉṟatu, mēl cellā tīmō eṉa maṟuttaṟku ētuk kūṟiyavāṟu. ākavē, varuvatu niṉ tēr eṉa yāy aṟintaṉaḷākaliṉ, nī niṉakku āvatu ceyka eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉṟavāṟām.
nīrāṭiyataṉāl nārikaḷip pōla makaḷir tam kūntaliṉ īram keṭap pularttum tuṟiva eṉṟatu niṉṉōṭu uṇṭākiya naṭpiṉāl ivaṭku varuttam uṇṭākātavāṟu varintu koḷka eṉak kuṟippāy varivu kaṭāyavāṟu.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ, talimakaṉ tērūrntu varutal amiyum eṉpatu, “tērum yāṉiyum kutiriyum piṟavum, ūrntaṉar iyaṅkalum uriyar eṉpa 1” eṉpataṉāl aṟika.
	187.	notuma lāḷar koḷḷā riviyē
emmoṭu vanta kaṭalāṭu makaḷirum
neytalam pakittaḻip pāvi puṉiyār
uṭalakam koḷvō riṉmiyiṉ
toṭalik kuṟṟa cilapū viṉarē.
itu, tōḻi kiyuṟi maṟuttatu. kiyuṟiyāvatu kuṟi
yuṟuvār cellumāṟu pōlat talimakaṉ kikkoṇṭu collum taḻiyum, kaṇṇiyum, pavaḻakkoṭiyum, muttum piṟavumām.
uri :
peruma, emmuṭaṉ pōntu kaṭalāṭum makaḷir neytalē talimippaṭa viravit toṭutta ippakittaḻiyiṉi maṇaṟkaṇ niṟuviyāṭum pāvikkum puṉiyār: vēṟu pūkkaḷ viravāmal muṟṟum neytalē toṭuttu aṇintu koḷ vōrum ilarākaliṉ, toṭali toṭuppōrum itaṉiṟ cila pūvē koḷvar; ākaliṉ, itaṉi yām ēṟpiṉ notumalāḷarum ārāytaṟ kiṭaṉām eṉṟavāṟu.
notumal - pakimiyum kēṇmiyumillāta naṭuvu nilimi. koḷḷāmi - ēlāmi. pakittaḻi - niṟattāl vēṟupaṭṭa malarkaḷālum kuḻiyālum toṭukkappaṭṭa taḻiyuṭi; nīlaniṟamuṭiya neytalē toṭuppiṉ aḻaku piṟavāmi karuti, ceruntiyē, puṉṉiyē, aṭumpē eṉṟa pala vēṟu pūkkaḷiyum paccilikaḷiyum viravit toṭuttaliṉ, pakittaḻi eṉappaṭṭatu. poṉṉiṟamum veṇṇiṟamum pacuminiṟamum koṇṭa pala pūkkaḷum pacciliyum viravik kiṭappiṉum iṭiyē maṇipōṟ kiṭantu miḷirtaliṉ, ney talam pakittaḻi eṉṟār. “koṭuṅkaḻi nivanta neṭuṅkāl neytal, ampaki neṟittaḻi yaṇipeṟat tii 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. “neytal naṟumalar ceruntiyoṭu virii 2” eṉṟum, “aṭumpi ṉāymalar virii neytal, neṭuntoṭi vēynta nīrvār kūntal 3” eṉṟum, “pācaṭik kalitta kaṇikkāl neytal, pūvuṭaṉ neṟitaru toṭali 4” eṉṟum varuvaṉa vaṟṟāl  cerunti mutalāyiṉavaṟṟi neytalōṭu viravit toṭuttal aṟika. neytalam pakittaḻi, virikkum vaḻi varittal. ummi pirittuk kūṭṭappaṭṭatu.
uṭalakam - muḻutum. uṭalakam koḷvōr ilar eṉṟa taṉāl, toṭali koḷvōr uḷar eṉpatu eytavē, ataṉi vilakki orumaruṅku taḻī i yurippāḷ, toṭalik kuṟṟa cila pūviṉar eṉṟum, ivvāṟu, emmōṭu āṭum makaḷirālum, toṭali toṭuppōrālum vilakkappaṭṭa taṉi yām ēṟpiṉ, ayalār kaṇṭu ivarkaṭku ivi āyiṉavāṟu eṉṉi yeṉa ārāyvar eṉa maṟuppāḷ, notumalāḷar koḷḷār iviyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. koḷḷāmi, “koḷḷār aṟivuṭiyār 5” eṉṟāṟ pōla. kātalarāya makaḷirum, tāyarum ēṟpiṉum eṉpatu paṭa varum eccavummi vikārattāl tokkatu.
“nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 6” eṉṟa cūttirattup “pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭu vakiyum” eṉpuḻi “vaki” eṉṟataṉāl amittu itu kiyuṟi maṟuttatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
	188.	iruṅkaḻic cēyiṟā viṉappuḷ ḷārum koṟkik kōmāṉ koṟkiyam peruntuṟi vikaṟi, malarum neytal pōlat takiperi tuṭiya kātali kaṇṇē.
itu, viruntu vāyilākap pukunta talimakaṉ taliviyatu ilvāḻkkic ciṟappuk kaṇṭu makiḻntu colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
viṭiyaṟkāli malarum neytalik kaṇṇiṟku uvamiyākak kūṟiṟṟu, vanta poḻutē malarnta ciṟappu nōkki eṉak koḷka. iruṅkaḻic cēyiṟā iṉappuḷ ārum eṉṟatu, viruntiṉar nukarcci kūṟiyavāṟu eṉak koḷka.
uri :
kūṭṭamākiya kurukukaḷ kariya kaḻikkaṇ vāḻum cemmi yāṉa iṟāmīṉkaḷi yuṇṇum koṟki vēntaṉatu koṟki eṉṉum ūriṉ periya tuṟiyil vikaṟip pōtiṉkaṇ malartalic ceyyum neytal pōla em kātaliyiṉ kaṇkaḷ aḻaku mikavuṭiyavāyiṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
cemmi yuṭimiyiṉ cēyiṟā eṉṟār. “teṇkaḻic cēyiṟā 1” eṉpuḻiyum ituvē kūṟik koḷka. koṟkik kōmāṉ eṉṟatu. pāṇṭi vēntarkkuriya ciṟappup peyaruḷ oṉṟu. “koṟki vēntaṉ 2” eṉṟu piṟkālak kalveṭṭuk kaḷum pāṇṭivēntarik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. vikaṟi, viṭiyaṟkālam. “viṭiyal pulari vikaṟi yeṉpaṉa oruporuṭ kiḷavi 3” eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar. neytal, viṭiyaṟkālattu malartali “vikaṟik kaṭkamaḻ neytal 4” eṉṟum, “meṉ melat teṟukatir iṉṟuyiṟ pacuvāy tiṟakkum, pēti neytal 5” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar. taki - aḻaku. aḵtāvatu malarcciyum kuḷircciyu muṭimi.
taṉṉi nōkki varum viruntic cēymikkaṭ kaṇṭuḻi iṉmukamum, atupaṟṟi naṇṇiyavaḻi iṉcollum, atupaṟṟi viruntuṭaṉ paṭṭavaḻi naṉṟāṟṟalum eṉa viruntōmpaṟ kuriya aṟaneṟi mūṉṟaṉuḷ mutaṟkaṇṇa tākiya mukattāṉ amarntu nōkkutalic ceypavaḷ, tāṉ viruntu vāyilākappuka, taṉṉic cēymikkaṭ kaṇṭa appoḻutē mukamalarntu iṉitu nōkkiṉāḷ eṉpāṉ, takiperi tuṭiya kātali kaṇṇē eṉṟum, vanta viruntu malarāta mukanōkku muṉ kuḻiyum meṉmiyuṭimi paṟṟic cēymiyiṟ kaṇṭa tuṇiyē malarum ciṟappiṉi yāppuṟuttaṟku ñāyiṟṟiṉ varaviṉic cēymiyi ṉuṇarntu vikaṟi malarum neytali uvamittum kūṟiṉāṉ. iḵtu aṉpu toka niṟṟal. koṟkip peruntuṟikkaṇ puḷḷiṉam cēyiṟā uṇṇum eṉpataṉāl talimakaḷatu viruntōmpum māṇpiṉic ciṟappittamiyiṉ, kātaliyiṉ kaṇṇi vitantōtip pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.
iḵtu “aruntoḻil muṭitta cemmaṟkāli, viruntoṭu nallavi vēṇṭaṟ kaṇṇum 1” eṉpataṉāl amiyum.
	189.	puṉṉi nuṇṭā tuṟittaru neytal poṉpaṭu maṇiyiṟ poṟpat tōṉṟum mellam pulampaṉ vanteṉa nalla vāyiṉa tōḻiyeṉ kaṇṇē.
itu, variviṭi vittup pirinta talimakaṉ varivāṉ vantuḻik kaṇṭu uvakiyōṭu vanta tōḻi, “niṉ kaṇ malarc cikkuk kāraṇam eṉ?” eṉṟu viṉaviya talivikkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
puṉṉi nuṇṭātu uṟittarum neytal poṉpaṭu maṇiyiṉ poṟpat tōṉṟum eṉṟatu, niṉ kuṭippiṟappup paṇṭiyiṉum ciṟattaṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟ ṟeṉṟu avaṉvarivu kūṟiyavāṟām.
uri :
tōḻi, puṉṉimalariṉ nuṇṇiya tukaḷ paṭintu kiṭakkum neytal poṉṉiṭip paṭutta nīla maṇi pōla viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum melliya kaṭaṟkariyiyuṭiya talivaṉ varitaṟporuṭṭu varutalāl eṉ kaṇkaḷ nalla vāy malarcciyuṟṟaṉa kāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
tātu, tukaḷ. uṟittarum neytal, utirntu kiṭappa viḷaṅkum neytal. puṉṉit tātu poṉṉiṟamum, neytal maṇiniṟamu muṭimiyiṉ, ataṉ tātu paṭinta neytali poṉpaṭu maṇiyiṉ poṟpat tōṉṟum eṉṟār. “maṇiyēr neytal māmalar niṟiyap, poṉṉēr nuṇṭātu puṉṉit tāam 1” eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. vanteṉa nallavāyiṉa eṉṟataṉāl, varitaṟporuṭṭu vantā ṉeṉpatu peṟṟām.
variviṭi vittut talimakaṉ pirintataṉāl, āṟṟāmi tōṉṟit taliviyi varuttap pētuṟ ṟiruntāṭku avaṉ varivoṭu vantami kāṇa iṉpam mīkkūrntamiyiṉ, ataṉit talimakaṭku aṟivippāḷ, mellam pulampaṉ vanteṉa eṉṟu avaṉ varavum, varivu paṟṟiya vēṭki talivi yuḷḷattē uṭaṉeḻuvatu kaṇṭu, nalla vāyiṉa eṉṟu avaṉ varivoṭu vantamiyum tōḻi uṇarttiṉāḷ. tirumaṇac ceyti terivittaliṉ eṉkaṇ nalla vāyiṉa eṉat talivi kūṟṟit tāṉ koṇṭukūṟiṉāḷ eṉa aṟika.
varivāl talimakaḷiṉ kuṭippiṟappup paṇṭiyiṉum ciṟappuṟuvataṉālum, varivu kaṇṇiya varavāl avaḷatu mēṉi vēṟupāṭu nīṅkip polivuṟutaliṉālum, eṉ maṉam uvaki mikuntatu eṉṟaṟku ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉṟumām. puṉṉi nuṇṭātu paṭitalāl neytal poṉpaṭu maṇiyiṉ tōṉṟum eṉṟatu, talimakaṉ varivoṭu vantamiyiṉ avaṉ toṭarpāl niṉ kuṭic ciṟappuṟum eṉa uḷḷuṟuttavāṟu.
	190.	taṇṇaṟu neytal taḷiyaviḻ vāṉpū veṇṇe larinar māṟṟiṉa raṟukkum mellam pulampaṉ maṉṟavem pallita ḻuṇkaṇ paṉicey tōṉē.
itu, tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.
paḻiya uri :
arivōr neytaṟpūvi nīkki veṇṇel ariyum eṉṟatu, taṉakku aṭuttatu ceytalaṉṟip piṟarkku varuttam ceyyāṉ eṉat talimakaṉ kuṇam kūṟiyavāṟām.
uri :
emmuṭiya palavākiya itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pūppōlum miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ nīr vārntu varuntuvittavaṉ, veḷḷiya nellariyum uḻavar kuḷirnta naṟuviya neytaliṉ muṟukkaviḻnta aḻakiya pūvi nīkki anneṟ payiriyē aṟukkum melliya kaṭaṟkariyi yuṭiyō ṉāvaṉ, teḷivāka eṉṟavāṟu.
neytaṟkuc ciṟantamiyiṉ, taṇṇaṟu neytal taḷiya viḻ vāṉpū eṉac ciṟappittār. nelvayaliṭattu neytalum uṭaṉtōṉṟi malartal iyaṟki. “vaṇṭu mūcu neytal nelliṭi malarum, ariyalaṅ kaḻaṉi ārkkāṭu 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. veṇṇel nelvaki. vāṉpū, aḻakiya pū. māṟṟiṉar, muṟṟeccam. pallitaḻ, tāmari kuvaḷi mutaliya pū. talivi yuṟuppiṉit taṉatāka uḷappaṭuttik kūṟal vaḻuvā yiṉum, “tāyattiṉ aṭiyā vīyac cellā, viṉivayiṉ taṅkā vīṟṟuk koḷappaṭā, emmeṉa varūum kiḻamit tōṟṟam, allā vāyiṉum pulluva vuḷavē 2” eṉpataṉāl amiyum eṉka. paṉi, varuttam.
talimakaḷ mēṉi vēṟupaṭṭuk kaṇ paṉittu niṟṟalik kaṇṭa tāyar, ataṟ kētu cūḻvār veṟi mutalāyiṉa eṭut taṟku eṇṇiyatu uṇarntu aṟattoṭu niṟkiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, mellam pulampaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ makkaḷiṉattu makaṉē yaṉṟit teyvamallaṉ eṉpāḷ, maṉṟa eṉṟum, uḷḷuṟiyāl avaṉatu kātal māṇpu kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, ivaḷ ivvāṟu paṉippac ceytōṉ yāvaṉ eṉa ārāyum tāyar kūṟṟiṉiyē koṇṭu pallitaḻ uṇkaṇ paṉicey tōṉē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
uṇkaṇ paṉiceytāṉ eṉac cevili mutalāyiṉār paḻitūṟṟak kaṇṭu poṟāta vuḷḷattaḷāya tōḻi, kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum ārāyntu vēlaṉik koṇṭu veṟiyayarak karutum tāyar ceyali aṟintu ataṉi vilakki aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ, ivaṭku ivvēṟupāṭu eytuvittōṉ mellam pulampaṉ eṉṟu veḷippaṭa moḻintu, coṟpalkāmip poruṭṭu, nellarivōr vayaliṭiyē peruki malarntirukkum neytali māṟṟi veṇṇelliyē aṟuttuk koḷḷum meṉpulat talivaṉākaliṉ, ivaḷiyē varintu koḷḷum tuṇi vuṭiyaṉāvaṉ eṉa uḷḷuṟiyāl vaṟpuṟuttiṉāḷ.
20. vaḷippattu
pāṭṭuttōṟum vaḷiyākiya karupporuḷē ciṟantu varat tokukkappeṟṟiruttaliṉ, ippattu ippeyari yuṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
vaḷi eṉpatu, nīrnilikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum ēral, nantu, cippi, palakaṟi, kiḷiñcil, caṅku ākiyavaṟṟiṟkup potuvāka vaḻaṅkum peyarāyiṉum, caṅkukkuc ciṟappākavum atu vaḻaṅ kum. itu nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa aḷavil vakippaṭum eṉavum, ivaṟṟip paṟṟiya ārāycci oruvakik kaliyām (Conchology) eṉavum aṟiñar urikkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ potuvāṉa ārāycciyē ki.pi. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil (ki.pi. 1795-l) tāṉ toṭaṅkiṟṟu. itukāṟum nikaḻntuḷḷa ārāycciyāl, ivaṟṟil muppatiṉāyiram vaki uṇṭu eṉpatu teriya vantuḷḷatu. nilattiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaḷiyiṉam toṭak kattil kaṭalil vāḻntiruntu piṉṉar nilattiṟ paravip paṭarn tirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu uyirnūlaṟiñar tuṇipu.
ivaṟṟiṉ ōṭukaḷ mutalil ciṟu vaṭṭamākat tōṉṟi nāḷaṭivil cuḻivaṭivil perukukiṉṟaṉa. ōṭukaḷiṉ ākkatti ārāyntapōtu ataṉkaṇ kaṭaluppum cuṇṇāmpumē peruka vuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu terintatu. iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḷiyiṉat tiṉ ōṭukaḷic cuṭṭuc cuṇṇāmpu iṟakkum ceyal naṭipeṟṟuvaruvatu kaṇkūṭu. iṭikkālattum ic cuṇ ṇāmpu payaṉpaṭṭa tiṟattik kampar “caṅkaveṇ cuti yuṭit tavaḷa māḷiki 1” eṉṟataṉāl iṉitu aṟiyalām.
vaḷikaḷuḷ āṇ peṇ vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. vēṉiṟ kālattil nīrniliyiṭattu vemmi mikukiṟapōtu ivi tammiṟ kūṭi muṭṭiyiṭat toṭaṅkum. akkālattē mīntōl 1 pōṉṟa cavviṉāl aminta pi yoṉṟi nīril nīḷaviṭum; ap pikkuḷḷē tāṉ muṭṭikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. kaṭal vaḷi oṉṟiṉ pikkuḷ irunta muṭṭikaḷi eṇṇiṉōr, oru piyil 1,50,000 muṭṭi mutal 6,00,000 muṭṭikaḷ vari irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
ivvaḷikaḷ nīr cūḻnta nilavulaku muṟṟum paravi uḷḷaṉa. teṉturuvattu aṉṭārṭṭik kaṭal mutal vaṭaturuvattic cārnta pirāṉcu jōcaplāntu 2 kirīṉlāntu variyil ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmi kāṇappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. nilattiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaḷikaḷ kaṭal maṭṭattukku mēl 18000 aṭi uyaram variyil uḷḷaṉa. kaṭal vaḷikaḷik kāṇa muyaṉṟōr kaṭalakattil 14,225 aṭi āḻattil vāḻum vaḷikaḷik kaṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
peruṅkaṭalil vāḻum vaḷikaḷiṉ aṭippakuti kaṭalnīri ṉiṉṟum pirikka muṭiyātavāṟu oruvakip paciyāl oṭṭik koṇ ṭiruttalāl, pēralikaḷ malipōl eḻuntu tammil mōtik koḷḷiṉum ivi ciṟitum ūṟu eytuva tilli.
vaḷikaḷ perumpālum kaṭaṟpācikaḷiyum kaṭaṟpūṭu kaḷiyum uṇṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. ivvaḷikaḷiṉ ōṭukaḷ pal vēṟu niṟam peṟṟum, nīṇṭa muḷpōṉṟa kōṭukaḷ (kompu kaḷ) vaḷarappeṟṟum, iṭamcuḻittum, valam cuḻittum kāṇap paṭum. āyiṉum avaṟṟāl ivi peṟum payaṉ iṉṉateṉa itukāṟum kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭavilli. kōṭuṭimi paṟṟiyē cila vaḷikaḷit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ “kōṭu” eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. “kōṭunaral puvam 3” “kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷi 4” eṉa varutal kāṇka. iṭañ cuḻittavaṟṟiṉum valañcuḻittavi ariya vātaliṉ, avi valampuri eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. “valampuri vāṉkōṭu naralum ilaṅkunīrt tuṟi 5” eṉavum, “avirkatir muttamoṭu valampuri corintu, paḻiyar makaḷir paṉittuṟi parava 6” eṉavum varuvaṉa kāṇka.
uyirnūl aṟivut tuṟiyil ivvaḷikaḷippaṟṟiya ārāycci iṉṉum muṟṟuppeṟavilli; ivaṟṟip paṟṟiya aṟivu aṉittum iṉi mēlum pallāṇṭukaḷ ārāya vēṇṭiya niliyil uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, iv vaḷikaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ nam tamiḻt tokinūlkaḷil kiṭippatu pārāṭṭattakkatu. ivvaḷikaḷiyē ciṟanta poruḷākak koṇṭu ippattup pāṭṭukkaḷum iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉavākaliṉ iṉi avaṟṟik kāṇalām.
ivvaḷikaḷ iṭum muṭṭikaḷ palavum kēṭiṉṟip porittu vaḷarumāyiṉ, kaṭalulakamē yaṉṟi nilavulakum iṭam kāṇātu eṉṟō, yātō, ivaṟṟik karuvilēyē uṇṭu aḻikkum uyiriṉam peruka vuḷḷaṉa. kaṭalil vāḻum peru mīṉkaḷum timiṅkilaṅkaḷum kaṭaṟ paṟavikaḷum ivaṟṟi vēṭṭuṇṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. nilattilum ivaṟṟukkuk kēṭu ceyyum vakiyil paṟavikaḷum naṇṭukaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. veyil vemmiyum ōraḷavu ivaṟṟukkuk kēṭu ceykiṟatu. “eṉpi lataṉi veyilpōlak kāyumē 1” eṉṟa tiruvaḷḷuva ṉāratu pēruri itaṟkum poruntukiṟatu. ivvaḷiyiṉattuc cilavaṟṟi mēṉāṭṭavarum piṟarum uṇavākak koḷḷu kiṉṟaṉar. 1922il laṇṭaṉ nakarattu pilliṅs kēṭ cantiyil 4100 ṭaṉ niṟiyuḷḷa vaḷikaḷ uṇavukkāka vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. kaṭalmīṉ timiṅkilam mutaliyavaṟṟip piṭippataṟkākavum ivikaḷ makkaḷāl koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvaḷikaḷuḷ muttuṇṭātal oru ciṟappiyalpu. maḻi peyyuṅkāl ivvaḷiyiṉam nīrmaṭṭattukku vantu tamatu ōṭṭit tiṟantu maḻiyi nōkkumeṉavum, maḻittuḷi oṉṟu ōṭṭiṟkuḷ vīḻntatum ōṭṭi mūṭikkoṇṭu kaṭaṟkuṭ ceṉṟu viṭu meṉavum, attuḷiyē piṉṉar muttāku meṉavum kūṟuva toru koḷki paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. “micippeytanīr kaṭaṟparantu muttākuntu 2” eṉak karuvūrk katappiḷḷi eṉpār kuṟikkiṉṟār. muttup piṟakkum tiṟatti ārāyntapōtu, ivvaḷikaḷiṉ uṭaṟkuḷ vēṟṟupporuḷ ētēṉum pukuntuviṭiṉ, ataṉ uṭaliliruntu oruvakip pacinīr ūṟi ataṉi vaḷittuk koḷḷum; atuvē nāḷ mutirap peruki muṟṟi muttākum. avvā ṟātaṟku aintāṇṭu mutal ēḻāṇṭukaḷ cellum eṉṟu kūṟuvar. ivvaḷikaḷi ūṟiṉṟi vaḷaraviṭṭāl irupattintu āṇṭukaḷ ivi uyir vāḻum. oru vaḷi tōṉṟi iraṇṭu mūṉṟu āṇṭu kaḻintapiṉ tāṉ muttuṇṭātaṟkuriya pacinīr ūṟum cevvi ataṟku uṇṭākiṟatu. ivviyalpiṉāl nilattiluḷḷa vaḷikaḷiṭattum muttukkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. perumaliyiṭattu nīrnili kaḷiṭattē piṟanta muttukkaḷ nīrpperukkāl āṟṟiṭi varaṉṟik koṇarappaṭum. atukaṇṭa paḻantamiḻc cāṉṟōr āṟukaḷil muttukkaḷik kūṟiyatu kaṟpaṉi yaṉṟu eṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
immuttukkaḷil naṉku uruṇṭu tiraṇṭuḷḷa muttamē ciṟantatākum. coṭṭiyum coḷḷiyumāka iruppaṉa ciṟappuṭiya valla. uṭaṟkuṭpukum vēṟṟup poruḷ mayir nuṉiyiṉum nuṇṇitā yiruppiṉum atu vaḷiyiṉ uṭaṟkuḷ pukuntu viṭiṉ muttuṇṭām eṉṉum itaṉik kiṟittuvukku 2200 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kaṇṭavar cīṉarkaḷ eṉṟu mēṉāṭ ṭavar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. cīṉarkaṭkumuṉpē nam tamiḻakam muttukkup peyar ciṟantirunta teṉpatu uṇmiyē. āyiṉum tamiḻakattiṉ toṉmiyiṉi mēṉāṭṭavarkku eṭuttuk kāṭṭum aḷavil tamiḻ nūlaṟivu annāḷil illāmiyē cīṉaratu toṉmi ittuṟiyil ciṟappākak kuṟikkappaṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ pati aṟiñar uṇartal vēṇṭum. piṉṉar accīṉarōṭu cappāṉiyarum kūṭi muttu ārāycciyil talippaṭṭaṉar. cappāṉ palkalikkaḻaka uyirnūṟ pērāciriyar, miṭcukkuṟi 1 eṉpār kaṇṭa muṟipaṟṟi 1891l mikimōṭō 2 eṉpār, vaḷiyiṉ uṭaṟkuḷ ceyaṟkiyāka oru ciṟu poruḷip pukutti muttu viḷivikkum tiṟattik kaṇṭār. piṉṉar atu peruki vaḷam peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. muttuk kaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷi naṉku ārāyntu kuṇam kuṟṟam kaṇṭu perukiya aḷavil tamiḻc cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷil kuṟittiruppati nōkkiṉ, avarkaḷum oru kālattil muttarāycciyil ciṟantami teḷivākum. koṟki mutaliya nakarattavar muttuk kuḷitta ceyal piṟitōr iṭattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.
kaṭal vaḷikaḷuḷ malēyā nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟpakutiyil kāṇap paṭupavi ōṭṭiṉ viḷimpu kaṟuttirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟip paṇṭā vaḷi 1 eṉpar. āstirēliya, niyūkiṉiya nāṭukaḷilum avaṟṟi aṭuttuḷḷa turutti (tīvu) kaḷilum ivi akappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum ivaṟṟuḷ muttu viḷippaṉa cilavē. kiḻakkup pāliṉīciyāvilum cila vaki vaḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avi nalla ōṭum muttum uṭiyaṉa. avi cīṉā, ilaṅki, antamāṉ, mālatīvu ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. maṭa kāskar pakutiyil uḷḷavi maṭakāskar vaḷi eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟē pampāy vaḷi, ekiptiya vaḷi, paṉāmāvaḷi eṉap palavakiyāka vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ṭākṭar jēmcaṉ eṉpār ivaṟṟi ārāyntu muppattiraṇṭu vaki kaṇṭirukkiṉṟār. itukāṟum kiṭittuḷḷa vaḷikaḷuḷ oru aṭi cuṟṟaḷavum āṟupavuṇṭu niṟiyumuḷḷa vaḷiyē mikap peritākak karutap paṭukiṟatu.
ṭākṭar jēmcaṉ kaṇṭa vakikaḷuḷ māṟkariti pēri valkaṟi2 eṉpatu oṉṟu. ivvaḷikaṭku oru vakiyāṉa paṟkaḷuṇṭām. ivi ilaṅki, tamiḻakam, pāracīkavaḷikuṭā, ceṅkaṭal ākiyavaṟṟil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cila cūyacu kālvāy veṭṭiṉ piṉpu mattiya tarikkaṭaṟkuḷ pukuntuḷḷaṉa. malēyā āstirēliyā niyūkiṉiyā ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil ivi muttukkākavum ōṭṭukkākavum kaṭaṟkuḷ mūḻki eṭukkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. cappāṉ pakuti vaḷikaḷum inta valkaṟi vakiyic cērntaviyē.
vaḷikaḷiṉ ōṭukaḷ palavakiyil payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āppirikkāviluḷḷa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṭiyē ivi nāṇaya māka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. pacipik kaṭaṟ ṟuruttikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷil aracarkaḷum talivarkaḷum ivaṟṟi māliyākak kōttu aṇintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. mattiyatarik kaṭaṟkarikaḷilum āppirikkāviṉ kīḻināṭukaḷilum ivvaḷikaḷ ūtukaruvi kaḷākavum, nīrarunta utavum kuvaḷikaḷākavum vaḷḷaṅkaḷākavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cīṉa cappāṉ poruḷkaḷiṉ pūccuvēlikkum cuvarkaḷil veḷḷi tīṟṟutaṟkum uṇavut taṭṭukkaḷi aḻaku ceytaṟkum ivi nalla payaṉ tarukiṉṟaṉa. teṉkaṭaṟ pakuti yiṉar ivaṟṟāl tūṇṭilmuṭkaḷ ceykiṉṟaṉar.
it tokinūṟ pāṭṭukkaḷ eḻunta kālattu makkaḷ vāḻvil vaḷi mikacciṟanta iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. tōḷilaṇiyum toṭiyum muṉkiyi laṇiyum vaḷiyum ivaṟṟiṉāl āki yavi. vaḷiyiṭattup piṟanta muttup paṇṭit tamiḻakattuc celva vakiyuḷ ciṟanta toṉṟākat tikaḻntatu. mēlināṭṭu yavaṉarkaḷum cōṉakarkaḷum kīḻināṭṭuc cāvakarum aru maṇarum 1 tamiḻakatti muttuviḷi nāṭu eṉṟē karutiṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉar ivvaḷikaḷi ūtukaruviyākavum ciṟārkaṭkup pālūṭṭum pālāṭiyākavum payaṉpaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻakat til eṅkum evarum ivaṟṟi uṇṭatāka yātoru kuṟippum ilakkiyaṅkaḷilātal kalveṭṭuk kaḷilātal vēṟṟunāṭṭuk kuṟippukkaḷilātal ituvariyiṟ kāṇappaṭavē illi.
maṟṟu, ivaṟṟi uṇpavarkku ivaṟṟāl īralnōy uṇṭākiṟa teṉṟum, naṉku tūymi ceyyappaṭāta ūṉ makkaṭkup peruntīṅku ceyyum eṉṟum mēṉāṭṭavar kaṇṭu, viṟpaṉikkuc cellu muṉpē ataṉic cevviyāka ārāy kiṉṟaṉar. ivi mikkuḷḷa nīrnilippakutikaḷil mēyum āṭumāṭukaṭkum ivaṟṟāl īralnōy uṇṭākiṟatām. nila vaḷikaḷuḷ oruvaki iṉattāl karumpu mutaliya payirkaṭku mikka tīṅku uṇṭākiṟa teṉṟu aṟiñar kaṇṭurikkiṉṟaṉar.
	191.	kaṭaṟkōṭu ceṟinta vaḷiyār muṉkik
kaḻippūt toṭarnta virumpal kūntal
kāṉal ñāḻaṟ kaviṉpeṟu taḻiyaḷ
variyara makaḷiri ṉariyaḷeṉ
niṟiyaru neñcam koṇṭoḷit tōḷē.
itu, niṉṉāṟ kāṇappaṭṭavaḷ evviṭattu ettaṉmiyaḷ eṉṟu viṉāviya pāṅkaṟkut talimakaṉ kūṟiyatu.
uri :
kaṭaṟkarikkaṇ tiriyāl koṇarappaṭṭuc ceṟivuṟṟa caṅkiṉāṟ caminta vaḷi yaṇinta muṉkiyiṉiyum, kaḻik kaṭ pūtta malarkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa māli aṇinta kariya palavākiya kūntaliṉiyum, kāṉalil niṉṟa ñāḻaliṉ taḻi yāṟ ceyta aḻakiya taḻiyuṭiyiṉiyum uṭiyaḷāy, niṟiyāl kōṭaṟku aritākiya eṉ neñciṉik koṇṭu maṟintavaḷ cūraramakaḷirip pōl peṟutaṟ kariyaḷāvāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
kaṭaṟkōṭu - kaṭaṟkari. kōṭu - caṅkumām. kaḻippūviṉ toṭarcci ataṉiyuṭiya kūntaṟku aṭiyāyiṟṟu. niṟi yāl piṟar koḷaṟku aritākiya tiṇmiyuṭimiyiṉ, niṟiyaru neñcam eṉappaṭṭatu. niṟuppa nillātu ōṭum iyalpiṟ ṟeṉṟaṟku niṟiyaru neñcam eṉṟār eṉiṉ, appeṟṟiyataṉik kōṭalum kavartalum arumi yākāmi yiṉ, atu poruḷaṉmi aṟika. muṉkiyum kūntalum taḻiyu muṭiyaḷ eṉṟatu talimakaḷ iyalum, kaṭalum kaḻiyum kāṉalum kūṟiyatu iṭamum cuṭṭi niṉṟaṉa.
iyaṟkippuṇarccikkaṇ tāṉ avaḷi etirppaṭṭuk kūṭumuṉ avaḷiṭit tōṉṟiya puṇarcci vēṭkiyi mūṉṟāmavattikkaṇ, “alkul tivaral aṇintavi tiruttal 1” mutaliya meyppāṭukaḷāl uṇarttiya ciṟappuṭimiyiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ vaḷiyār muṉki eṉṟum, meyyum meyyum tīṇṭiyavaḻi vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṭṭiya talimi kuṟittu, irumpal kūntal eṉṟum, uḷḷattu nikaḻnta nekiḻcciyiṉi uṭampoṭu toṭarpupaṭṭatāy nekiḻntu kāṭṭum ciṟap piṉmi kuṟittut taḻiyiṉi, kaviṉpeṟu taḻiyaḷ eṉa iṟutikkaṇṇum kūṟiṉāṉ. kūṟṟuvakiyāl kaṭippaṭṭa tāyiṉum, uruvanalam pārāṭṭik kaviṉ peṟu taḻi eṉap paṭṭatu eṉka. puṇarcci peṟṟu nīṅkuṅkāl, avaḷ āya veḷḷam vaḻipaṭa niṉṟatu kaṇṭu, “ivaḷik kūṭiṉēṉ eṉpatu māyamō” eṉa eṇṇi niṟkiyil, avaḷ maṟitalum, taṉ niṉivum collum ceyalum avaḷpālē amintoḻiya, vēṟē ceyalaṟṟu, ivaḷ teyvamakaḷ pōlum eṉa arumi karuti mayaṅkiṉamiyiṉ, variyara makaḷiriṉ ariyaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. piṟarum, “neṭuvarik kavāaṉ, cūraramakaḷiriṉ peṟaṟkari yōḷē 1” eṉpar. piṟarāl uṇaravum kavaravum kūṭāta neñciṉik kūṭumvaki uṇarntu taṉpālē amivittaṉaḷ eṉṟāṉ; appeṟṟiyārit “teyvattōṭu oppak koḷal 2” eṉṉum muṟimipaṟṟi variyaramakaḷirōṭu uvamittāṉ eṉiṉumām. taṉvari nillātu avaḷvari niṉṟu oḻukutalāl, kavarntu koṇṭāḷ eṉṟāṉ. iṉi, niṟuppa nillā eṉ neñciṉit taṉvayamākki, mīḷa eṉpāl vārāvakip piṇittuk koṇṭāḷ eṉṟaṟku, niṟiyaru neñcam kavarntu koṇṭāḷ eṉṟāṉ eṉiṉumām. itu tuṉpattuppulampal; eṉṉi? tāṉē varuntuvatākak kūṟukiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ.
	192.	kōṭupulaṅ koṭpak kaṭaleḻuntu muḻaṅkap
pāṭimiḻ paṉittuṟi yōṭukala mukikkum
tuṟivaṉ pirinteṉa nekiḻntaṉa
vīṅkiṉa mātō tōḻiyeṉ vaḷiyē.
itu, variviṭi vittup pirinta talimakaṉ vantuḻit talivi muṉintu kūṟuvāḷ pōlat taṉ melivu nīṅkiṉami tōḻikkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
kōṭu pulam kōṭpak kaṭa leḻuntu muḻaṅkap pāṭu imiḻ paṉittuṟi ōṭukalam ukikkum eṉṟatu, notumalar akala nam cuṟṟattār makiḻa alar kūṟum ayalār citiya avar tēr vantatu eṉpatām.
uri :
tōḻi, caṅkukaḷ karikkaṇ pōntu ulava, kaṭaṟkaṇ tirikaḷ eḻuntu muḻakkañ ceyya, olittaliyuṭiya kuḷirnta tuṟiyiṉkaṇ ōṭutaṟkuriya kalaṅkaḷi nīril celut tum tuṟivaṉ muṉṉarp pirintataṉāl nekiḻntu nīṅkiya eṉ vaḷikaḷ avaṉ varavāl muṉ kikkaṭ kiṭantu vīṅkiṉa; kāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
pulam - kari - pāṭu - ōci. “pāṭimiḻ paṉikkaṭal 1” eṉṟum, “pāṭi ṉaruvi 2” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar. kāṟṟāl untappaṭṭu alikaḷiṭiyē uyarntum tāḻntum cellutal nōkkik kalattiṉ celavi ōṭutal eṉpa. “naḷiyiru munnīr nāvāy ōṭṭi 3” eṉac cāṉṟōrum, “kaṭalōṭum nāvāyum ōṭā nilattu 4” eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum, “ōṭum timil koṇṭu 5” eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum kūṟupa. “ōṭam 6” eṉṉum vaḻakkum itupaṟṟi vantatu eṉakkoḷka. nekiḻtal. uṭal vāṭic curuṅkutalāl vaḷi kaḻaṉṟu, nīṅkutal. vīṅkutal, uṭal pūrittut taṭittalāl, taṉṉiliyiṟ peruki virintu kāṭṭutal. pirinteṉa nekiḻntaṉa eṉṟataṉāl, vīṅkiṉataṟ kētuvākiya varavu varuvikkappaṭṭatu. uḷḷuṟiyāl varavu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, vīṅkiṉataṟku ētu veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟiṟṟilar eṉiṉum amiyum.
itaṉ kaṇ, tuṟivaṉ pirinteṉa nekiḻntaṉa eṉṟu piriviṭi nikaḻntatu niṉittaliṉ muṉivāl melinta vāṟum, vīṅkiṉa mātō tōḻi eṉvaḷiyē eṉṟu varavu kaṇṭu kūṟutaliṉ melivu nīṅkiyavāṟum aṟika. variviṭi vittut talimakaṉ pirinta kālattut tāṉ mēṉi vēṟupaṭṭu uṭampu curuṅki yiruntataṉi nekiḻntu kāṭṭiya nili neñcil niṉṟu nilavutaliṉ, tuṟivaṉ pirinteṉa nekiḻntaṉa eṉṟum, avaṉ varavāl tuyar keṭṭu mēṉi taḷirttu ākkam peṟ ṟiruttalit tōḻikkuk kāṭṭi makiḻkiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, vīṅkiṉa mātō tōḻi eṉ vaḷiyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
kōṭu pulamkoṭpak kaṭal eḻuntu muḻaṅkap pāṭimiḻ paṉittuṟi ōṭukalam ukikkum eṉṟa viṭattu, kōṭu notu malarākavum, kaṭal cuṟṟattārākavum, tuṟi ayalārākavum, kalam talivaṉ tērākavum uḷḷuṟi koḷka.
	193.	valampuri yuḻuta vārmaṇa laṭikari
ilaṅkukatir mutta miruḷkeṭa vimikkum
tuṟikeḻu koṇkanī tanta
aṟipuṉal vālvaḷi nallavō tāmē.
itu variyātu vantoḻukum talimakaṉ, talimakaṭku vaḷi koṇṭu vantu koṭuttuḻi, “paṇṭi vaḷikaḷip pōlāvāy melintuḻi nīṅkā nalaṉuṭiyavō ivi” eṉat talimakaḷ melivu collit tōḻi varivu kaṭāyatu.
paḻiya uri :
muttam iruḷnīṅka imikkum tuṟivaṉākaliṉ, ivaṭku varukiṉṟa tīṅkik kaṭitiṉ nīkki varintu koḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatām.
uri :
valampuric caṅkukaḷ ūrntu celluvatāl uḻappaṭṭa nīṇṭa maṇal paranta kaṭaṟkarikkaṇ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa katir kaḷiyuṭiya muttukkaḷ iruḷ nīṅkumāṟu oḷirum tuṟi poruntiya taliva, nī tanta, olikkiṉṟa nīriṟ piṟanta veḷḷiya vaḷikaḷ, paṇṭu ivaḷ aṇintirunta vaḷikaḷ pōlātu melintuḻi nīṅkāta nalaṉ uṭiyavō? kūṟuka. eṉṟavāṟu.
valampuric caṅkukaḷ maṇalil ūrntu cuvaṭupaṭac cellu vatu uḻuvatu pōṟaliṉ, valampuri yuḻuta vārmaṇal eṉap paṭṭatu. “tuṟimēy valampuri tōyntu maṇal uḻuta  tōṟṟam 1” eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ. aṟipuṉal vālvaḷi, olikkiṉṟa nīrōṭṭam aminta veḷḷiya vaḷi eṉṟumām. nīrōṭṭam, naṉku kaṭicca liṭappaṭṭa vaḷiyil tōṉṟum oḷinalam. aṟital, iyaṟkiyaṭiyākum. tām eṉpatu kaṭṭuric cuvipaṭa niṉṟatu.
talimakaṉ piriyuntōṟum talimakaḷ vēṟupaṭṭu uṭampu curuṅkiyavaḻi nekiḻntōṭal vaḷikaṭku iyalpāyiṉum, pirivuḻi eytum vēṟupāṭu pulappaṭuttu varivukaṭāvum kuṟippiṉa ḷākaliṉ, nītanta..... vālvaḷi nallavō eṉṟāḷ. naṉmi, mēṉi melivuṟiṉum nīṅkā nilimi. nīṅkiyavaḻip piṟarkkup pulaṉāki alar payakkum nīrmiyāl, nīṅkāmiyi naṉmi eṉṟu kuṟittāḷ. oṉṟu niṉṟē ēṉiyatu muṭikkum eṉṉum muṟimiyāl, paṇṭu aṇintirunta vaḷikaḷiṉ tīmiyik kuṟippāl eytuvit tāḷ.
niṉ tuṟikkaṇ, ilaṅkukatir muttam iruḷ keṭa imit tal pōla, niṉ varivāl ivaḷ mēṉi vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu alar kūṟuvārāl uḷatākum varuttam nīṅkumeṉa uḷḷuṟiyāl alaraccam kūṟit tōḻi kuṟippāl varivukaṭāya vāṟu.
	194.	kaṭaṟkō ṭaṟutta varampō ḻavvaḷi
oṇṭoṭi maṭavaraṟ kaṇṭikum koṇka
naṉṉuta liṉṟu māal ceyteṉak
koṉṉoṉṟu kaṭuttaṉa ḷaṉṉiyatu niliyē
itu, pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ vantu nīṅkum talimakaṉi etirp  paṭṭu, maṉikkaṇ nikaḻntatu kūṟic ceṟippaṟivuṟīi varivu  kaṭāyatu.
uri :
koṇka, kaṭaṟcaṅkiṉi aṟuttu arattāl pōḻntu ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya vaḷiyiṉiyum, oḷḷiya toṭi yiṉiyumuṭiya maṭappam poruntiya talimakaḷik kāṇ pāyāka: avaḷuṭiya nalla neṟṟi iṉṟu oḷi maḻuṅki vēṟupaṭṭataṉāl, itaṟkuk kāraṇam oṉṟuṇṭu eṉa aṉṉi aiyuṟṟaṉaḷ; atu maṉikkaṇ nikaḻum nilimi yākaliṉ, nī niṉakku ēṟṟatu ceyka eṉṟavāṟu.
kaṇṭikum, muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu; ikum, muṉṉili aci. viḷakkam mikunta oḷi maḻuṅkiyataṉi māl ceyteṉa eṉṟār. koṉ, accapporuṭṭu. kaṭuttaṉaḷ eṉpatu kaṭi eṉṉum uriccol aṭiyākap piṟanta muṟṟuviṉi; “kaṭutta piṉ tēṟṟutal yārkkum aritu 1” eṉṟāṟ pōla. kaṭi, ipporuṭṭātal, “aiyamuṅ karippum ākalu murittē 2” eṉpata ṉāl aṟika. nī niṉakku ēṟṟatu ceyka eṉṟatu kuṟippeccam.
pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ, talimakaḷik kūṭi nīṅkukiṉṟāṉi etirp paṭṭuk kūṟutaliṉ, maṭavaraṟ kaṇṭikum eṉṟum, kuṟivaḻi vantu oḻukum oḻukkattiṟku āṟṟātu tīmiyum, kāval mikutiyum piṟavum iṭiyīṭā meṉa eṇṇi vēṟupaṭṭatu kāraṇamāka ivaḷ nutal oḷi ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭāḷ eṉpāḷ, naṉṉutal iṉṟu māl ceyteṉa eṉṟum, itaṉi aṉṉi uṇarntu aiyuṟṟu nōkkiṉā ḷākaliṉ, iṉi iṟceṟippu mikum eṉpāḷ, koṉṉoṉṟu kaṭuttaṉaḷ aṉṉi eṉṟum, ataṉāl kūṭutal aritākaliṉ nī niṉakku ēṟṟatu ceyka eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷ, ētu niliyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. eṟṟatāvatu viriya varintu kōṭal. nutal iṉṟu māl ceyteṉa eṉṟatu pacali pāytal. kaṭuttaṉaḷ eṉṟatu. tāyaṟivu kūṟal. atu niliyē eṉṟatu accam kāraṇamākat tōṉṟiya tiḷippu viṉi maṟuttal.
	195.	vaḷipaṭu muttam paratavar pakarum
kaṭalkeḻu koṇkaṉ kātaṉ maṭamakaḷ
keṭalarun tuyara nalkip
paṭaliṉ pāyal vuvi yōḷē.
itu variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṟ pirinta tali makaṉ taṉittuṟivu āṟṟāṉāyc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
peṟutaṟkariya muttip paratavar viṟkum kaṭal keḻu koṇ kaṉ eṉṟatu, avarkaḷ tārātār allar; yām avarkaḷukku vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuttuk koḷḷa māṭṭātu varuntukiṉṟōm eṉpatām. 1
uri :
caṅkiṉiṭattē piṟakkum muttukkaḷik koṇarntu paratavar ēṉi nilattu vāḻvārkku viṟkum kaṭal poruntiya koṇkaṉuṭiya aṉpu mikka makaḷ nīṅkutaṟkariya tuyarattit tantu, kaṇpaṭutalākiya iṉiya tuyilik kavarntukoṇṭā ḷākaliṉ, yāṉ āṟṟumāṟu eṉ? eṉṟavāṟu.
pakartal ēṉi uṇavupporuḷ karutiyākaliṉ, avi kiṭikkum ēṉi nilaṅkaḷ eṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu. pakarum eṉṉum peyareccam ētupporuḷ koṇṭu muṭintatu. tuyarattiṉ payaṉāṉa tuṉpavuṇarcci kaṇikamāy maṉattiṭi nikaḻvatoṉ ṟākaliṉ ataṉi māṟi niṉikkumiṭattuk keṭum iyalpiṟṟāka, poruḷmēl maṉam vittup pirintapōtum, keṭātu niṉṟu varuttutaliṉ, keṭalarun tuyaram eṉappaṭṭatu; paṭaliṉ pāyal eṉpataṟku “iṉtuyil 2” eṉṟataṟkuk kūṟi yataṉik kūṟikkoḷka.
uyiriṉum ciṟappap peṟṟōrāl talimakaḷ pēṇappaṭum iyalpiṉik kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻuki aṟintuḷāṉākaliṉ, kātal maṭamakaḷ eṉṟum, poruṭkoṭi etirntu ataṟku nēreṉa makaṭkoṭi yāṟṟum peṟṟōr māṇpaṟiyātu vēṇṭap paṭāta tuyaram nalki, vēṇṭi niṟkum uṟakkatti valintu kavarntamiyiṉ, maṭamakaḷ eṉṟum, taṉṉiṟ ṟīrntu cēymiyiṟ pirintuḻiyum taṉ pirivāṉākiya tuyaram, neñcil niṉṟu varuttu taliṉ, keṭalarun tuyaram eṉṟum, itukāṟum kaṇṭaṟiyātu, ivaḷoṭu toṭarpu peṟṟa piṉpē kāṇutaliṉ, nalki eṉṟum, tuyaruṟṟārkku uṟakkamiṉmi iyalpākaliṉ, paṭaliṉ pāyal vuviyōḷē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. uṟakkattiṉkaṇ tām pirinta kātalarik kaṉaviṭik kāṇum kāṭciyiṉpa muṭimi yālum, tuyarāṟ kalakkuṟṟa neñcam tuyiliṟkup piṉ teḷintu mēl ceyyakkaṭavaṉavaṟṟit teḷitalālum, paṭaliṉ pāyal eṉṟum, tāṉ aṟiyāvakiyil nīṅkiṉami yiṉ, vuvi yōḷē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
vaḷiyīṉṟa muttiṉit tantu paratavar vēṇṭuva koḷvar eṉṟatu, ivaḷ emakkut tuyaram nalkip pāyalik koṇṭāḷ eṉṟaṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu. vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuppak koṇṭu tām aritiṟ peṟṟataṉik koṭukkum peṟṟiyar avaḷ tamarāka, avaḷ yām vēṇṭātatu koṭuttu vēṇṭiṟṟuk koṇṭāḷ eṉpatoru nayam tōṉṟutal kāṇka. vēṇṭātatu keṭalarun tuyaram; vēṇṭiṟṟu, paṭaliṉ pāyal. itaṉāl, talimakaṉ kaṇ kaṇṭuyil maṟuttal eṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. iḵtu oruvakiyāl, peṟṟōrip paḻicciyatumām.
vaḷipaṭu muttam paratavar pakarum kaṭalkeḻu koṇ kaṉ eṉṟataṉāl talimakaḷip peṟal eḷimiyum, keṭalarum tuyaram nalkip paṭaliṉ pāyal vuvi yōḷē eṉṟa taṉāl pirivarumiyum tōṉṟak kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, peṟaleḷimi poruḷvayiṉ uḷḷattit turappa, pirivarumi mīḷaluṟutta, iṭiyē ceyviṉi viriya muṭiyāmi varuttat talimakaṉ āṟṟātu kūṟuvāṉāyiṉāṉ eṉa uṇarka. “puṟantāḻ piruṇṭa kūntaṟ pōtiṉ, niṟampeṟum īritaḻp polinta uṇkaṇ, uḷḷam piṇikkoṇ ṭōḷvayiṉ neñcam, cellal tīrkañ celvā meṉṉum, ceyviṉi muṭiyā tevvam ceytal, eyyā miyō ṭiḷivutalit tarumeṉa, uṟuti tūkkat tūṅki aṟivē, ciṟunaṉi viriyal eṉṉum āyiṭi, oḷiṟēntu maruppiṟ kaḷiṟu māṟupaṟṟiya, tēypurip paḻaṅkayiṟupōla, vīvatu kolleṉ varuntiya uṭampē 1” eṉṟu talimakaṉ niṉittu varuntumāṟu kāṇka.
“marapunili tiriyā māṭciya vāki, viravum poruḷum viravu meṉpa 2” eṉpuḻi, “variviṭi vittup pirintōṉ taliviyi niṉintu varuntik kūṟiyatu” eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.
iṉip paṭaliṉpāyal vaṟuvi yōḷē eṉṟa pāṭattukku, pāyal illi yākkiṉāḷ eṉpatu poruḷāyiṉum, mēṟ kāṭṭiya nayam tōṉṟaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟātal kāṇka.
	196.	kōṭī relvaḷik koḻumpal kūntal
āytoṭi maṭavaral vēṇṭuti yāyiṉ
teṇkaḻic cēyiṟāp paṭūum
taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa varintaṉi koṇmō.
itu, kuṟimaṟukkappaṭṭa talimakaṉ piṉṉum kuṟi vēṇṭiya vaḻit tōḻi colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
teṇkaḻic cēyiṟāp paṭūum cērppa eṉṟatu, niṉittaṉa akappaṭuttum tuṟivaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
uri :
caṅkiṉi aṟuttuc ceyta vaḷiyiṉiyum, koḻuviya palavākiya kūntaliṉiyum, nuṇṇiya toṭiyiṉiyu muṭiya maṭavaralākiya ivaḷik kūṭutal vēṇṭiṉi yāyiṉ, teḷinta nīriṉiyuṭiya kaḻikkaṇ vēṇṭuvōr koḷḷu māṟu cevviya iṟāmīṉkaḷ akappaṭum kuḷirnta kaṭaṉilat talivaṉē, vēṟu vaki yiṉmiyiṉ varintu kōṭalic ceyvāyāka.
vaḷi, caṅkiṉi aṟuttuc ceyyappaṭum eṉpataṉi, “vēḷāp pārppāṉ vāḷaram tumitta vaḷikaḷin toḻinta koḻuntu 1” eṉṟu kūṟumāṟṟāṉ aṟika. “kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷi nekiḻtta, pīṭukeḻu kuricil 2” eṉṟār piṟarum. iruṇṭu ceṟintiruttaliṉ, koḻum pal kūntal eṉappaṭṭatu. toṭi, vaḷi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṉṉaṇivatu. vaḷi caṅkiṉālāyatu; toṭi, vaḷiyālum maruppālu māyatu eṉa uṇarka. vaḷiyum, kūntalum,  toṭiyum uṭiya maṭavaral eṉa iyiyum. vaḷiyum toṭiyum kūntalum eṉṉātu, vaḷiyum kūntalum cērak kūṟiyatu, cilavum palavumām iyipu karuti yeṉa aṟika. “cilvaḷi viṟali 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. ovvoru kikkum ovvoru toṭiyē aṇiyappaṭu mākaliṉ, ataṉip pirittuk kūṟiṉār.
orukāl kuṟi maṟukkappaṭṭavaṉ, varintukōṭalit teruḷātu, piṉṉum kuṟiyirantu niṟṟaliṉ, maṭavaral vēṇṭutiyāyiṉ eṉṟum, kuṟiyuṟṟuk kūṭutal peṟiṉum atu piṉṉarp pirivukkum, alarkkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum añcappaṭṭuk kūṭāmiyē pōl varuttam ceyyumākaliṉ, varintaṉi koṇmō eṉṟum, uḷḷuṟiyāl varitaṟkuriya muṟimi yum kūṟiṉāḷ.
teṇkaḻiyiṭattu vēṇṭuvōr koḷḷumāṟu iṟāmīṉ kaḷ akappaṭum tuṟiva eṉṟataṉāl, nī vēṇṭivariṉ tamar makaṭkoṭi nērva rātaliṉ varintu koḷka eṉṟāḷām.
koḻumaṇik kūntal eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, koḻuviya maṇi pōlum kariya kūntal eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka. “maṇiyēcum iruṅkūntal 2” eṉavum, “maṇimaru ḷimpāl 3” eṉavum cāṉ ṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi, “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 4” eṉṟa cūttirattu “ulakurit toḻippiṉum” eṉpataṟku iḷampūraṇarum, nacciṉārkkiṉi yarum itaṉi eṭuttukkāṭṭuvar.
	197.	ilaṅkuvaḷi teḷirppa valava ṉāṭṭi
mukamputi katuppiṉa ḷiṟiñciniṉ ṟōḷē
pulampukoṇ māli maṟiya
nalaṅkē ḻāka nalkuva ḷeṉakkē.
itu, talimakaṉ iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ taliviyatu nilimi kaṇṭu colliyatu.
uri :
viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷikaḷ olikka alavaṉik kālāl alittu, mukatti maṟikkum kūntaliyuṭiyaḷāyt tali kuṉintu niṉṟavaḷ taṉimi koḷḷum mālip poḻutu maṟitalum, naṉmi poruntiya taṉ mārpiṉi eṉakku nalkuvaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
teḷirttal - olittal. alavaṉ - naṇṭu. āṭṭutal - alittal. katuppiṉa ḷeṉṉum muṟṟeccam iṟiñci niṉ ṟōḷ eṉṉum viṉippeyar koṇṭatu. taṉimiyuṟṟārkku ēṟṟa kālamākaliṉ, pulampukoḷ māli eṉappaṭṭatu. ceyaveṉeccam, itu nikaḻāniṟka itu nikaḻum eṉum uṭa ṉikaḻccip poruṭṭu.
alava ṉāṭṭutal makaḷir viḷiyāṭṭu vaki. “tiṇi maṇal aṭikari alavaṉ āṭṭi, aciyiṉaḷ irunta āytoṭik kuṟumakaḷ 1” eṉṟum, “perunīr aḻuvat tenti tanta, koḻumī ṉuṇaṅkaṟ paṭupuḷ ōppi, ekkarp puṉṉiyiṉ iṉṉiḻal aciic, cekkar ñeṇṭiṉ kuṇṭaḷi keṇṭi, ñāḻal ōṅkuciṉit toṭutta koṭuṅkaḻit, tāḻi vīḻkayiṟṟu ūcal tūṅkik koṇṭa liṭumaṇaṟ kuravi muṉiyiṉ, veṇṭalip puṇari yāyamo ṭāṭi, maṇippūm pintaḻi tii aṇittakap, palpūṅ kāṉal alkiṉam varutal 2” eṉṟum varuvaṉavaṟṟāl aṟika. alavaṉi alikkuṅkāl, uṭal kuluṅkak kiyum acitaliṉ vaḷi olippatu paṟṟi ilaṅku vaḷi teḷirppa eṉṟār. “cilavilaṅ kelvaḷi ñekiḻa, alavaṉ āṭṭuvōḷ 3” eṉṟār piṟarum. aḷiyiṭi yamarntu alavaṉik kiyāl alittalum  uṇmiyiṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟalum oṉṟu.
cēymikkaṇ varumiṭattu avaḷatu uṇmi uṇarttu taliṉ, ilaṅkuvaḷi teḷirppa eṉṟum, avaḷ taṉṉi niṉintu niṟkum niṉiviṉi, nōkkuvārkku mukam uṇart tāmipporuṭṭuk kūntal nekiḻntu mukam putiyac carintu kiṭantamiyiṉ, mukamputi katuppiṉaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. uḷḷa nikaḻcciyi mukam muṉṉam uṇarttum mutukkuṟi vuṭiya teṉpatu, “muṉṉam mukam pōla muṉṉurippatil 4” eṉpataṉāl aṟika. iyaṟkiyē nāṇmika vuṭiyaḷ ākaliṉ, iṟiñci niṉṟōḷ eṉṟāṉ. “nāṇaṭac cāynta nalaṅkiḷar eruttiṉ 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. māli maṟitalum tamittuk kūṭaluṟuvārkkuk kūṭṭam iṭiyīṭiṉṟi iṉitu eytu mākaliṉ, pulampukoḷ māli maṟiya eṉṟum, iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ tāṉ talippeyta viṭattu, ivvaṇṇamē etirppaṭṭā ḷākaliṉ, avviyipiṉāl iṉik kūṭṭa meytutal orutali yeṉat teḷintu kūṟukiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ, nalaṅkē ḻākam nalkuvaḷ eṉakkē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
iṉi, “ilaṅkuvaḷi teḷirppa alava ṉāṭṭi”yatu, pūkkoytal mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭiṉic ceyyāmi kuṟittu āyattār ārāyāmip poruṭ ṭeṉṟum, puṟattē alavaṉi alittu niṉṟā ḷāyiṉum, akattē taṉṉi niṉintu niṉṟamiyiṉ, iṟiñci niṉṟāḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
katuppiṉaḷ āyattu niṉṟōḷē eṉṟa pāṭattukkuk katuppiṉaḷāy āyattiṭi niṉṟāḷāyiṉum, māli maṟi talum ākam nalkuvaḷ eṉa urikka. āyiṉum, itu poruḷ ciṟavāmi uṇarka.
neytaṟkaṇ puṇartal kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, itu tiṇi mayakkam. “tiṇimayak kuṟutalum kaṭinili yilavē 2” eṉpatu viti. iṉi, “meytoṭṭup payiṟal 3” eṉṟa cūttirattuk kūṭuta luṟutal taṉiyē vantataṟku itaṉik kāṭṭuvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talimakkaḷ tāmē kūṭuta luṇṭēṉum, perunāṇiṉaḷāya talimakaḷpāl talimakaṉ, meytoṭṭup payiṟal mutalāyiṉa viṉṟik kūṭutaluṟutal amiyāmiyālum, avi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟaṟku itaṉ kaṇ coṟ kuṟippu oṉṟēṉum iṉmiyālum, tiṇimayakkat tukku utāraṇam kāṭṭiyavaḻi “iḵtu iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉ kaṇ taliviyatu nili kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu” eṉṟē kūṟi irutta lālum atu karuttaṉmi yaṟika.
	198.	vaḷiyaṇi muṉki vāleyiṟ ṟamarnaki
iḷiya rāṭum taḷiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ
kuṟuntuṟi viṉavi niṉṟa
neṭuntō ḷaṇṇaṟ kaṇṭikum yāmē.
itu, parattiyar maṉikkaṭ paṉṉāḷ taṅkip piṉpu āṟṟāmiyē vāyilāka vanta talimakaṉi etirppaṭṭa tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
kuṟuntuṟi viṉavi niṉṟa neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟu kaḷavukkālattu nikaḻntataṉik kūṟiṟṟu, talimakaḷ pulavi nīṅku taṟkum tāṉ neṭunāṭ pirintami kūṟukiṉṟavāṟu eṉṟu avaṉ varuntutaṟkum eṉak koḷka.
uri :
vaḷi yaṇinta muṉkiyiyum, veḷḷiya paṟkaḷ viḷaṅkum muṟuvaliyu muṭiya iḷamakaḷi rāṭum muṟukka viḻum malarkaḷiyuṭiya kāṉaliṭattuk kuṟuntuṟi  yāṇṭuḷatu eṉa viṉavi niṉṟa neṭiya tōḷkaḷiyuṭiya aṇṇali yām kāṇpōm, varuka eṉṟavāṟu.
amarnaki - viruppan tarum naki eṉṟumām. iḷiya rāṭum kuṟuntuṟi, taḷiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ kuṟuntuṟi yeṉa iyiyum. iṭattu nikaḻ poruṭkuriya aṭi iṭattiṉmēl niṉṟatu. iḷiyarāṭum tuṟiyātal paṟṟik kuṟuntuṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. viṉavi niṉṟa aṇṇal eṉka.
parattiyar maṉikkaṇ paṉṉāḷ taṅki avar kūṭṭamē niṉintu oḻukiṉāṉ eṉpataṉik kuṟippāl, vaḷiyaṇi muṉki vāleyiṟṟu amarnaki iḷiyarāṭum taḷi yaviḻ kāṉal kuṟuntuṟi viṉavi niṉṟa eṉṟum, atu kāraṇamākat talimakaḷ pulakkumeṉṟu añci, kaḷaviṉkaṇ yām puṉaliṭi vīḻntu keṭap puka, kuṟuntuṟi viṉavi niṉṟu nammi yeṭutta uyarnta tōḷi eṇṇip pulavātoḻital vēṇṭum eṉpāḷ, neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟum kūṟi ṉāḷ. nampāl nikaḻnta melivu kaṇṭu vārātu, taṉ āṟ ṟāmiyē vāyilākap pukuntāṉi nērtali nām ceyaṟpāṟṟu eṉṟaṟkuk, kaṇṭikum yāmē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “iḷiya rāṭum taḷiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ kuṟuntuṟi viṉavi” niṉṟatu, kaḷaviṉ kaṇ matiyuṭam paṭuttaṟku; “ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum, nīriṟ kuṟippiṉ nirampak kūṟit tōḻiyik 1” kuṟiyuṟuvāṉ kūṟiyataṉi niṉintu kūṟiya teṉka. iḵtu aḻivil kūṭṭattu avaṉ pirivāṟṟāmi.
iṉi, “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 2” eṉṟa cūttirattu, “kuṟinta vaṭ paṭariṉum eṉṟatu, mēl talivaṉatu puṇarcciyuṇmi yaṟintu tāḻntu niṉṟa tōḻi, tāṉum kuṟiyuṟṟut talivi māṭṭuc cellutaṟkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum” eṉṟum, “ik kiḷavi irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talivaṉ uḷḷappuṇarcci uḷvaḻiyum, kuṟiyuṟṟu meyyuṟu puṇarcci vēṇṭit talivimāṭṭuc cellum kālattum okkum” eṉṟum kūṟi itaṉi utāraṇam kāṭṭuvar iḷampūraṇar; ivvāṟē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum 2 koḷvar. itaṟkum poruḷ okkumāṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, vaḷiyaṟi muṉki eṉṟa pāṭattukku vaḷi olikkum muṉki eṉṟu urikka. vāleyiṟ ṟiṉṉaki eṉṟum, virunteṉa viṉavi niṉṟa neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟum, neṭuntēr aṇṇal eṉṟum varum pāṭaṅkaṭku muṟiyē, vāliya eyiṟu viḷaṅkum iṉiya muṟuval eṉṟum, “putiyēṉ mika, ippuṉattiṟku yāṉ taṉip pōntaṉaṉ” eṉa ūrum pērum neṟiyum viṉavi niṉṟa neṭuntōḷ aṇṇal eṉṟum, tērūrntu varutalum kaḷaviṉkaṇ urittākaliṉ, neṭiya tēri yuṭiya aṇṇal eṉṟum poruḷ urittukkoḷka. tuṟi iraṇṭaṟkum poruḷ okkum eṉka.
	199.	kāṉalam peruntuṟik kalitiri tiḷikkum
vāṉuyar neṭumaṇa lēṟi yāṉātu
kāṇkam vammō tōḻi
ceṟivaḷi nekiḻttō ṉeṟikaṭa ṉāṭē.
itu, talimakaṉ oruvaḻit taṇantuḻi āṟṟāḷākiya tali makaḷi āṟṟuvikkum tōḻi colliyatu.
uri :
tōḻi, nam muṉkikkaṇ ceṟintu niṉṟa vaḷikaḷ nekiḻntōṭuvitta talimakaṉatu ali yeṟiyum kaṭal nāṭṭiṉik kāṉaṟ peruntuṟikkaṇ muḻaṅkukiṉṟa alikaḷ pōntu alikkum vāṉuṟa uyarnta neṭiya maṇaṟṟiṭarmēl ēṟi āṇṭē amintoḻiyātu avaṉatu varaviṉik kāṇpām, varuka eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṉalam peruntuṟi eṉpuḻi ammuccāriyi virikkum vaḻi virittal. vāṉuyar neṭumaṇal, mika uyarnta maṇaṟkuṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu. neytal nilat talimaka ṉākaliṉ nekiḻttōṉ eṟikaṭal nāṭu eṉṟār.
kuṟivaḻip piḻiyātu pōntu nalaṉ nukarntu ceṉṟavaṉ taṇattalāl mēṉi melintu vaḷi nekiḻa niṉṟamiyiṉ, ceṟivaḷi nekiḻttōṉ eṉṟum, avaṉatu nāṭu kāṇṭalum avaṉik kaṇṭāṟ pōlum iṉpam payakku mākaliṉ eṟi kaṭal nāṭu eṉṟum, neṭu maṇaṟ kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟavaḻi āṇṭut tōṉṟum iyaṟkikkāṭci neñciṉkaṇ nilavum varuttam keṭat taṉ iyaṟkiyiṉpam payantu neñcip piṇikku mākaliṉ vāṉuyar neṭumaṇal ēṟi āṉātu eṉṟum, uyariya maṇaṟ kuṉṟa mākaliṉ avaṉatu kaṭalnāṭṭiṉi muṭiyak kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉpāḷ, kāṇkam vammō eṉṟum kūṟi ṉāḷ. “kuṉṟiṉ tōṉṟum kuvavumaṇa lēṟik, kaṇṭaṉam varukam ceṉmō 1” eṉṟār piṟarum, “orutali yurimi vēṇṭiyum 2” eṉṟaviṭattu, “oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi mēṉa” eṉṟataṉāl, tōḻimāṭṭup pōkkum varavum tōṉṟum eṉak koḷka. ceṟivaḷi nekiḻttōṉ eṉṟatu uṭampunaṉi curuṅkal.
iṉi, “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum 3” eṉṟa cūttirattu, vēṇ ṭāp piriviṉkaṇ nikaḻum kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ itu taliviyi āṟṟuvittatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
neṭuvari yēṟi eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉum, neṭiya malimē lēṟi avaṉatu eṟikaṭal nāṭṭiṉik kāṇkam vammō eṉa urikka. neytal nilattē varikaḷ uḷavāyiṉum avi neyta lākumēyaṉṟik kuṟiñci eṉak koḷḷaṟka; “nila ṉoruṅku mayaṅkutal iṉṟu eṉa moḻipa 1” eṉpatu poru ḷilakkaṇam.
	200.	ilaṅkuvīṅ kelvaḷi yāynutal kaviṉap
polantērk koṇkaṉ vantaṉa ṉiṉiyē 
vilaṅkari neṭuṅkaṇ ñekiḻmati
nalaṅkavar pacaliyi nakukam yāmē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkkut tuṇintavaḻi ataṟku iraviṉkaṇ talimakaṉ vantatu aṟinta tōḻi talimakaḷip pāyal uṇarttic colliyatu.
uri :
viḷakkamuṟa nekiḻntu ilaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷiyiṉiyuṭi yāy, niṉ oḷi nuṇukiya neṟṟi aḻakeytumāṟu poṟṟēri ṉiyuṭiya koṇkaṉ ippoḻutu uṭaṉkoṇṭu cēṟaṟku vantuḷā ṉākaliṉ, vilaṅkutaliyuṭiya ari paranta neṭiya kaṇkaḷ tuyil nīṅkit teḷivāyāka; ataṉāl nam nalam kavarnta pacaliyiṉ kēṭu kaṇṭu yām makiḻalām eṉṟavāṟu.
vīṅku eṉṉum mutaṉilit taṉiviṉi viṉiyeccap poruṭṭu; “varippuṉi pantu 2” eṉṟāṟ pōla. āynutal eṉpuḻi āytal curuṅkutal; pacaliyāl oḷi curuṅkiṉamiyiṉ āynutal eṉappaṭṭatu. iṉi, elvaḷiyāy eṉavum, nutal kaviṉ eṉavum koḷḷiṉum amiyum. kaviṉa eṉṉum peyaraṭiyākap piṟanta viṉiyeccam vantaṉaṉ eṉṉum kāraṇaviṉi koṇṭatu. poṉ eṉpatu, ceyyuḷākaliṉ, polam eṉa vantatu.3 ñekiḻtal īṇṭut tuyileḻutal. nakukam: paṉmit taṉmi. naki, eḷḷal paṟṟip piṟappatu. kaṇṇiṉ veḷviḻiyil mēlum kīḻumākac civanta nuṇṇiya koṭipōl parantirukkum varikaḷi vilaṅkari eṉpa; neṭu viḻiyiṉ kuṟukkē niṟṟaliṉ vilaṅkari eṉak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
varivu nīṭṭittalāl, iṟceṟippu mutaliyavaṟṟāl mēṉi vēṟupaṭṭu nutal pacantu vāṭi yiruntā ḷākaliṉ, āynutal eṉṟum, aḻivil kūṭṭattā ṉaṉṟi appacappu nīṅkāmi uṭkoṇṭu uṭaṉpōkku niṉintiruntāḷākaliṉ āy nutal kaviṉa eṉṟum, pōkkiṉkaṇ tamar etirntu takiyāvāṟu viripari pūṭṭiya tēroṭu talimakaṉ vantuḷāṉ eṉṟaṟkup polantērk koṇkaṉ vantaṉaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ ip poḻutē vantāṉāyiṉum nī tāḻttal kūṭā teṉṟaṟku iṉiyē vilaṅkari neṭuṅkaṇ ñekiḻmati eṉṟum, tali makaṉ pirivālum varivu nīṭṭittamiyālum nutal oḷi karappap paṭarntu māmikkaviṉ keṭa iruntamiyiṉ nalaṅ kavar pacali eṉṟum, talivaṉoṭu uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻi eytum aḻivil kūṭṭattāl pacali paṭartal illi yeṉpāḷ nakukam yām eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ.
pōkkuṭaṉpaṭṭa talimakaṉ iraviṉkaṇ vantami aṟintatum, tōḻi talimakaḷit tuyiluṇarttuvāḷ avaḷ virinteḻiṉ vaḷiyoliyāl tāyar mutalāyiṉār kaṇ viḻippa reṉpatu aṟivittaṟku elvaḷi eṉavum, avvāṟu eḻuvāṭkut talimakaṉ pōkku niṉintu vantami uṇart taṟkup polantērk koṇkaṉ vantaṉaṉ eṉavum, neṟipaṟṟit talimakaṉpāl virintu aṭiya vēṇṭutaliṉ “eḻuka” eṉṉātu neṭuṅkaṇ ñekiḻmati eṉavum, pacali parantami nōkki alar kūṟum ayaṟpeṇṭir talimaṭaṅku vataṉik kuṟippumoḻiyāl nalaṅkavar pacaliyi nakukam yāmē eṉavum tōḻi cila coṟkaḷāl viḷaṅka vuṇarttum tiṟam kāṇka.
nutal kaviṉpeṟa eṉṟum, neṭuntērk koṇkaṉum eṉṟum, neṭuṅkaṇ aṉantal tīrmati eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu. avaṟṟiṟku muṟiyē nutal tāṉ iḻanta aḻakip peṟa eṉṟum, neṭiya tēriyuṭiya talimakaṉum eṉṟum, ummiyāl avaṉ pōkku uṭaṉpaṭātiruntamiyum, kaṇkaḷāṟ peṟum tuyil nīṅkuka eṉṟum urikka. nalaṅkiḷar pacali eṉṟa pāṭamum kuṟippumoḻiyāy nalaṅ kavarnta pacali eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum eṉka.
āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar neṭuṅkaṇ aṉantar tīrmati eṉṟē pāṭaṅ koṇṭu, “talivarum viḻuma nili yeṭut turippiṉum 1” eṉpuḻip “pōkkaṟ kaṇṇum” eṉṟataṟku itaṉik kāṭṭi, “kaṇkaḷ aṉantal tīrmati eṉṟata ṉālē uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōvataṟku vantāṉ eṉap pāya luṇarttik kūṟiṟṟu” eṉṟār. itu neytaṟkaṇ pāli vantatu.
itukāṟum uri kūṟip pōnta neytaṟ pāṭṭukkaḷ nūṟum, tāykkuritta pattu mutal vaḷippattu īṟākap pattup pattukka ḷāka vakukkap peṟṟiruppatu kaṇṭōm. ivaṟṟuḷ tāyk kuritta pattu, tōḻik kuritta pattu, kiḻavaṟkuritta pattu, pāṇaṟ kuritta pattu eṉṟa nāṉkum uripporuḷic cārnta kēṭpōr  poruḷākavum, ñāḻaṟpattu, veḷḷāṅ kurukup pattu, ciṟuveṇ kākkippattu, toṇṭippattu, neytaṟ pattu, vaḷippattu eṉṟa āṟum neytaṟ karupporuḷ poru ḷākavum tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mutaliṟ karuvum, karuviṉ uriyum “muṟi ciṟantaṉa 2” eṉpataṉāl uripporuṭ kuriya tāyar mutaliyōri muṟpaṭa niṟuttiyum, ēṉik karup poruḷip piṟpaṭa niṟuttiyum inneytaṟ pakutiyip pulat
tuṟi muṟṟiya kūṭalūr kiḻār tokuttuḷḷār.
iraṇṭāvatu
āciriyar ammūvaṉār pāṭiya neytal muṟṟum.

1.	makaḷirkku nāṇam niṟi mutaliyaṉa akanalamāy niṉṟu ciṟappikka, im māmi puṟanalamāy viḷaṅki avarkku iṉiya cāyali viḷivikkum eṉpar. itaṉip pantāṭi niṉṟa vimaliyiṉ akappuṟa nalaṅkaḷic ciṟappikkappukka tiruttakka

1.	akam. 8.
1.	tol.po. 491.	    2. kuṟun. 27.
3.	tol. po. 161.
4.	parattiyar, kātaṟparatti, iṟparatti, cēripparatti eṉap palavakiyāyk kūṟappaṭuvar. avarit tollāciriyar parattiyar eṉap potuppaṭa moḻivar.
	iṉi, kāmakkiḻattiyar, parattiyar eṉa vēṟupaṭuttu, “kāmakkiḻattiya rāvār piṉmuṟi yākkiya kiḻattiyar; avar mūvakippaṭuvar: otta kiḻattiyarum, iḻinta kiḻattiyarum. variyappaṭṭārum eṉa. otta kiḻattiyar, muntuṟṟa maṉiyāḷaṉṟik kāmam poruḷākap piṉṉum taṉ kulattuḷḷāḷ oruttiyi varital; iḻintavarāvār
	antaṇarkku araca kulattiṉum, vaṇika kulattiṉum, vēḷāṇ kulattiṉum koṭukkappaṭṭārum, aracarkku ēṉiyiraṇṭu kulattiṉum koṭukkappaṭṭārum, vaṇikarkku vēḷāṇ kulattiṟ koṭukkappaṭṭārum. variyappaṭṭār, celvarāyiṉār kaṇikik kulattiṉuḷḷārkku iṟkiḻami koṭuttu varintu koḷḷappaṭṭār. avar, kaṉṉiyil variyappaṭṭārum, ataṉ piṉpu variyappaṭṭārum eṉa iruvakiyar. avviruvarum urimi pūṇṭamiyāṟ kāmakkiḻattiyarpāṟ paṭṭaṉar. parattiyarāvar yāreṉiṉ, avar āṭalum pāṭalum vallarāki aḻakum iḷamiyuṅkāṭṭi iṉpamum poruḷum veḵki oruvar māṭṭum taṅkātār” eṉpar āciriyar iḷampūraṇar (tol. 149 : uri.)
	nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, kāmakkiḻattiyar, parattiyar eṉpāruḷ. “kāmakkiḻattiyarāvār kaṭaṉaṟiyum vāḻkki yuṭiyarākik kāmak kiḻamipūṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttum parattiyar” eṉṟum, “avar talivaṉatu iḷamipparuvattiṟ kūṭi mutirntōrum, avaṉ taliniṉṟu oḻukappaṭum iḷamip paruvattōrum, iṭinilip paruvat
tōrum, kāmañcālā iḷamiyōrum eṉap pala pakutiyiṉarām” eṉṟum, ēṉip parattiyar, “kūttum pāṭṭum uṭiyarāki varum cēripparattiyarum, kulattiṉkaṇ iḻintōrum, aṭiyarum, viṉivala pāṅkiṉarum piṟarumām” eṉṟum kūṟi, iḷampūraṇar ottakiḻattiyar mutal mūvakiyiṉarākak kūṟiya pākupāṭṭic ciṟappuṭit taliviyarōṭu parattiyariyuṅ kūṭṭik kāmakkiḻattiya reṉṟu cūttirañ ceyyiṉ mayaṅkak kūṟal eṉṉum kuṟṟam taṅkumākaliṉ, aḵtu āciriyarkkuk karuttaṉṟu eṉa maṟuttu, “cāṉṟōr palarum kāmakkiḻattiyarip parattiyarākat tōṟṟuvāy ceytu kūṟumāṟum uṇarka” (tol.po. 151.) eṉpar.
	nāṟkavirāca nampiyār, “oruvaṉ taṉakkē yurimi pūṇṭu. varukulap parattiyar makaḷirākik, kāmakku varintār kāmak kiḻattiyar, “yāriyum nayavā viyalpiṟ ciṟanta, cērip parattiyar makaḷi rākik, kātaliṟ puṇarntōr kātaṟ parattiyar”, “avaruḷum varitaṟ kuriyā ruḷarē” (113-5) eṉpar-iḵtu urikaḷil varumāṟu. nāṭṭil aṭikkaṭi nikaḻnta pōrkaḷāl, makaḷiriṉum āṭavar toki kuṟintatāka, āṭavariṉṟi mikka makaḷir parattiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. parattiyar, mikkavar. itu varalāṟu kāṭṭumāṟu.

1.	paṇṭi nāḷil tēṉūr eṉṟa peyaruṭiya ūrkaḷ pala iruntuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ nilipēṟuṭiya kuṟippukkaḷ koṇṭavi pāṇṭināṭṭil iraṇṭum, naṭunāṭṭil oṉṟum kalveṭṭukkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. naṭunāṭṭiṉuḷḷatu peṇṇit teṉkari makati nāṭṭut teṉkarip paḻaṅkūrppaṟṟut tēṉūr (A.R. 409 of 1938) eṉappaṭukiṟatu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭil mutukuḷattūrp pakutiyil tirumālukantāṉ kōṭṭiyeṉpatu paṇṭi nāḷil tēṉūr eṉṟa peyarkoṇṭu viḷaṅkiyiruntatu. itaṉik kalveṭṭukaḷ, tirumāvukantanallūr eṉṟum, ēḻurc cempi nāṭṭut teṉmuṉippaṟṟut tēṉūrāṉa tirumāvukanta nallūr (A.R. 43 of 1931-32) eṉṟum, aḷaṟṟunāṭṭut tēṉūr (A.R. 56 of 1931-32) eṉṟum, kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. tēṉūric cārnta ēḻūr tirunāvukkaracarāl (284.5) ciṟappikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. maṟṟoṉṟu viyikkariyil tiruvēṭakattuk kaṇmiyil uḷatu. tiruvēṭakam ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil tiruñāṉa campantar paravum ciṟappup peṟṟatu. ataṉi aṭuttirukkum tēṉūrip paḻaṅkālak kalveṭṭukkaḷ pākaṉūrk kūṟṟattup peruntēṉūr (A.R. 604 of 1926) eṉavum, pākaṉūrk kūṟṟattuk
	 tēṉūrt tiruvēṭakam eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. patiṉōrām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta kalveṭṭukkaḷ, itaṉit tēṉūrāṉa virutarāca payaṅkara nallūr (606 of 1926) eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ittēṉūr viyi pāyum nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum mikuntu iṉṟum ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvatōṭu “vēṉilāyiṉum taṇpuṉaloḻukum” ciṟappuk kuṉṟāmalē yuḷatu, itaṉ veṟṟili vaḷatti, tēṉūr veṟṟiliyum, māṉūr cuṇṇāmpum eṉṟa paḻamoḻi kāṭṭi niṟkiṟatu. tiruñāṉacampantar “vāṉūrāṉ viyakattāṉ vāḻttuvār maṉattakattāṉ. tēṉūrāṉ ceṅkāṭṭaṅ kuṭiyāṉ ciṟṟampalattāṉ” ñāṉa. 61.9) eṉavum, “poykiyiṉ poḻiluṟu putumalart teṉṟalār, viyiyiṉ vaṭakari maruviya ēṭakam” (ñāṉa. 290.6) eṉavum ciṟappittaliṉ iv vēṭakat tēṉūrē caṅkakālat tēṉūr eṉalām.
